maliyāḷam	inti	āṅkilam
ituentākuṉṉu?	yaḵ kiyā hi?	What is this?
itu oru maramākuṉṉu.	yaḵ pēṭ hi.	This is a tree.
vaṭanāṭṭu āriyamoḻikaḷiṉ tiraviṭa aṭippaṭi
āriyamoḻikaḷākak kūṟappaṭum vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷil, tiraviṭac coṟkaḷ maṭṭumiṉṟit tiraviṭa moḻiyamitiyum amintiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. iti intivāyilāṟ kāṭṭuval.
(1)	āyā, pōlā eṉa āvīṟukoṇṭu iṟantakālam kāṭṭum inti viṉimuṟṟukkaḷ ceyyā eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiyeccatti yottaṉa.
(2)	āyiyē, pōliyē eṉa `iyē' īṟukoḷḷum inti vēṇṭu kōḷ viṉikaḷ, ceyyiya eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viyaṅkōḷ viṉi yottaṉa.
(3)	paṭē paṭē, calttē calttē eṉa aṭukkivarum inticcol liraṭṭikaḷ, periya periya, naṭantu naṭantu eṉa varum aṭukkut toṭari muṟṟum ottaṉa.
(4)	vaḵ kiyā karttā hi? (mt‹ v‹d brŒ»wh‹?), rāmā pāt kāttā hi (irāmaṉ cōṟu uṇkiṉṟāṉ) eṉa amiyum intic coṟṟoṭarkaḷ muṟṟum tamiḻoḻuṅkip piṉpaṟṟiyaṉa.
āṅkilat tamiḻamiti yoppumi
(1) taṉikkuṟil muṉṉoṟṟuyirvariṉ iraṭṭal.
	e-kā: Thin+er=thinner, sit+ing=sitting.
(2) nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccam eḻuvāyātal.
	e-kā: To err is human.
eṉakkup pāṭat teriyum.
(3) viṉikkālaṅkaḷiṉ nālvēṟupāṭu.
e-kā :	nikaḻkālam varukiṟāṉ - taṉippu (Indefinite)
	vantu koṇṭirukkiṟāṉ - toṭarcci (Continuous)
	vantirukkiṟāṉ - niṟivu (Perfect)
	vantu koṇṭiruntirukkiṟāṉ-niṟivut toṭarcci (Perfect Continuous).
kālṭuvel aiyariṉ caṟukkalkaḷ
kālṭuvel aiyar taliciṟanta tiraviṭa moḻiyārāycci col lārāycci valluṉarēṉum, tolkāppiyap payiṟciyum caṅkanūṟ kalviyum moḻiyārāycciyum akkālattaṉmiyālum, ellāt tuṟiyilum āriyat talimi etirppiṉṟiyiruntu vantatiṉālum tamiḻiṉ taṉit taṉmiyi yaṉṟit tāymiyiyum talimiyiyum uṇarātu ilakkaṇa muḷḷiṭṭa uyartarak kali nūlkaḷ āriya vaḻiyiṉaveṉṟum, āṉmā pāvam mutaliya pala karuttukaḷiyuṇarttat tamiḻiṟ collilli yeṉṟum tamiḻp peyar vēṟṟumi vakuppu camaṟkirutattip piṉpaṟṟiya teṉṟum tamiḻar allatu tiraviṭar potuvāyt tāḻnta nākarika niliyiṉar eṉṟum kūṟippōntār. iṉi, tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiṟ ceyappāṭṭu viṉiyilliyeṉṟatum, āriya muṟiyi avar aḷaviyākak koṇṭatik kāṭṭum.
tamiḻiṉ potu viyalpu
tamiḻ ulaka mutaṟṟāy moḻiyātalāl, cīṉa moḻiyiṉ aci nilit taṉmiyiyum, āriya moḻikaḷiṉ vikuti nilittaṉmiyiyum, cittiya moḻikaḷiṉ koḷuvu nilit taṉmiyiyum, cēmiya moḻikaḷiṉ uyirt tirivut taṉmiyiyum, āppirikka moḻikaḷiṉ pālicivut taṉmiyi yum, amerikka moḻikaḷiṉ tokuti nilit taṉmiyiyum oruṅkē koṇṭuḷḷatu.
2
coṟ poruḷ varici
coṟkaḷ, poruṭ ṭoki paṟṟi, 1. oru poruṭcol 2. pala poruḷ col eṉa iru vakippaṭum. āyiṉum, oru colliṉ mutaṟ poruḷ vaḻiyākap palvēṟu poruḷkaḷ ovvoṉṟāyt tōṉṟutaṟ kiṭamirutta lāl, oru kālattu oru poruṭ collāyiruntaṉavum, piṟkālattup pala poruṭ coṟkaḷākiviṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru kālattu oru coṟku oru poruḷē tōṉṟuvatu iyalpātalāl, palaporuṭ coṟkaḷellām mutaṟkaṇ oru poruṭ coṟkaḷāy iruntaṉavē.
coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷ varici, atāvatu poruḷkaḷ muṉṉum piṉṉum tōṉṟiya muṟi 1. tuyh‰W Kiw (Historical Sequence) 2., ēraṇa muṟi (Logical Sequence) eṉa iruvakippaṭum. oru colliṉ poruḷkaḷi, mutal vaḻicārpu nūlkaḷil allatu muṉṉiṭi piṉṉūl kaḷil avi āḷappaṭṭu vanta varaṉ muṟippaṭiyē oruṅkupaṭuttuvatu varalāṟṟu muṟiyām; aḵtaṉṟi uttikkup poruttamāka avaṟṟi oḻuṅkupaṭuttuvatu ēraṇamuṟiyām. inti pōṉṟa putumoḻikaḷilum āriyam pōṉṟa mutumoḻikaḷilum muṉṉi allatu paṇṭi ilakkiyam aḻiyātiruttalāl ataṉvāyilākac coṟporuḷkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟu muṟiyi aṟital kūṭum. āyiṉ paṇṭi yilakkiyam muṟṟum iṟantupaṭṭa tamiḻ pōlun toṉ mutumoḻiyil ataṉi aṟiyumāṟilli. ātalāl, attakiya moḻic coṟ poruḷkaṭku ēraṇa muṟitāṉ iyalum. oru moḻiyiṉ vaḷarccik kālattuc coṟkaṭku ovvoṉṟāyt tōṉṟiya poruṭkaḷ, avi tōṉṟiya muṟiyē piṟkālattu uṇarttappeṟā: avvav iṭattiṟkēṟpavē uṇarttappeṟum. oruvaḻaṅku moḻi eṉṟum vaḷarntu koṇṭēyiruppiṉum, ataṉ peruvāric coṟkaḷ oru kuṟitta kālattiṟkuḷ amintu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉpiṉ, tōṉṟum coṟkaḷum perumpālum putuc coṟkaḷāyirāmal paḻañcoṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu amiyum tiricoṟkaḷum kūṭṭu coṟkaḷumākavē yirukkiṉṟaṉa, ātalāl, mutu paṇṭayilakkiyam muṟṟum iṟantupaṭṭatum. karuttiṟku meṭṭāk kālantoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvatumāṉa tamiḻil, coṟporuḷ varici ēraṇa muṟippaṭi tāṉ amital iyalum. varalāṟṟu muṟi iyaṟkiyākavum ēraṇa muṟi ceyaṟkiyākavum tōṉṟiṉum muṉṉatu uḷanūṉ muṟippaṭi nikaḻtaliṉ av uḷanūlit taḻuviya ēraṇa muṟiyum perumpālum iyaṟki yoṭṭiyē irukkumeṉa aṟika. ivvuṇmikaḷi aṟiyātār tamiḻilum coṟporuḷ variciyi āṅkilattiṟpōl varalāṟṟu muṟiyil amikka muyalvar. itaṉ pulimiyi eṭuttukkāṭṭāṟ kāṭṭutum.
`paḷḷi' eṉṉum coṟkuc ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka akarātiyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa poruḷvarici varumāṟu:
1. iṭam	10. tūkkam
2. ciṟṟūr	11. vilaṅku tuyiliṭam
3. iṭiccēri	12. paḷḷikkūṭam
4. nakaram	13. aṟi
5. muṉivarācciramam	14. aṟaccāli
6. ciṉa puttak kōyil 	15. cāli
7. araṇmaṉi	16. vaṉṉiyaṉ
8. (taccaṉ) vēlikkaḷam	17. paḷḷatti
9. makkaṭ paṭukki	18. kuṟumpar (ciṟṟaracar)
piṉṉiṇippil (aṉupantattil) kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa iṉṉoru poruḷ (19) `kiṟittavak kōyil', eṉpatu, ivaṟṟuḷ mutalum iṟutiyum (1, 19) varalāṟṟu muṟi taḻuviyavi: ēṉiya orumaṟiyun taḻuviyavalla. immuṟikēṭu ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka akarātiyiṉ ciṟappiyalpākum.
itupōluḷḷa tamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷ paḻimiyāṉa tolkāppiyattil, paḷḷi eṉṉum col iṭam eṉṉum poruḷil āḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
``colliya paḷḷi niliyiṉa vāyiṉum''	(tol. eḻuttatikāram, 100)
eṉpatu kāṇka. itaṉāl, iṭam eṉṉum poruḷ mutalilum, kiṟittavam tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pukuttap peṟṟapiṉ tōṉṟiya kiṟittuvak kōyil, eṉṉum poruḷ iṟutiyilum, vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
mēl kuṟikkappaṭṭa pattoṉpāṉ poruḷkaḷoṭu, paḷḷam, vīṭu, kōyil, kallaṟi (camāti) eṉpavaṟṟiyuñ cērkkalām. kōyil eṉṟatu īṇṭu ellā mataṅkaṭkum potuvāṉa iṟiyakatti.
kaḻā akkāl paḷḷiyuḷ vittaṟṟāṟ cāṉṟōr
kuḻāattup pētipukal, 	(840)
eṉṉum kuṟaṭku
``cāṉṟōraviyiṉkaṭ pētiyāyiṉāṉ pukutal, tūyavalla mititta kāli iṉpantarum amaḷik kaṇṇē vittāṟ pōlum'', eṉa uṟittār parimēlaḻakar.
``kaḻuvākkāleṉpatu iṭakkaraṭakku. ataṉāl avvamaḷiyum'' iḻikkappaṭumāṟupōla ivaṉāl avvaviyum iḻikkappaṭumeṉpatām, eṉpatu avar ciṟappukkuṟippu.
ivvuriyil paḷḷiyeṉpatu paṭukki allatu paṭukkik kaṭṭil eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭṭatu. cāṉṟōrit tēvaruk koppākak kūṟuvatu ilakkiya marapātalāl, iṅkup paḷḷiyeṉpatu teyvattaṉmiyuḷḷa iṭattik kuṟippateṉṟu koḷvatallatu iṉpan tarum iṭattikkuṟippa teṉṟu koḷvatu poruntātu. ākavē, paḷḷi eṉpataṟkuk kōyil eṉpatē poruḷām. cāṉṟōr kūṭṭattuḷ ōr aṟivili pukutal, oruvaṉ kaḻuvāta kāloṭu kōyiṟkuḷ pukuvatokkum. eṉpatē vaḷḷuvar karuttātal vēṇṭum. kuḷittu viṭṭu allatu ki kāl kaḻuviviṭṭuk kōyiṟkuḷ pukutal, iṉṟum palvēṟu matattārkkum vaḻakkamāyiruttal kāṇka, tēvarip pulavareṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar kuṟippiṭuvatiyum.
``aṅkaṇ vicumpiṉ akaṉilāp pārikkum
tiṅkaḷum cāṉṟōrum opparmaṉ''	(nālaṭi. 151) 
eṉṟu kūṟuvatiyum nōkkuka.
ākavē, ēṟkeṉavē kōyilip potuppaṭak kuṟitta paḷḷiyeṉ ṉum peyarē, piṟkālattut tamiḻakattup putuvatāka ēṟpaṭṭa camaṇa pavutta kiṟittava mukamatiyak kōyilkaḷiyum kuṟikka āḷappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. etupōleṉiṉ, āriya maṟiyiyē mutaṉmutaṟ kuṟitta vētam eṉṉum col, piṟkālattuk kiṟittavamaṟiyiyē ciṟappākak kuṟittatupōleṉka.
vittukkaḷ cēṟṟiṟ patintu kiṭattalip `paḷḷikkiruttal' eṉṟu nāñcil nāṭṭār colvatāl, paḷḷam eṉṉum poruḷum; araciyal tīrvi celuttātavaṉ vīṭṭil oru ciṟukampu naṭṭu avaṉ atic celuttum vari maṟiyal ceytalip `paḷḷikampu vittal' eṉa annāṭṭārē vaḻaṅkuvatāl `vīṭu eṉṉum poruḷum; iṟanta aracaṉ uṭali eritta allatu putitta viṭattuk kōyiṟ kaṭṭuvatip paḷḷip paṭi eṉṟu ilakkiyamuṅ kalveṭṭum kūṟuvatāl, kallari eṉṉum poruḷum; paḷḷi eṉṉuñ coṟkuk koḷḷap peṟṟaṉa.
paḷḷi eṉṉum colliṉ vēr `paḷ eṉpatum, vērp poruḷ `paḷḷam' eṉpatum ākum. paḷḷam allatu tāḻvāṉa iṭam eṉṉum poruḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, paḷḷamāṉa iṭattil vēli ceyyuṅkulattāṉ, (kīḻē) paṭukki, (paṭuttut taṅkum) maṉi, (tēvamaṉi yākiya) = kōyil, (mutaṟ kālattukkōvilil naṭatta peṟṟa, kalvic cāli, (palamaṉikaḷ cērnta) ūr, (maṉiyum ūrumākiya) iṭam eṉṉum eḻu parupporuḷkaḷum ivaṟṟuṭ palavaṟṟi nilikkaḷamākak koṇṭa irupattiru nuṇporuḷkaḷum, paḷḷi eṉṉuñ coṟkut tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. innuṇ poruṭ pākupāṭṭup paṭṭi varumāṟu:
1.	paḷḷam allatu tāḻvu:
	tāḻvāṉa vīṭukaḷ aminta ciṟṟūr allatu mullinilattūr, mulli nilattūr pōṉṟa iṭic cēri, āḻiṭam.
2.	paḷḷamāṉa vayalil vēli ceyyuṅkulattār:
	paḷḷatti, vaṉṉiyaṉ, vaṉṉiyakulac ciṟṟaracarāṉa kuṟumpar.
3.	paṭukki:
	makkaṭ paṭukki, vilaṅku tuyiliṭam, tūkkam.
4.	maṉi (vīṭu)
	vīṭu, aṟi, araṇmaṉi, vēlikkaḷam, aṟac cāli, muṉivar tavaniliyam, cāli.
5.	kōyil
	kōyil (potu), ciṉa pavuttak kōyil, kiṟittavak kōyil, mukamatiyar kōyil (paḷḷi vācal) kallaṟi. kōyil eṉpatu aracaṉ  maṉi pōṉṟa tēvamaṉi eṉṟumām.
6.	kalvic cāli
7.	ūr
	nakaram, paḷḷi eṉpatu oru kōyil allatu araṇmaṉi yiruppatu paṟṟiyum oru nakarik kuṟikkum.
8.	iṭam
	ic coṟ poruḷ varici ēraṇamuṟi taḻuviyatākum. itil iṟutiyil vantuḷḷaporuḷ, ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻaka akarātiyil mutaliṭam peṟṟiruttal kāṇka, iṅṅaṉam tali kīḻāka māṟiyatu, moḻi vaḷarccik kālattu ilakkiyam muṟṟum, maṟintapiṉ iṭikkālattu ilakkiyattip paṟṟuk kōṭākak koṇṭu, varalāṟṟumuṟi taḻuvamuyaṉṟataṉ payaṉē.
iṉi, ip paḷḷi yeṉṉuñ coṟporuḷ variciyi vaḻuppaṭa amittatōṭamiyātu, ic colli vaṭacolleṉṟuṅ kūṟa tuṇintuḷatu ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka coṟkaḷañciyam. iccol teṉcol eṉpati, paḷḷam allatu tāḻvu eṉṉum poruḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, itaṉoṭu toṭarpuṟṟa ēṉic coṟkaḷik kaṇṭu teḷika. aviyāvaṉa:
paḷ - paḷḷam - paḷḷaṉ = paḷḷamāṉa vayalil vēli ceypavaṉ
paḷ - paḷku - paḷkutal = patuṅkutal,
paḷ - paḷḷi = kuḷḷamāṉa āṭṭuvaki
paḷḷiyaṉ = kuḷḷaṉ
paḷḷi - paḷḷiyam = uṇkalam.
paḷḷiyam pōṭutal = teyvattiṟkup paṭittal
paḷku - paḷaku = kuṟṟam (tāḻvu)
paḷ - paṇ = nīrnili
paṇ - paṇṇi = kuḻi, neṟkuttumāṟu nilattiṟ patitta naṭuppaḷḷakkal, nīr nili, marattiṟku aṭiyil nīr pāycca amikkum pātti, marakkalam, vilaṅku tuyiliṭam.
paṇ - paṇi, paṇital = kīḻppaṭital, paṇi = toṇṭu, vēli, toḻil.
paṇi - pāṇi, pāṇittal = kālantāḻttal.
paṇ - paṭu = 1. (peyar) kuḷam, maṭu. 2. (peyareccam) iḻivāṉa.
paṭu - paṭukar = paḷḷam, vayal marutanilam, nīrnili,
paṭu - pāṭu - pāṭi = tāḻvāṉa vīṭukaḷ cērnta mulli nilattār allatu iṭiccēri.
pāṭu - pāṭi = kālkaḻikaṭṭil.
paṭu - paṭi -paṭittal = uṇṇumāṟu kīḻiṭutal
paṭuttal = tāḻakkiṭantu tūṅkutal,
paṭu - paṭi = paṭukki, tūkkam
paṭutal = viḻutal, cātal, paṭuttal = kollutal 
paṭu - paṭi = kollum āyutam allatu cēṉi.
paṭu - paṭi, paṭital = aṭiyil taṅkutal, taṅkutal, kīḻppaṭital viḻuntu vaṇaṅkutal, amuṅkutal, taṇital, kuḷittal, tūṅkutal.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, tamiḻil coṟporuḷ varici ēraṇamuṟip paṭitāṉ iyalum eṉpatiyum, paḷḷi eṉpatu paḷḷam allatu tāḻvu eṉṉuṅ karutti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa tūya teṉ col eṉpatiyum, teṟṟeṉat terintu koḷka.
vaṭa moḻiyil (camaṟkirutattil), ḷakara miṉmiyāl, paḷḷi eṉṉuñ col palli eṉṟum pallī eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉataṟku, ciṟṟūr, kāṭuvāḻ marapiṉar kuṭiyiruppu, kuṭici, vīṭu eṉṉum poruḷkaḷum; piṉṉataṟku ciṟṟūr, kuṭici, vīṭu, nakaram, orukūṭu mukattalaḷavu, ciṟu vīṭṭuppalli eṉṉum poruḷkaḷum; māṉiyar uvilliyam camaṟkiruta - āṅkila akarātiyil tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iv iru col vaṭivukaṭkum vērākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa col pall (pall) eṉpatu, ivvērp poruḷākak kuṟikkappaṭṭavi `pōtal' (to go), `iyaṅkutal' (to move) eṉpaṉa. iv vērikkuṟikkumiṭattu; oru kāl piṉvarum coṟkaṭku mūlaṅkāṭṭum poruṭṭup`pal' eṉṉum vēriyoṭṭip paṭikkappaṭṭatu! (``Invented after pal , prob. to explin the following words'') eṉṟu māṉiyar villiyam kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu, piṉvaruñ coṟkaḷ, eṉṟu kuṟikkap peṟṟavi, palla, pallakka, palli, pallikkā, pallī eṉpaṉa, pal eṉṉum vērkkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭa poruḷum, pōtal (to go) eṉpatē.
palla = peruṅkaḷañciyam, neṟkūṭu 
pallakka = takkāṇattil oru māvaṭṭap peyariṉ piṟpakuti.
pallikkā = ciṟṟūr, ciṟu vīṭṭuppalli.
paḷḷi eṉṉum col, paḷ eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟu tirintu pala poruḷkaḷik koṇṭiruppiṉum, ciṟappākap paṭukkiyi yuṇarttu meṉpatu, paḷḷi koḷḷutal, paḷḷi koṇṭāṉ, paḷḷi koṇṭa perumāḷ, paḷḷi yeḻucci, paḷḷi māṭam, paḷḷi maṇṭapam, paḷḷiyantulā, paḷḷiyampalam mutaliya coṟkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭum, paṭukki eṉṉum karutti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭē paḷḷi eṉṉuñ col muṟiyē, vīṭu, kōyil, paḷḷikkūṭam mutaliya poruḷkaḷi uṇarttum. iṅṅaṉamiruppa vum, ic colli vaṭamoḻiyil ataṉ eḻuttu muṟikkēṟpap palli eṉṟum palli eṉṟut tirittukkoṇṭu pōtal allatu iyaṅkutal eṉṉum poruntāp poruḷi ataṉ vērp poruḷāka poruttik kāṭṭuvatu maṭṭumiṉṟi, palli eṉṉum ūruyirip peyariyum paḷḷi eṉṉuñ colloṭu mayakki, ataṉiyum vaṭa colleṉa ēmāṟṟuvatu. aṟivārāycci mikka iv irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku eṭṭuṉiyum ēṟkumō? kaṇṭu teḷika. kaṭukaḷavēṉuṅ karuttuṭiyār.
tiruccirāppaḷḷi tamiḻ caṅkam maṇi viḻā malar 1958
3
vaṇṇaṉi moḻi nūl
iṉṟu taṉik kaliyāka vaḻaṅkiyum vaḷarntum varum moḻinūl 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭil mēṉāṭukaḷil muḷitteḻunta tēṉum, ataṟku vittu tamiḻ nāṭṭil ki. mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉpē iṭappaṭṭa teṉpatu, 
``ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē''	(tol. 640)
``moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā.''		(tol. 877)
``iyaṟcol tiricol ticiccol vaṭacoleṉ
ṟaṉittē ceyyuḷ īṭṭac collē.''	(tol. 880)
``vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē.''	(tol. 884)
eṉṉum nūṟpākkaḷāl uṇaralākum.
mēṉāṭukaḷil kaṭanta munnūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka vaḷarkkappeṟṟuvarum moḻinūl, toṭakkattil, āṅkilattil, (Science of speech, Science of Language, Linguistic science, Linguistics, Glottology, Philology) eṉap pala peyarkaḷip peṟṟiruntatu. ivaṟṟuḷ (Linguistics, Philology eṉṉum iraṇṭē iṟutiyil nilipeṟṟaṉa.
moḻinūl, orē camayattil, varalāṟu taḻuviyatākavum oppunōkkiya tākavum palakūṟukaḷākap pakukkappaṭātatākavum uḷḷatu. mākkacumullar (Max Muller) viṟṟiṉi (Whitney) cecup perceṉ (Jespersen) mutaliya moḻi nūṟpēraṟiñarellām mēṟkūṟiya moḻinūṟ pēraṟiñarellām mēṟkūṟiya muṟiyilēyē moḻi nūli vaḷarttu vantaṉar. āyiṉ, aṇmiyil, cila mēlimoḻi nūlaṟiñar, ciṟappāka amerikkar, moḻinūli,
1. vaṇṇaṉi moḻinūl (Descriptive Linguistics)
2. varalāṟṟu moḻinūl (Historical Linguistics)
3. oppiyal moḻinūl (Comparative Linguistics)
eṉa muk kūṟākap pakuttu, avaṟṟuḷ vaṇṇaṉi moḻinūliyē ciṟappāka vaḷarttu varukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉ pala kuṟikaḷ uṇmikku māṟākavum tamiḻukkuk kēṭākavum uḷḷaṉa. aviyāvaṉa:
1. varalāṟu taḻuvātatu
varalāṟu ekkalikkum aṭippaṭiyāy maṭṭumaṉṟi mutukan taṇṭāyu
muḷḷateṉpatu, ulakaṟinta uṇmi. kalikaḷuḷḷum, moḻinūl varalāṟṟiyali iṉṟiyamiyātu taḻuviyateṉpatu collāmalē peṟappaṭum. āyiṉum, varalāṟṟut toṭarpiṉṟi uṇmi taḻuvāta uyiraṟṟa niliyil vaṇṇaṉi moḻinūl vaḷarkkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. itu, annūlāriṉ muyaṟciyiṉmi, kaliyuṇarcciyiṉmi, naṭuniliyiṉmi ākiyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiyē kāṭṭum.
2. āriya aṭippaṭi koṇṭatu
muntiyal cemmoḻiyākiya tamiḻi ārāyāmiyāl, moḻinūl tiṟavukōl tamiḻilēyē potintu kiṭappati aṟiyātu, tiripil tiripum ceyaṟkiyil ceyaṟkiyum āriyattiṉ muṭiyumākiya camaṟkirutatti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, moḻinūṟceytikaḷiyum moḻikaḷiyum moḻiyuṟuppukkaḷiyum muṉ piṉṉākavum kīḻ mēlākavum muṟi piṟaḻak kūṟivaruvatu vaṇṇaṉi moḻi nūl.
tamiḻ āriyattiṉ aṭi eṉpati iṉṉum mēliyar uṇarntilar.
3. ellā moḻikaḷum iṭukuṟit tokutikaḷē eṉpatu
tamiḻpōlum iyaṉmoḻiyi ārāyātu āriyamākiya tirimoḻi kaḷiyē āyntatiṉāl, ellāmoḻic coṟkaḷum iṭukuṟikaḷē eṉṉum tavaṟāṉa muṭiviṟku vantuḷḷaṉar iṟṟi amerikka moḻi nūlāciriyar.
tamiḻil iṭukuṟic collē illiyeṉpataṟku. ``ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē.'' eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā voṉṟē pōtiya cāṉṟām. ``moḻip poruṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā.'' eṉpatu, colvērp poruḷ pārttavuṭaṉ allatu mikat teḷivākat tōṉṟātu (cila coṟkaḷil) eṉap poruḷpaṭumēyaṉṟi, tiru. viyāpurippiḷḷi kūṟṟuppōl tōṉṟavē tōṉṟātu eṉap poruḷpaṭuvataṉṟu.
iyaṟcoṟkaḷil teḷivākat tōṉṟum vērpporuḷ tiricoṟkaḷil maṟintupōm eṉṉum uṇmiyik kīḻk kuṟitta coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu aṟika
iyaṟcol	tiricol
iṭikaḻi	rēḻi
veṇṇey	veṉṉa (teluṅku)
Episcope	Biscop
moḻi tōṉṟiya vakiyi viḷakki niṟpatu ulaka muḻumiyiṉum tamiḻ oṉṟē.
4. coṟkaḷ tammaḷaviṟ poruḷuṇarttātu avaṟṟiṉ muṟippāṭṭiṉālēyē poruḷuṇarttuvaṉa eṉpatu,
itu moḻi tōṉṟiya muṟiyi aṟiyāmiyāl nērnta tavaṟākum.
ikkālattum, kuḻintikaḷum nōyāḷiyarum ayaṉmoḻit tuvakka māṇavarum cila vēḷikaḷil oru collālēyē tam karuttit terivittal kāṇka.
5. āyiram āṇṭiṟkoru muṟi nūṟṟiṟkuppattoṉpatu viḻukkāṭu ellāc coṟkaḷum māṟiviṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu.
ellā moḻikaḷilum, aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷ uṭpaṭa, āyiram āṇṭiṟkoru muṟi nūṟṟukku 19 mēṉi ellāc coṟkaḷum vaḻakkaṟṟup putuccoṟkaḷ tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu amerikka moḻi nūlār koṇṭu, ivvaṭippaṭiyil coṉmāṟṟak kālakkaṇippu (Glotto chrorology) eṉa oru kaṇippu muṟiyum vakuttirukkiṉṟaṉar. atu varumāṟu:-
``oru moḻi irukiḷikaḷākap pirintuviṭiṉ, āyiram āṇṭiṟkup piṉ ovvoṉṟum mūlac coṟṟokutiyil nūṟṟiṟkup pattoṉpatu iḻantuviṭṭu, eṇpattoṉṟēkoṇṭu niṟkum. mūlac coṟkaḷ 200 āyiṉ, ovvoṉṟilum 162 eñciniṟkum. āyiṉ, orē coṟṟokutiyi iraṇṭum iḻakkum eṉṟu karutak kāraṇamilli, oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟu koṇṭu niṟpatilum iḻantatilum taṉittaṉi nūṟṟukku 81 tāṅki niṟkum eṉpatu perumpāl nikaḻak kūṭiyatu. iṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, ovvoṉṟum mūlappotuc coṟṟokutiyil 132 allatu nūṟṟukku 66 koṇṭu niṟkum. ikkaṇippiṉ paṭi, irumoḻikaḷil, aṭippaṭic coṟṟokutiyil nūṟṟukku 66 iṉac collāyiruntāl avi pirintupōy āyiram āṇṭāyiṟṟeṉṟum, 44 iṉac collāyiruppiṉ, perumpālum īrāyiram āṇṭāyirukku meṉṟum, aṟintu koḷḷalām.''
ikkaṇippiṉpaṭi ovvoru moḻiyum aiyāyiram āṇṭiṟkuḷ muṟṟum māṟiviṭa vēṇṭum. ki. mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya tol kāppiyam iṟṟikku 2500 āṇṭukaṭku muntiyatu. atiluḷḷa ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ attaṉiyum iṉṟum tamiḻ nāṭṭil vaḻaṅki varuvatōṭu,
``a i uam mūṉṟuñ cuṭṭu''	(tol - 31)
``neṭṭeḻut tēḻē ōreḻut torumoḻi''	(tol - 43)
``paṉṉi ruyirum moḻimuta lākum.''	(tol - 59)
eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa ettuṇiyō nūṟpākkaḷum, iṟṟi muṟiyil amintu ellārum eḷituṇarakkiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl, mēṟkāṭṭiya amerikkac coṉmāṟṟak kālak kaṇippu muṟṟum puripaṭṭateṉka.
6. ellā moḻikaḷum tiruntiyaviyē eṉpatu
``iyaṉmoḻiyeṉṟum tirimoḻiyeṉṟum, tiruntiyamoḻiyeṉṟum tiruntāmoḻiyeṉṟum, moḻikaṭkuḷ pākupāṭilli. ellām tiruntiyaviyē, curuṭṭu eṉpatu teluṅkil cuṭṭu eṉṟiruppiṉ, ataṟku atu cariyē. poṉ eṉpatu kaṉṉaṭattil hoṉṉu eṉṟiruppiṉ, ataṟku atu tiruntiyatē.'' ivvāṟu koḷvatu vaṇṇaṉi moḻi nūl.
``uḷḷatic coṉṉāl noḷḷik kaṇṇikkup pollāppu.'' teluṅku kaṉṉaṭam mutaliyavi tamiḻiṉ tiripu eṉṟāl, teluṅkarkkum kaṉṉaṭarukkum varuttam piṟakkum. ātalāl, kōmuṭṭic ceṭṭipōl irucārārkkum potuvākak kūṟuvatu ciṟantamuṟi eṉpatu, vaṇṇaṉi moḻi nūlār karuttu. immuṟiyoṭṭic cila tamiḻp pērāciyarum, tiraviṭam eṉpatu, tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya pala moḻikaḷiṉ tokutip peyar. aviyellām ōriṉamoḻikaḷ eṉṟu koḷvatallatu. tamiḻ piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ tāyeṉak kūṟuvatu poruntātu,'' eṉak kaṭṭurippar.
taṉippaṭṭavar ceytikaḷil oruvar, oruvar kuṟṟaṅkuṟikaḷi maṟikkalāmēyaṉṟi, oru nāṭṭu makkaḷellārkkum potuvāṉa moḻit tuṟiyilum kalittuṟiyilum kuṟṟaṅkuṟikaḷi maṟippatu paṇpāṭaṉṟu.
7. ilakkaṇattip paḻippatu
ellā mutumoḻikaḷilum ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ aḻakiya muṟiyil aṟiñarāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. aṅṅaṉamiruppiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ arumiyaṟiyātu, avaṟṟi varituṟiyiṉṟip paḻittu, māṇavari mayakkuvaṉavum poruṭciṟappaṟṟaṉavum ōrīṟu koṇṭaṉavum oṉṟi yoṉṟu paṟṟuvaṉavumāṉa pala kuṟiyīṭukaḷip putitāyp paṭittu avaṟṟic cevviyāy viḷakkavum iyalātu iṭarppaṭuvatu, vaṇṇaṉi moḻinūlāciriyar vaḻakkamāka iruntu varukiṉṟatu. itu mikapparanta ceytiyātaliṉ, iti virikkātu immaṭṭil niṟuttukiṉṟēṉ.
8. olikkuṟikaḷi (Phonetic Symbols) aḷavip paṭuttātatu
vaṇṇaṉimoḻi nūlārāl oppukkoḷḷappeṟum pala eḻut toli nūlkaḷ, vevvēṟu kuṟikaḷik kiyāṇṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi iṉṉum oru neṟippaṭuttiyilar.
oruvar ellā moḻikaḷiyum tāymoḻiyākavuṭiyār pōṉṟē pēcaviyalum eṉpatu, vaṇṇaṉi moḻi nūṟkoḷkiyar tarukku. āyiṉ, itukāṟum oruvarum ataṉi meypittuk kāṭṭiyilar.
9. kiḷi vaḻakkip perukkuvatu
iṭavaḻakkum kulavaḻakkum paṟṟi ovvoru perumoḻiyilum cila kiḷivaḻakkukaḷ (Dialects) ēṟpaṭuvatu iyalpē. āyiṉ, ñālaviyaṟ kiḷivaḻakku (Geographical Dialect) eṉṟum, ūrkkiḷi vaḻakku (Dialect of a village town or city) eṉṟum, kūṭṭaraviyaṟ kiḷivaḻakku, (Social Dialect) eṉṟum, kuṭumpak kiḷi vaḻakku (Familly Dialect) eṉṟum, taṉippaṭṭavar kiḷivaḻakku (Individual Dialect) eṉṟum, vaṇṇaṉimoḻi nūlār varampiṉṟi vakuttukkāṭṭuvatu viyappiṉum viyappē.
10. pēcuvatē moḻi eṉpatu
pēccu vaḻakkiluḷḷatē uṇmiyāṉa moḻiyeṉṟum, eḻuttu vaḻakkiluḷḷatellām ēṭṭu moḻiyēyeṉṟum, tiruntiya vaḻakkeṉṟum kocci vaḻakkeṉṟum vēṟupāṭilliyeṉṟum, kocci vaḻakku evvaḷavu kēṭāyt tirintiruppiṉum atellāṅ koḷḷat takkatē yeṉṟum, vaṇṇaṉi moḻi nūlār ēṉittiripuṭi moḻikaṭkuk kūṟuvatu pōṉṟē iyaṉmoḻiyum ilakkaṇac cemmoḻiyumākiya  tamiḻiṟkum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
piṟa moḻikaṭkillāta cemmiyeṉṉum varampu tamiḻukkuṇmi yiyum, iṭikkālattil tamiḻ āriyattāl aḻippuṇṭatiyum, ik kālattum tamiḻ vaḷarcci pakivarāl taṭuppuṇṭu varuvatiyum, avar aṟiyār.
tamiḻil oruvaṉ eṅṅaṉam piḻipaṭap pēciṉum, mēṭiyēṟip pēcuṅkālum ēṭeṭutteḻutum pōtum ilakkaṇa naṭiyik kiyāḷa vēṇṭumeṉpatu toṉṟu toṭṭuvarum marapu.
``eppaṭi eccoliṉ evvā ṟuyarntōr
ceppiṉar appaṭic cepputal marapē''
tamiḻnāṭṭil mikak kocciyāyp pēcappaṭum māvaṭṭam vaṭārkkāṭu. aṅkum aṅkattu naṭiyil mēṭiyēṟip pēcuvārum nūleḻutuvārum oruvarumilar. ittakiya varampuṇmiyālēyē tamiḻ iṉṟuvari iruntu varukiṉṟatu.
iṭikkālattil, tamiḻ citikkappaṭṭamiyālum; tamiḻkkāvalarāṉa pulavar piḻippaṟṟup pōṉamiyālum, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa aruntamiḻc coṟkaḷ maṟintu vēṟṟu moḻic coṟkaḷ vēṇṭātu pukuntu viṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl, mukil, eḻili, mañcu, kār, māl, vāṉ eṉap palacoṟkaḷ iruppavum, mēkam eṉṉum vaṭacollē vaḻaṅkavum, mukil eṉṉum taṉ coṟku mēkam eṉṉum ayaṟ collāṟ poruḷ kūṟavum nērntu viṭṭatu. itu tamiḻar virumpi mēṟkoṇṭa nilimiyaṉṟu. āṇkaḷ peṇkaḷ eṉṉum vaḻaṅku tamiḻccoṟkaṭkup patilākap puruṣar tirīkaḷ eṉṉum vaḻaṅkā vaṭacoṟkaḷip pukivaṇṭi niliyaṅkaḷiṟ pukuttiyiruppatum, vāṉoli eṉpati ākācavāṇi eṉṟu māṟṟiyiruppatum, tamiḻik keṭuttaṟkum ataṉ vaḷarcciyit taṭuppataṟkum ciṟanta eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷām.
tamiḻar takka vaḻikāṭṭiyiṉṟit tāymoḻiyuṇarcciyaṟṟuppōy, ēṟkaṉavē kāṟpaṅku vaṭacoṟkaḷik kalantatoṭu iṉṟu arippaṅku āṅkilac coṟkaḷiyum kalantu, iṭiyiṭi pāracīkam arapi mutaliya piṟa moḻic coṟkaḷiyum peytu, kalavi naṭiyāka vaḻaṅkum kacaṭṭu moḻi. pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷākap paṇpaṭutticcīriyōr vaḷartta centami ḻākumō!
vēliyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭam mikka ikkālattil, iḷiñar cilar tam takutiyip perukki varuvāyi yuyartta viyāpurikaḷi vaḻipaṭṭu vaṭaamerikkā ceṉṟu īrāṇṭiruntu mīṇṭu, ārāycciyeṉṉum peyarāl aruntamiḻik kulittu varuvatu ettuṇi iraṅkat takka ceyti!
avarkaḷ eṉṉum colliṉ kocci vaṭivukaḷāṉa avaka, avuka, avika, aviya, aviyal avaṅka, avuṅka, aviṅka, avā eṉpavaṟṟi yellām aḷaviyākak koṇṭu ārāyvatiṉum, nakarāṇmik kuppiyik kiṇṭik kiḷippatu naṉmipayakkumē!
tamiḻit tamiḻnāṭṭil takkār vāykkallātu amerikkā ceṉṟu āyvataṟkut tamiḻolikaḷit tavaṟāka viyāpurikaḷ vāyk kēṭṭup pativu ceyta olippeṭṭikaḷaṉṟi vēṟu aṅku eṉṉuḷḷatu? ulaka vaḻakkum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkum ārāyātu cila olikkuṟikaḷi maṭṭum amerikkāviṟ kaṟṟuvantatiṉāl eṉṉa payaṉ? akkuṟikaḷi iṅkiruntē kaṟkalāmē!
tamiḻar pēcuvatellām tamiḻeṉiṉ, kūl, kālēj, kiḷā. ṭiviṣaṉ, pīriyaṭu, heṭmāṭṭar, cār, (Sir) puk, nōṭpuk, pēppar, peṉ, peṉcil, rappar, pēkku, (bag) kārṭiyaṉ līvu, lēṭ (Late), ṭirāyiṅ, cayaṉcu mutaliya ceyalkaḷellām tamiḻātal vēṇṭumē! aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, tamiḻ āṅkilattiṉ kiḷimoḻiyāvaṉṟō māṟiviṭum! aṅṅaṉam māṟāmi aṉivarum aṟivar. ātalāl, iṭikkālattiruṇ niliyil ēṟpaṭṭa kēṭṭiyum ataṉ vaḻivanta tīṅkukaḷiyum aṟavē nīkki, tamiḻip paṇṭupōl tūymip paṭuttuvatē, aṟivārāycciyum urimiyuṇarcciyum mikka iṟṟit tamiḻark kēṟṟatām.
iṉi, malivāṇarāṉa tiraviṭarellām paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷeṉṟum, avarkaḷ pēcuvaṉa muntiya moḻikaḷeṉṟum, tamiḻ valliṉam vaṭamoḻi aivarukkamutalolikaḷi yottateṉṟum, tamiḻ arivari vaṭamoḻi neṭuṅkaṇakkip piṉpaṟṟiyateṉṟum, cila tavaṟāṉa karuttukaḷ mēli
moḻi nūlaṟiñariṭit toṉṟutoṭṭu iruntuvarukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiyellām maṟuppiṉ maṟṟoṉṟu virittalākum.
āyiṉum, kuṟiñci nilattiluḷḷa māntar tamiḻar nākarika vaḷarcciyiṉ mutal niliyi tāṅki niṉṟa kālam muḻukippōṉa kumarikkaṇṭattilēyē kaṭantu viṭṭateṉṟum, iṟṟi nāvalantēya malivāṇarellām maliyaṭivārattil māṭumēyttum uḻavuttoḻil ceytum vantu akkālattu aṭikkaṭi nikaḻntu vanta pōrukkum koḷḷikkum tappumāṟu malimēṟceṉṟu vāḻntavar vaḻiyiṉareṉṟum; tamiḻ valliṉa volikaḷ vaṭamoḻi aivarukka mutalolikaḷiṉum melliyaveṉṟum; tamiḻ neṭuṅ kaṇakkē vaṭamoḻi neṭuṅkaṇakkiṟku mūlameṉṟum; īṇṭikkaṟintu koḷka.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, varalāṟṟic ciṟitum nōkkātu, vaṭa moḻiyit talimiyākavum tiraviṭamoḻikaḷiyellām oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu camamākavum koṇṭu, tamiḻukkukkēṭu viḷiyumāṟu koccic coṟkaḷiyum piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷiyum aḷavippaṭuttuvatē vaṇṇaṉi moḻi nūliṉ iyalpeṉṟum, itu tamiḻik kāṭṭik koṭuttut tannalam perukkum viyāpurikaṭkut taliyāya vāyppeṉṟum, kaṇṭukoḷka.
4
poruṭ pākupāṭu
poruḷkaḷic ciṉi, kuṇam, toḻil mutaliyaṉapaṟṟip pala vakiyāka vakuttuk kūṟal poruṭ pākupāṭākum. ituvē entak kaliyaṟi viṟkum ārāyccikkum toṭakkamām. oru poruḷippaṟṟic ciṟappā yārāyumuṉ, atu enta iṉattic cērntateṉṟu aṟintu koḷvatu iṉṟiyamiyātatākum.
taṟkālattil, poruḷkaḷip paṟṟiya ārāycciyil mēṉāṭṭār ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, muṟkālattilō inniliyik kīḻnāṭṭār, ciṟappākat tamiḻar, aṭintiruntaṉar.
paṇṭi tamiḻar, poruḷkaḷellāvaṟṟiyum mika nuṭpamāka ārāyntu avaṟṟi vakuttuk kūṟal ōriṉappaṭuttal vēṟiṉappaṭuttal eṉṉum mūvakiyilum vakiyīṭu ceytatu mikap pōṟṟattakkatākum.
1. vakuttuk kūṟal
(1) iruvakip poruḷ-iyaṅkutiṇi, nilittiṇi.
ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum iṭam peyarvatum peyarāmiyum paṟṟi iyaṅkutiṇi nilittiṇi eṉa iraṇṭāy vakuttuk kūṟiṉar. ivaṟṟi muṟiyē caram acaram eṉṟum caṅkamam tāvaram eṉṟum kūṟuvar vaṭanūlār. iyaṅkutiṇi uyiruḷḷatu maṭṭum. nilittiṇiyil uyiruḷḷatum illatum aṭaṅkum.
(2) mūvakipporuḷ-uyir, mey, uyirmey.
ellāp poruḷkaḷum taṉiyuyir, taṉiyuṭampu, uyiruḷḷa uṭampu eṉa mūvakippaṭum. ivaṟṟi muṟiyē uyir mey uyirmey eṉṟaṉar paḻantamiḻar. kaṭavuḷum pēyum uṭalip pirinta āviyum taṉiyuyir; piṇamum uyiraṟṟa aḵṟiṇi yuṭampum uyiraṟṟa poruḷum taṉimey; uṭampōṭu kūṭi vāḻum makkaḷ vilaṅku paṟavi mutaliyaṉa uyarmey. uyirmey eṉṉum col iṉṟu vaḻakkaṟṟu, ataṟkup patilākap pirāṇi eṉṉum vaṭacol vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. pirāṇaṉiyuṭiyatu pirāṇi, uyiriyuṭiya mey uyirmey.
uyir mey uyirmey eṉṉum mūvakip poruṭ peyarkaḷiyē, mūvaki yeḻuttukkaḷukkum avaṟṟōṭoppumi karutiyiṭṭaṉar ilakkaṇikaḷ eṉka.
mūvakip poruḷkaḷilum uyirum uyirmeyyum iyaṅku tiṇiyum meynilittiṇiyumākum.
uyir meyyic curukkam paṟṟi uyiri eṉṟu kūṟalām. piṟkālattil mata nūlār poruḷkaḷiyellāvaṟṟiyum kaṭavuḷ āṉmā kaṭṭu eṉa mūvakiyākap pakuttaṉar.
(3) aṟuvaki yuyir
ellā yuyirikaḷiyum, aimpulaṉum pakuttaṟivumākiya āṟaṟivu paṟṟi, ōraṟiyuyir mutal āṟaṟivuyir vari āṟākap pakuttiruntārkaḷ.
``oṉṟaṟi vatuvē uṟṟaṟi vatuvē
iraṇṭaṟi vatuvē ataṉoṭu nāvē
mūṉṟaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu mūkkē
nāṉkaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu kaṇṇē
aintaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu ceviyē
āṟaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu maṉaṉē
nēritiṉ uṇarntōr neṟip paṭuttiṉarē''	(572)
``pullum maraṉum ōraṟi viṉavē
piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē''	(573)
``nantum muraḷum īraṟi viṉavē
piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē''	(574)
	nantu = natti, muraḷ = caṅku.
``citalum eṟumpum mūvaṟi viṉavē
piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē''	(575)
	cital = kaṟiyāṉ
``naṇṭum tumpiyum nāṉkaṟi viṉavē
piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē''	(576)
``māvu mākkaḷum aiyaṟi viṉavē
piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē''	(577)
``makkaḷ tāmē āṟaṟi yuyirē
piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē''	(578)
ivi tolkāppiya nūṟpāk (cūttiram) kaḷ. tolkāppiyattiṉ kālam ki.mu. 2000 ātalālum, atu oru vaḻi nūlātalālum, ``nēritiṉ uṇarntōr neṟippaṭuttiṉarē'' eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatālum, mēṟkūṟiya ceytikaḷ toṉṟu toṭṭu neṭuṅkālamāka vaḻaṅki aṭippaṭṭa vaḻakku kaḷik kūṟum marapiyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruppatālum, tamiḻariṉ āṟuyirp pākupāṭu eṇṇukku meṭṭāta toṉmit teṉṟaṟiyalām.
iṉi, oraṟivuyiriyum maṉitariyum ivviruvakiyākap pakuttatu mika viyakkattakkatākum.
``pullu maraṉum oraṟi viṉavē'' eṉṉumpōtē ōraṟivuyir iraṇṭākap pakukkappaṭṭuviṭṭatu. itaṉāl, pullukkum marattukkum iṭiyil uḷḷa pūṇṭu, putar, ceṭi, koṭi mutaliyavum pulliṉ vakiyāyaṭaṅkum eṉpatu pulaṉākum. āṉāl, mūṅkilāṉatu uṟuppāl pullāyum uyarattāl maramāyumiruppatāl, atu evvakiyuḷ aṭaṅkum eṉṉum aiyatti nīkkuvataṟku
``puṟakkā ḻaṉavē pulleṉṉamoḻipa''	(tol.626)
``akakkāḻaṉavē marameṉa moḻipa''	(tol. 627)
eṉṟaṉar.
ivaṟṟuḷ, ``moḻipa'' eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatāl ivi muṉṉōr kūṟṟeṉṟaṟital vēṇṭum. puṟakkāḻ veḷivayiramuḷḷatu, akakkāḻ uḷvayiramuḷḷatu. ākavē, mūṅkil pulliṉ vakiyeṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
ōraṟivuyir pōṉṟē māntariyum makkaḷ mākkaḷ eṉa iru vakiyākap pirittaṉar. maṉita vaṭivukoṇṭa māttirattilēyē oruvaṉ maṉitaṉākamāṭṭaṉeṉṟum avaṉ maṉitaṉāvaṉeṉṟum tamiḻ muṉṉōr kaṇṭuṅkoṇṭu miruntaṉar. ataṉāl, aṟivoḻukkamuḷḷavari makkaḷeṉṟum aviyillātavari mākkaḷ (vilaṅkukaḷ) eṉṟum kūṟiṉar. iti.
``māvu mākkaḷum aiyaṟi viṉavē'' 
``makkaḷ tāmē āṟaṟi vuyirē''	(tol. )
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷālum,
``ceviyiṟ cuviyuṇarā vāyuṇarviṉ mākkaḷ
aviyiṉum vāḻiṉum eṉ'' 	(kuṟaḷ.. )
``makkaḷē pōlvar kayavar avaraṉṉa
oppāri, yāṅkaṇṭa til''	(kuṟaḷ.. )
``vilaṅkoṭu makka ḷaṉiyar ilaṅkunūl
kaṟṟārō ṭēṉi yavar''	(kuṟaḷ.. )
eṉṉum kuṟaṭpākkaḷālum aṟiyalākum. makkaḷukku nūlaṟivillā viṭṭālum oḻukkattiṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa pakuttaṟiviruntāṟ pōtumeṉpatu muṉṉōr karuttu. ākavē, makkaḷukku oḻukkam iṉṟiyamiyātatu. makkaḷ eṉṉum peyarukku makaṉ eṉpatum mākkaḷ eṉṉum peyarukku mā eṉpatum orumiyākum. makaṉ makaḷ eṉṉum orumip peyarum makkaḷ eṉṉum paṉmip peyarum, maṉitap poruḷum muṟimip poruḷum oruṅku koṇṭiruttaliṉ, avaṟṟāl mayakkamuṇṭākāmip poruṭṭu, maṉitap poruḷiyē tarutaṟku māntaṉ eṉṉum peyar piṟkālattil tōṉṟiṉatākat terikiṉṟatu. māntaṉukkup palar pāl māntar; peṇpāl māntiyākat tōṉṟukiṟatu.
``māntar makkaḷ eṉṉum peyarum.....
appatiṇintum avaṟṟōraṉṉa''	(tol. 647)
eṉṟu māntar eṉṉum peyari uyartiṇiyākat tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
(4) irutiṇi-uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇi.
tamiḻilakkaṇattil, poruḷkaḷellām uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi eṉa irutiṇiyāka vakukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itu tamiḻariṉ ciṟanta aṟiviyum nākarikattiyum kāṭṭuvatākum. tiṇi = kulam.
pakuttaṟivuḷḷa allatu oḻukkamuḷḷa mēṉmakkaḷ uyartiṇi yākavum, avarallāta kīḻmakkaḷum uyiruḷḷaṉavum uyirillaṉavumākiya piṟaporuḷkaḷum aḵṟiṇiyākavum kūṟappaṭṭaṉar. oruvaṉukkup pakuttaṟiviruntum oḻukkamillāviṭiṉ avaṉ uyartiṇiyākāṉ. ākiyāl, pakuttaṟiveṉpatu eppōtum oḻukkattiyum taṉṉuṭaṉ uḷappaṭuttum.
al + tiṇi = aḵṟiṇi, uyarvallāta tiṇi aḵṟiṇi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
``uyartiṇi yeṉmaṉār makkaṭ cuṭṭē
aḵṟiṇi yeṉmaṉār avarala piṟavē	(tol.)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam.
itil makkaḷ eṉṉum vakuppāriyē uyartiṇiyākak kūṟiyuḷḷatu.
āṉāl, piṟkālattil 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya pavaṇantiyār,
``makkaḷ tēvar narakar uyartiṇi
maṟṟuyir uḷḷavum illavum aḵṟiṇi''
eṉa, uyartiṇiyuṭaṉ tēvar narakariyum cērttatumaṉṟi, tolkāppiyar viḷakkiya kīḻmakkaḷiyum uyartiṇiyākak kūṟiviṭṭār.
ulakil tōṉṟiya makkaḷē, immiyiṟceyta nalviṉi mikutiyāl maṟumiyil tēvarāyp piṟappareṉpatum, tēvarulakil vīṭu pēṟṟu muyaṟciyē yilliyeṉpatum,
koḷkikaḷātaliṉ tēvappiṟappu makkaṭ piṟappiṉuñ ciṟantataṉ ṟeṉpatum, tēvarum makkaḷāyaṭaṅkuvar eṉpatum peṟappaṭum. āṉāl, narakarōveṉiṉ muṟpiṟappil mikutiyum tīmiceytu ataṉāl taṇṭaṉi yaṭiyum kīḻmakkaḷātaliṉ, avariyum uyartiṇiyākak kūṟiyatu tamiḻilakkaṇattiṟkē muṟṟum māṟāṉatākum.
iṉi, `makkaḷ tēvar', eṉṉum naṉṉūl nūṟpāvil, `narakar' eṉpati nākar eṉṟu māṟṟikkoṇṭu, ``makkaḷ, tēvar, nākar'' eṉpavar muṟiyē tamiḻarum, āriyarum, nākarumāvar eṉakkūṟiya tāṉtōṉṟit tamiḻp pakivarumuḷar.
(5) aṟuvakipporuḷ
tamiḻilakkaṇattil, peyariyalil, maṟṟoruvakiyil poruḷ kaḷellām poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, ciṉi, kuṇam, toḻil eṉa aṟuvaki yākap pakukkappaṭaṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ poruḷeṉṟatu iṭam peyarakkūṭiya uṭpulap poruḷi, ciṉi uṟuppu.
iṉi, nūṟporuḷkaḷi, akam puṟam eṉṟiraṇṭākavum mutal, karu, uri eṉa mūṉṟākavum vakuppatu poruḷilakkaṇa marapum aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭeṉa nāṉkāka vakuppatu nītinūṉ marapum, poruḷ kuṇam karumam potu ciṟappu oṟṟumi iṉmi eṉa ēḻāka vakuppatu tarukka nūṉmarapumākum.
ivaṟṟuḷ, akam eṉpatu kātal; puṟam eṉpatu aḵtallāta piṟa poruḷkaḷ; mutal eṉpatu kālamum iṭamum; karu eṉpatu ōriṭattil tōṉṟum poruḷkaḷum kātalallāta viṉikaḷum; uri eṉpatu kātal allatu kātaṟpakuti. piṟavaṟṟi vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka; iṅku kūṟiṉ viriyum.
2 ōriṉappaṭuttal
ōriṉappaṭuttalāvatu vevvēṟiṉattic cērnta pala poruḷkaḷi ōr oppumipaṟṟi oriṉamākak kūṟal.
yāṉikkum paṉṟikkum kaḷiṟu kēḻal eṉpaṉa potup peyarkaḷ. ivviru vilaṅkum ōriṉam eṉpatu avaṟṟiṉ vaṭivālum niṟattālum viḷaṅkum. kaḷiṟu eṉpatu yāṉiyāl paṉṟikkum kēḻal eṉpatu paṉṟiyāl yāṉikkum ēṟpaṭṭa peyar. kaḷippatu kaḷiṟu. kaḷittal mataṅkoḷḷutal. mataṅkoḷvatu āṇyāṉi. yāṉiyōṭoppumipaṟṟip paṉṟiyiṉ āṇum kaḷiṟeṉappaṭṭatu. kiḷippatu kīḻvatu kēḻal, kiḷittal nilatti muṇṭippōṭutal. paṉṟiyōṭoppumipaṟṟi yāṉiyum kēḻal eṉappaṭṭatu.
yāṉikkum oru vaki vaṇṭiṟkum tumpi eṉappeyar. tumpi yuṭiyatu tumpi. tumpu eṉpatu tūmpu. tūmpāvatu uṭṭuḷiyuḷḷatu allatu kuḻāy. kuḻāypōṉṟa oru muṉṉūṭṭi (proboscis) yāṉikkum tumpi yeṉṉum vaṇṭukku miruttal kāṇka. yāṉikkut tutikkiyum vaṇṭukkut tēṉuṟiñcum neṭu mūkkum muṉṉūṭṭiyākum. yāṉiyiṉ tutikki tumpikki yeṉappaṭukiṟatu. itu mutalāvatu tumpiyiṉ ki eṉap poruḷpaṭṭu, iṉṟu poruḷ maṟintu, akkikkē ciṟappup peyarāy allatu taṉippeyarāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
aracu āṟṟaracu pūvaracu eṉṉum maraṅkaḷ orē peyarip peṟ ṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. immummaraṅkaḷum neñcāṅkulivaṭivāṉa (Cordate) iliyuṭiyaṉavākum. aracamaram veḷippaṭiyākap pūkkātatu. itaṉāl atu pūvillātateṉṟē karutappaṭṭatu. 
``pūvātu kāykkum maramuḷa''
eṉṟār ōḷaviyār.
pūppatāy aracu pōṉṟa iliyuṭiyatu pūvaracu. aracu pōṉṟa iliyuṭiyatāy āṟṟōrattil vaḷarvatu āṟṟaracu.
(3) vēṟiṉappaṭuttal
vēṟiṉappaṭuttalāvatu orēyiṉattic cērnta poruḷkaḷi nuṭpa vēṟupāṭupaṟṟi vēṟupaṭuttik kūṟal.
``vaṇṭun tēṉu ñimiṟuñ curumpu
mumiḻnaṟa varunti yuṟaṅku ceñcaṭiyōṉ'' 	(55)
eṉpatu kallāṭam.
``vaṇṭu tēṉ ñimiṟu curumpu vaṇṭiṉ vakikaḷ'' eṉṟār. ataṉ uriyāciriyar.
``mutaliyum iṭaṅkarum karāmum'' (kuṟiñcippāṭṭu, 267) eṉa mutali mūvakiyākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu. mūṅkilil, uṭṭuḷiyuḷḷatu vēy eṉṟum uṭṭuḷiyillātatu ami eṉṟum muḷḷuḷḷatu kaḻi eṉṟum muḷḷillātatu paṉi eṉṟum piṟa piṟavāṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟuḷ, vēyum amiyum ciṟu mūṅkilum kaḻiyum paṇiyum peru mūṅkilumākum. ippōtu vēy tuḷimūṅkileṉṟum ami kalmūṅkileṉṟum vaḻaṅku kiṉṟatu. ilikaḷ vaṭivum kaṉamumpaṟṟi, ili tāḷ tōki ōli eṉa nālvakiyākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum, itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, tamiḻ muṉṉōr mika nuṇṇaṟivuṭiyavarā yiruntaṉar eṉpatum poruḷkaḷi naṉṟāyārāyntu avaṟṟip palavakiyiṟ pākupaṭuttiṉar eṉpatum, oru poruṭ pala peyar kaḷil ovvoṉṟum ovvoru nuṭpa vēṟupāṭṭik kāṭṭumeṉpatum aṟiyappaṭum.
5
``ulaka vaḻakku kocci vaḻakkaṉṟu''
ilakkiyamuḷḷa ellā moḻikaḷilum ulaka vaḻakkum ilakkiya vaḻakkum eṉac colvaḻakku iruvakippaṭṭuttāṉ irukkum. āyiṉ tamiḻil maṭṭum paṇṭiyilakkiyamellām ceyyuḷilēyē iruntami yāl, ilakkiya vaḻakkuc ceyyuḷ vaḻakkeṉappaṭṭatu.
``vaṭa vēṅkaṭan teṉkumari
āyiṭit
tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu
vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum āyirumutaliṉ''
eṉṟu paṉampāraṉār kūṟutal kāṇka. itil ``vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum'' eṉṟu curukki kūṟiyatu muṟiyē ulaka vaḻakkiyum, ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiyum. potu makkaḷum pulamakkaḷumākiya ellār vāyilum vaḻaṅkutal paṟṟi, ulaka vaḻakkē vaḻakkeṉac ciṟappittu kūṟappaṭṭatu. ciṟupāṉmiyarāṉa pula - makkaḷē pāṭum ceyyu
ḷiruttal paṟṟic ceyyuḷ vaḻaṅkuc ceyyuḷeṉṟē kuṟikkappaṭṭatu.
``pāṭṭuri nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē
aṅkatam mutu colō ṭaṅkēḻ nilattum
vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varippiṉ
nāṟpeya relli yakattavar vaḻaṅkum
yāppiṉ vaḻiya teṉmaṉār pulavar''	(tol.)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā urinaṭiyilirukka vēṇṭiya mūlac ceyyuḷuriyum kallā makkaḷum kūṟum viṭukatikaḷum, paṇṭi nāḷiṟ ceyyuḷ vaṭiviliruntamiyik kuṟittal kāṇka.
uriyiṉ oruvakiyē aruñcoṟkaṭkup poruḷ kūṟum uriccoṟṟokutiyum (nikaṇṭum) tivākaram, piṅkalam mutaliya uriccoṟ ṟokutikaḷ iṉṟum ceyyuḷ vaṭivilēyē iruttali nōkkuka.
ulakam eṉṉum iṭavāku peyar ulakattiluḷḷa makkaḷellāriyum potuvāka kuṟippiṉum, col vaḻakkip poṟuttavariyil, nākarikattilum aṟiviluñ ciṟanta paṇpuṭi makkaḷiyē ciṟappākavuṇarttum
``vaḻakkeṉap paṭuva tuyarntōr mēṟṟē
nikaḻcci yavarkaṭṭākalāṉa''	(tol. )
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam pirinili yēkāraṅ koṭuttu variyaṟuttuk kūṟutal kāṇka.
iṅṅaṉamiruppavum, tamiḻp pakivarāṉa āriyarum avar vaḻippaṭṭa mēliyarum kōṭaṉmārum tamiḻiyalpaṟiyāt tamiḻ pērāciriyarum, kallā makkaḷum, kīḻ makkaḷ vaḻaṅkum iḻivaḻakkiyum (Slang), kocci vaḻakkiyum (Barbarism) ulakavaḻakkoṭu (Golleqsualism) cērttoṉṟāka koṇṭu, tamiḻ marapoṭum ilakkaṇa nūlāroṭum muraṇpaṭuvar.
maṉiviyip peṇcāti eṉpatum, pīrkkaṅkāyi pīccaṅkā eṉpatum ivipōṉṟa piṟavum iḻivaḻakkām. vantatu eṉpati vantuccu eṉṟum, vittirukkiṟāṉ eṉpati vaccirukkāṉ eṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvatu iḻivaḻakkām. ittakiya vaḻakkukaḷiṉṟi ilakkaṇa naṭiyiṟ pēcuvatē uyarntōr vaḻakkāṉa ulaka vaḻakkam.
āyiṉ, cila teṉcoṟkaḷ kaṟṟārkkēyaṉṟi maṟṟōrkkup poruḷ viḷaṅkātaṉavākavum, avaṟṟiṟku nērāṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷum piṟa coṟkaḷum eḷitiṟ poruḷuṇarttuvaṉavākavum iruttalālum, karuttaṟivikkuṅ karuviyē moḻiyātalālum, tūyanaṭi tamiḻukkēṟkāteṉiṉ, cila teṉ coṟkaḷi vaḻaṅkāmiyālēyē avi aruñcoṟkaḷāka māṟiṉa veṉṟum, orukālattil eḷiya coṟkaḷākavē avi vaḻaṅkivantaṉa eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉ vaḻakkip putuppippiṉ mīṇṭum avi eḷiya coṟkaḷāka māṟiviṭumeṉṟum, tamiḻiṉ vāḻviṟkum vaḷarccikkum ataṉ colvaḷam iṉṟiyami
yātateṉṟum, vaṭacoṟkaḷ tēvamoḻic colleṉṉum ēmāṟṟiṉālēyē tēviyiṉṟi vaṭavarāl pukuttappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟum, avaṟṟit tārāḷamāka vaḻaṅkiṉatiṉālēyē tamiḻar tāymoḻi uṇarcci iḻantu piṟa urimi
yuṇarcciyum taṉmāṉappaṇpum mikka ikkālattil tamiḻit tūymiyāka vaḻaṅkutalē takkateṉṟum, atuvē tamiḻaṉ muṉṉēṟum vaḻiyeṉṟum, piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷ tamiḻar tāmāka virumpik kaṭaṉ koṇṭavi alla eṉṟum, tamiḻiṉ tūymiyik kulippavarellām vāḷpōṟ pakivarum, kēḷpōṟ pakivarumē yeṉṟum kūṟiviṭukka.
iṉi vaṭamoḻittuṇiyiṉṟit tamiḻit taṉittu vaḻaṅka iyalāteṉṟum, irucār pakivarum kūṟik kalāyppar. ulakiluḷḷa mūvāyiram moḻikaḷuḷ, tamiḻ oṉṟē tēvamoḻiyeṉṟum .................... vaḷampaṭuttiya moḻiyeṉṟum tampaṭṭamaṭittuk koḷḷum vaṭamoḻiyil aintiliru pakuti tamiḻēyeṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyē tamiḻt tuṇiyiṉṟi vaḻaṅkāteṉṟum, ivvuṇmi aṇmiyilēyē veṭṭa veḷiccamāku meṉṟum, avar aṟivārāka.
ceyyuḷ vaḻakku, kuṟippiṭṭa coṟkaḷiyum, col vaṭivukaḷiyumē vaḻaṅki varuvatālum, curuṅkac collal eṉṉum aḻakip peritākap pēṇuvatālum, ulaka vaḻakkiṉiṉṟu mikavum vēṟupaṭṭatākum.
paṇṭic ceyyuḷiḷellām kāṭi, kavutāri eṉṉum paṟavikaḷ kuṟumpūḻ, cival eṉṉum coṟkaḷālēyē muṟiyē kuṟikkappeṟum. ceyyū, ceypu, ceyteṉa, ceyyiyar, ceyyiya, ceymmaṉ, ceyiṉ mutaliya ceyyuḷ viṉiyecca vaṭivukaḷ ulaka vaḻakkilum ceytu koṇṭu, ceykiṟataṟku, ceytāl, ceytuviṭṭāl mutaliya ulaka vaḻakku viṉiyecca vaṭivukaḷ ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilum iṭampeṟuvatilli.
muḻuṅku  eṉṉum vaṭivē mūlamāyiṉum, ataṉ tiripāṉa viḻuṅku eṉpati ceyyuṭkēṟṟatākak koḷḷappeṟum. iṅṅaṉamē miñcu eṉpatiṉum ataṉ tiripāṉa viñcu eṉpatē ciṟantatām. koṇṭuvā eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa koṇṭā eṉpatu ceyyuḷil iṭampeṟātu; āyiṉ koṇṭā eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa koṇā, koṇar eṉpavi iṭampeṟum.
makkaḷi uyarntōr, ottōr, iḻintōr eṉa mūvakuppārāka vakuttu ataṟkēṟpa īṟukoṭuttup peyariyum viṉiyiyum āḷvatu muṟṟum ulakavaḻakkēyaṉṟic ceyyuḷ vaḻakkaṉṟu. orumi paṉmi eṉṉum eṇ pakuppē ceyyuṭ kuriyatām.
	peyar	viṉi
iḻintōṉ	nīṉ, nī, avaṉ	vā, vantāṉ
ottōṉ	nīm, nīr (nīyir) avar	vārum, vantār
uyarntōṉ	nīṅkaḷ, avarkaḷ	vāruṅkaḷ, vantārkaḷ
``oruvarik kūṟum paṉmik kiḷaviyum
oṉṟaṉik kūṟum paṉmik kiḷaviyum
vaḻakki ṉākiya uyarcoṟ kiḷavi
ilakkaṇa maruṅkiṉ collā ṟalla.''
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam (col - kiḷavi - 27) teḷivākak kūṟutal kāṇka.
iṉi viṉiyiṉ mukkāla vaṭivum āṅkilattiṟpōl taṉittaṉi taṉippu (Indifinite), toṭarcci (Continuous), niṟivu (Perfect), niṟivut toṭarcci (Perfect Continuous) eṉṉum nālvaki koṇṭiyaṅkuvatum, tamiḻil ulaka vaḻakkē aṉṟic ceyyuḷ vaḻakkaṉṟu. cilar ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiyē aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, ulaka vaḻakkiṟkuc ciṟappāka uriya kūṟukaḷiyellām, piṟkālattaṉavākak kūṟuvar. avar moḻiyiyali ciṟappākat tamiḻiyali aṟiyār. ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiṉum ulaka vaḻakkē muntiyateṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum.
iṟantakālam
taṉippu -vantāṉ
toṭarcci - vantukoṇṭiruntāṉ
niṟivu - vantiruntāṉ
niṟivu toṭarcci - vantu koṇṭiruntāṉ.
nikaḻkālam
taṉippu - varukiṉṟāṉ
toṭarcci - vantu koṇṭirukkiṉṟāṉ
niṟivu - vantirukkiṉṟāṉ
niṟivu toṭarcci - vantu koṇṭiruntirukkiṟāṉ
etirkālam
taṉippu - varuvāṉ
toṭarcci - vantu koṇṭiruppāṉ
niṟivu - vantiruppāṉ
niṟivut toṭarcci - vantu koṇṭiruntiruppāṉ
iṟantakāla niṟivut toṭarcci iṉṟu vaḻakku
vīḻntatu. etirkāla niṟivum, niṟivut toṭarcciyum aiya iṟanta kālattiyum uṇarttum.
āṅkila nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccattiṟku (Present Participle) otta ceytu koṇṭu eṉṉum tamiḻt toṭarcci viṉiyecca vāyppāṭu, toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakkēyaṉṟi iṭikkālattatō, āṅkilattip piṉpaṟṟiyatō aṉṟu. koḷ eṉṉum taṟporuṭṭu (Reflexive) tuṇiviṉi (Auxiliary Verb) eṅṅaṉam tamiḻ marapiṉatō aṅṅaṉamē koṇṭu eṉṉum toṭarccikālat tuṇiviṉiyeccamum tamiḻ marapiṉatām. tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlār nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccamākak kuṟikkum ceyya eṉṉum vāypāṭu, taṉ eḻuvāy viṉiyoṭu muṭiyum pōtu, āṅkilap poruṭṭu viṉiyeccatti (Infinitive Mood) yottu etirkālattiyuṇarttuvatāl, uṇmiyil etirkāla viṉiyeccamēyām. ceyiṉ (ceytāl) eṉṉum kuṟippatē takkatu.
taṉippu eṉṉum kālavaki vaḻakkuñ ceyyum eṉṉum iruvaki vaḻakkiṟkum potuvām niṟivum ceyyuṭku ēṟkum. (e-kā) ``ētu nikaḻcci yetirntuḷḷa tātaliṉ (maṇi - 3 : 4) etirntuḷatu etirntirukkiṉṟatu. (nikaḻ kāla niṟivu) toṭarcci viṉiyeccam poruḷ ceyyuḷil muṟṟeccattāl uṇarttap peṟum.
e-kā :
``āṭiṉir pāṭiṉir celiṉē''	(puṟam - 109 :17)
āṭiṉir pāṭiṉir = āṭikkoṇṭum, pāṭikkoṇṭum iṭa vēṟṟumi urupēṟṟa toḻiṟ peyarum ilakkiya vaḻakkil toṭarcci viṉip poruḷuṇarttum.
e-kā : taṭṭuppuṭikkaṇ - taṭṭuppuṭittuk koṇṭirunta (koṇ ṭirukkiṉṟa, koṇṭirukkum) pōtu.
niṟivut toṭarcci ceyyuḷil iṭam peṟātu.
ceyyuḷ vaḻakku ulaka vaḻakkiṉṟu peritum vēṟupaṭṭum, tamiḻ tamiḻt tamiḻp perumpulavarum maruḷumāṟu toṉmutu tōṟṟattatāyum, irukkavum, cekkōcilōvōkkiya nāṭṭu koccit tamiḻp pulavarāṉa kāmil cuvelapil eṉpār, āriya muṟiyil tamiḻic cillāṇṭu kaṟṟapiṉ tammit tamiḻp pēratikāriyākak karuti, naṟṟiṇiyeṉṉum paṇṭi akapporuṭ ceyyuṭpaṉuvaliyum, nāluvēli nilam eṉṉum iṟṟik kaṭuṅkoccit tamiḻ uri naṭi nāṭakap paṉuvaliyum aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu nari taṉvāli viṭṭuk kaṭalāḻaṅ kaṇṭāṟ pōl tamiḻiṉ toṉ miyi āyntu ataṉ tōṟṟam ki. mu 1500 eṉṟu variyaṟuttiruppatu, taṉṉārāycci illāta talimit tamiḻp pērāciriyarkku ettuṇi aṟiviyal uṇmiyākak kāṭciyaḷippiṉum, muṟippaṭṭa moḻi yārāycciyāḷarkku ettuṇi nakiyāṭpiṟkuriya pakaṭik kūttām.
iṉi,
centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccoṟ kiḷavi
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvip (col - ecca) piṟaḻavuṇarntu teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, maliyāḷam mutaliya (tiraviṭam eṉṉum) koṭuntamiḻ moḻikaḷiṉ koccic coṟkaḷiyē ticic colleṉṟu, ilakkaṇa nūlār koṇṭatākak kūṟuvārumuḷar. koṭuntamiḻ moḻikaḷiṉ perumpāṟ coṟkaḷ kocci muṟiyil tirintiruppiṉum, aviyallāta, tiruntiya vaṭivuccoṟkaḷiyē tēviyāṉa iṭattuc centamiḻar mēṟ koṇṭaṉar eṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum.
``teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭār ā erumi eṉpavaṟṟip peṟṟa meṉṟum'' kuṭṭanāṭṭār tāyit taṉṉi yeṉṟum, nāyi ñeḷḷi yeṉṟum; kuṭanāṭṭār tantiyi accaṉeṉṟum; cītanāṭṭār ēṭāveṉpataṉi eluvaṉeṉṟum, tōḻiyi ikuḷiyeṉṟum, tammāmi yeṉpataṉi tantuvi yeṉṟum; pūmi nāṭṭār nāyi ñamali eṉṟum, ciṟu kuḷattip pāḻi yeṉṟum, aruva nāṭṭār ceyyic ceṟu veṉṟum, ciṟu kuḷatti kēṇi yeṉṟum; aruvā vaṭataliyār kuṟuṇiyi kuṭṭi yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkupava.''
``iṉic ciṅkaḷam antō veṉpatu; karunaṭam kariya cikka kuḷira eṉpaṉa; vaṭuku ceppeṉpatu; teluṅku eruttip pāṇpu leṉpatu; tuḷu māmaratti kok keṉpatu. oḻinta vaṟṟiṟkum vantuḻi kāṇka eṉṟē nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ticic coṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyiruttaliyum, ēṉiyuri yāciriyarum avarip pōṉṟē yaṉṟi vēṇam (vēṇṭum), lētu (ilatu) pāḷe (vāḻi) mutaliya koccit tiripukaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭāmiyum nōkkit teḷika.
mēṉāṭṭār iṟṟi aṟiviyal tuṟikaḷik kaṇṭu nuṭpamāka vaḷarttu mēṉmēlum muṉṉēṟi varupavarē yaṉṟi, toṉṟu toṭṭu varum moḻit tuṟiyil paṇṭit tamiḻar pōl tērcci peṟṟavarallar. ōrolikkup palavari vaṭivum oru varivaṭiviṟkup palavoḷiyum koṇṭatum, uyir meykkut taṉi varivaṭivillatum, taṉiyoliyi iṇivariyālum iraṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa tokuti variyālum kuṟippatum, olikkāta ūmi varikaḷuṭiyatum, ettaṉi uyarkalvi kaṟpiṉum āciriyaṉtuṇi allatu akaramutalit tuṇiyiṉṟi oru collip palukkum (uccarikkum) muṟi aṟiyaviyalātatum avar moḻiyākum. iṟṟi aṟiviyalkaḷi ellām kaṇṭataṉālum, mēṉmēlum viyakkavum maruḷavum takka putuppuṉivukaḷit toṭarntu iyaṟṟik koṇṭē varuvataṉālum, ellāt tuṟikaḷilum veḷḷiyar colvatē viḻumiya uṇmiyeṉa intiyaruṭ perumpālōr ciṟappākat tamiḻar karutukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻmoḻi amippiyum ataṉ marapiyum mēliyariṉ moḻi yamippu marapukaḷōṭu oppunōkkiṉ, moḻit tuṟiyiṟ kīḻiyarē iṉṟum mēliyar eṉpatu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum.
pētimi eṉpatu kaṟṟōr kallār ākiya iru cārārkkum potut taṉmiyākum. ātalāl paṭṭakkalvi, kaṟṟamaṭṭil oruvar tam piṟavik kuṇamāṉa pētimi nīṅkap peṟār. ākavē, kallāp pētiyar pōṉṟē kaṟṟa pētiyarum uḷar eṉa aṟika.
ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭuvari, moḻinūṟkalvi kālṭuvel pōṉṟa nuṇmatiyum naṭuniliyum ciṟanta pēraṟiñarāl naṉmuṟiyil vaḷarkkappaṭṭu vantatu. inta nūṟṟāṇṭil āriyattiyē aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu talikīḻāka āynta mēliyar, moḻi mūlattik kāṇātu muṭṭuppaṭṭu, varalāṟṟu muṟimi aṭiyōṭu vaṇṇaṉi muṟiyiyē vaḷarttu varukiṉṟaṉar. ataṉāl tammiyum tam moḻikaḷiyum pōṉṟē piṟariyum piṟar moḻikaḷiyum karuti, ilakkaṇa aṟivum, moḻippaṇpāṭum pulamiyum illāta potumakkaḷ tamvāy kōṇiyavāṟellām olittu pēcuṅ kocci vaḻakkiyē aḷavi moḻiyākak koṇṭu ataṉiyē ulaka vaḻakkeṉap pōṟṟivarukiṉṟaṉar. itu tamiḻiṟku eḷḷaḷavum ēṟkāteṉṟum ulakavaḻakku vēṟu kocci vaḻakku vēṟeṉṟum moḻittuṟiyil mēṉāṭṭār iṉṉum tamiḻariṭamiruntē aṟivupeṟaṟ
kuriyar eṉṟum, teṟṟeṉat terintu koḷka.
6
ellārāycciyum collārāycciyā?
makkaḷ aṟivaṭiyum vaḻikaḷ, kalvi, kēḷvi ārāycci tuyppu eṉa nāl vakippaṭum. oruvar piṟar eḻuti vittatit tāmāyk kaṟṟaṟivatu kalvi; aṅṅaṉamaṉṟi aṟiñariyaṭuttut tamakku vēṇṭiyavaṟṟik kēṭṭaṟivatu kēḷvi; ivviraṇṭumaṉṟi, oṉṟip putuvatākat turuvi yāyntaṟivatu ārāycci; iṉi vāḻnāḷil paṟpala vakiyil tāmē tuyttu aṟivatu tuyppu, (aṉupavam) ivaṟṟil kalvi eṉpatu ikkālattil kēḷviyiyun taḻuvum.
ārāycci ārāyap peṟum poruḷ nōkkip palatiṟappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ collārāycciyum oṉṟu. atu moḻiyārāycciyuṭpaṭṭatu. oru moḻikkuṭpaṭṭa colliṉ allatu coṟkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟi āyvatu collārāycci; oru moḻikkup piṟa moḻiyōṭu allatu moḻikaḷōṭu uḷḷa toṭarpi āyvatu moḻiyārāycci. moḻikaḷellām perum pālum cilavum palavumāyt tammuṭ toṭarpu koṇṭiruppatāl, moḻi yārāycciyillātār ceyyum collārāycci kaṭṭuppaṭuvatē.
collārāycci ceyya virumpum oruvar mutaṟkaṇ, ētēṉumoru mēli moḻiyil, ciṟappāka āṅkilattil, uḷḷa moḻi nūlkaḷiyum collārāycci nūlkaḷiyum colliyalakarātikaḷiyum kaṟṟal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam kaṟkum pōtē, moḻiyoliyiyal (phonology), col vaṭiviyal (Morphology), poruṭpāṭṭiyal (Semasiology) mutaliya moḻi nūṟṟuṟikaḷic cevvaṉ uṇarntukoḷḷutal vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ collārāycci ceyya virumpum moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇattik kaṟpatuṭaṉ, atiluḷḷa coṟ kuṭumpaṅkaḷiyellām taṉittaṉiyākavum tokuti tokutiyākavum nōkki, avaṟṟiṉ toṭarpukaḷi aṟintu koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. piṉṉar ammoḻic coṟkaḷi ammoḻik kuṭumpattic cērnta piṟa moḻic coṟkaḷōṭum akkuṭumpattiyaṭutta ayaṟ kuṭumpa moḻic coṟkaḷōṭum oppunōkkik kāṭṭal vēṇṭum. col lārāyccikkuc col varicikaḷi oppu nōkkuvatiṉum, ilakkaṇa neṟi muṟikaḷi oppu nōkkuvatē mikutiyum vēṇṭap peṟuvatākum.
moḻinūl allatu colliyalnūl, kaṇitampōlat taṟcārpuk kaliyaṉṟu. ataṟkup piṟakaliyaṟivum iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappeṟum. uḷanūl (Psychology) varalāṟṟu nūl (History), ñālanūl (Geography), māntaṉūl (Anthropology), eṉpaṉa moḻi nūṟkup peritun tuṇi ceyyum. vaṇikam paṟṟi oru moḻiyiṉiṉṟu iṉṉoru moḻikku vaḻaṅkuñ coṟkaṭkellām varalāṟṟaṟivum ñāla nūlaṟivum iṉṟiyami yātaṉa.
e-kā : tuki (tōki), teak (tēkku).
mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu karuvi nūlkaḷuṅ kalikaḷum kivarappeṟṟapiṉ, ellā moḻikaṭkum potuvāṉa colliyal neṟi muṟikaḷiyum, ārāyvāṉ eṭuttukkoṇṭa oru moḻikkēyuriya collākka neṟi muṟikaḷiyum aṟintu koḷḷal vēṇṭum.. ``ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉa vāyiṉum'' pala coṟku, ``moḻip poruṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā'' ātaliṉ avi iṭukuṟiyeṉak koḷḷutal kūṭātu. caṟṟu āḻntu nōkkiṉ avaṟṟiṉ vērpporuḷ mella mella miḷirntu tōṉṟum. cila coṟkaḷ kālakkaṭappil mikamikat tirintum iṉac coṟkaḷi iḻantum vērc col vaḻakkaṟṟum pōṉatiṉāl, avaṟṟiṉ vērpporuḷ ettuṇi āḻntu nōkkiṉum tōṉṟuvatilli. avaṟṟuḷ oru cilavaṟṟiṟku, ayaṉ moḻiyiluḷḷa iṉac coṟkaḷ vēr kāṭṭiṉuṅ kāṭṭum.
cuṭali, koṭukku mutaliya pala coṟkaḷiṉ vērp poruḷ nōkkiya maṭṭil tōṉṟum, maram poṉ mutaliya coṟkaḷiṉ vērp poruḷ āḻntu nōkkiṉālaṉṟit tōṉṟā. tamiḻ, karuvi mutaliya cila coṟkaḷiṉ vērp poruḷ āḻntu nōkkiṉum tōṉṟuvatilli. porunta pukavalāka oru coṟku poruṭ kāraṇaṅkāṭṭuvatu poruntātu. oru coṟkuk kāṭṭum vēr ellāt taṭikaṭkum viṭiyamiyumāṟu. ulakattōṭum, kalika ḷōṭum, maliyā tiruttal vēṇṭum. ampu (vaḷiyal) eṉṉum colliṉ vērp poruḷ, amphi(around) eṉṉum kirēkkac collāl aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. tamiḻukkum kirēkka moḻikkum uḷḷa toṭarpu eṉ 'Orgin of Culture' eṉṉum āṅkila nūlil viḷakkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. āṇṭuk kāṇka.
oru colliṉ poruṭ kāraṇaṅ kāṇutaṟku, mutaṟkaṇ, accol liṉ tiruntiya vaṭivik kaṇṭukoḷḷal vēṇṭum. maṇattakkāḷi eṉpataṉ tiruntiya vaṭivu maṇittakkāḷi eṉpatu. takkāḷi iṉattiṟ ciṟiyatu eṉpatu ataṉ poruḷ. maṇi eṉpatu oru kuṟumip poruḷ muṉṉoṭṭu. maṇat takkāḷi eṉṉum tavaṟṟu vaṭiviṟku, maṇamuḷḷa takkāḷi eṉṟutāṉ poruḷkūṟa muṭiyum. iṅṅaṉamē ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻaka akarātiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
oru colliṉ tiruntiya vaṭikik kaṇṭapiṉ, accollāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum poruḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpi nōkka vēṇṭum. oru poruḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpu, apporuḷ muḻumiyun taḻuviyatākavō ataṉ oru maruṅku paṟṟiyatākavō irukkalām. pariti (vaṭṭamāṉatu) eṉpatu muḻutun taḻuviyatu. vāḻi (vaḻavaḻappāṉatu) eṉpatu oru maruṅku (aṭimaram) paṟṟiyatu, vāḻiyiṉuñ ciṟappākat toṭarntu vāḻvatu mūṅkilātalāl, vāḻvatu vāḻi eṉpatu poruntātu.
pala kuṇaṅkaḷ pala poruṭkum potuvāyiruttaliṉ, ciṟappiyal peṉṟatu, perumpālum oru cār poruḷkaṭkuc ciṟappāyiruppatē.
e-kā :	vaḷḷam, vaḷḷi, vaḷi, vaḷiyam, vaḷiyal, vaṇṭu, vaṇṭi, vaṇar, vaṇakkam, vaṭṭu, vaṭṭi, vaṭṭil, vaṭṭi. iviyellām vaḷintatu allatu vaḷiyamāyiruppatu eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭaviyē.
kuṇam eṉpatu toḻiliyum taḻuvum.
e-kā : kēḻal (nilatti kiḷippatu)
oppumiyum oru kuṇamē.
e-kā : nuṇā (nuṇal pōluṅkāyiyuṭiyatu).
oru collāṉ pala karuttukaḷ eḻuppap peṟalām. avaṟṟiṉ muṉmi piṉmit toṭarpu aṟiyappaṭṭa piṉṉarē, moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam tuṇiyap peṟalvēṇṭum. cilaviṭattup pala kāraṇaṅkaḷ otta poruttamuṭiyaṉavākat tōṉṟum. avaṟṟuḷ mikap poruttamāṉati ēraṇa muṟiyilum oppu nōkkiyumē tuṇital kūṭum.
e-kā:	viḻā eṉṉuñ coṟku viḻuttal, viḻital eṉṉum iraṇṭum poruṭ kāraṇamākat tōṉṟalām. viḻuttal ciṟattal, viḻital virumputal, virumpic ceyvatu eṉṉuṅ kāraṇattiṉum ciṟappuc ceyvatu eṉṉuṅ kāraṇamē poruttamām. ``ciṟappoṭu pūcaṇi'' eṉṟu vaḷḷuvar kūṟiyiruppatālum ``ciṟappucceytal'' eṉṉum vaḻakkuṇmi yālum piṉṉataṉ mikuporuttam aṟiyappaṭum. viḻu - viḻā, vēḷviyē virumpic ceyyappaṭuvatu. vēḷviyoṭu ceyyappaṭum viḻākkaḷ, ākupeyar muṟiyil vēḷvi yeṉappeṟum.
iṉi coṟṟirivu muṟikaḷiyum muṟpaṭa aṟintukoḷḷal vēṇṭum. e.ṭu: iru kuṟil oru neṭilākat tiriyum eṉṉum tirivu muṟiyāl, akal-āl (viḻutūṉṟip paṭarum ālamaram), vaṇaṅku vāṅku mutaliyaṉavum; ṭakaram, rikaramākat tiriyum eṉṉum tirivu muṟiyāl, paṭavar-paravar, viṭicci-viricci (maṟi poruḷi veḷipṭuttalāṉa pākkattu viricci) mutaliyaṉavum, ḻakaram ṭakaramākat tiriyum eṉṉum tirivu muṟiyāl, puḻal puṭal (puḻali-puṭali), kuḻal-kuṭal mutaliyaṉavum; `il' īṟu oru kuṟumip poruṭpiṉ ṉoṭṭu eṉpatāl, toṭṭi-toṭṭil kuṭi-kuṭil mutaliyaṉa vum; am īṟu oru perumip poruṭ piṉṉoṭṭu eṉpatāl, mati-matiyam (muḻumati), nili - niliyam mutaliyaṉavum; ukaram akaramākat tiriyum tirivu muṟiyāl, kuṭumpu-kaṭumpu, nuri-nari (veṇmi) mutaliyaṉavum; ukara ūkāram muṟiyē ikara īkāramākat tiriyum tarivu muṟiyāl puraṇṭi-piraṇṭi, tūṇṭu tīṇṭu mutaliyaṉavum; viḷaṅkutal kāṇka.
centamiḻc colliyal neṟi muṟikaḷum (Principles of Etymology) coṟṟirivu muṟikaḷum (Modes of Derivation) ettuṇiyō pala. aviyum avaṟṟiṉ vakikaḷum eṉatu `collākka neṟimuṟikaḷ' eṉṉum nūliṟ kūṟappeṟum.
collārāycci ceyvār mēṟkūṟiyavāṟellām kaṟṟiruppatuṭaṉ iṟivaṉāl itaṟkeṉa aḻippum (call) peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum. allāk kāl uḻippellām viḻalukkiritta nīrāyviṭum. tamiḻc collārāyccit tuṟiyil talimiyāṉa aḻippup peṟṟavar kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇi yārāvar. 
ikkālattil cilar, moḻiyārācci ceyyātum moḻiyārāccikkum collārāyccikkum vēṟupāṭu teriyātum, colliyal neṟi muṟikaḷi yum, coṟṟirivu, muṟikaḷiyum aṟiyātum, tām talimip patavi peṟṟiruppatu kāraṇamākattarukki, ellām valla cittarum aṉittu nūluṅ kaṟṟa aṟiñarumpōl naṭittu poyyum vaḻuvum maliyac cila col lārāyccik kaṭṭurikaḷ varintiruppatuṭaṉ, kālṭuvel aiyarkāṭṭiya vaḻi niṉṟu uṇmiyāṉa ārāycci ceyta piṟari, neṟippaṭa ārāyātavar eṉṟu kuṟippākavum veḷippaṭiyākavum kūṟiyum varukiṉṟaṉar. eḷiya coṟkaṭku evarum vērccol kāṭṭuvar. ariya coṟkaṭkuk kāṭṭuvatē aritu. colliyal neṟimuṟikaḷi aṟiyātār kūṟum pōlic colliyal, piṉvarumāṟu eḻutiṟappaṭum.
(1) olimuṟic colliyal (Sound Etymology)
e-kā:	pārāḷumaṉṟu - parliament. pārāḷumaṉṟu eṉpatu pār āḷum maṉṟu eṉṉum muccoṟṟoṭar parliament eṉpatu parler (speak) eṉṉum pireñcuc collum ment eṉṉum āṅkila vikutiyum cērnta orē col
(2) uṉṉippuc colliyal (Guessing Etymology)
e-kā :	aṇil = aḻaku (aḻakiya varikaḷ) uṭiyatu.
	aṇi = aḻakukalam = kallāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu, vēntaṉ = vemmiyāy. atikārañ celuttupavaṉ. vēm = vēntaṉ.
(3) aṟivākulac colliyal (Pedantic Etymology)
e-kā :	kiḷi = kiḷippatu (pēcum paṟavi)
	maṇ = maṇappatu (nāṟṟamuṭiyatu)
(4) aṭippaṭṭa colliyal (Popular Etymology).
e-kā :	nūl (puttakam) = iḻinūlum eṟṟu
	nūlum pōlavatu.
(5) kuṟikkōṭ colliyal (Tendentious Etymology)
e-kā :	aiyaṉ ī āriyaṉ (ārya)
	accaṉ ī ajja (pirākirutam) īī ārya
	veṟukki (celvam) = veṟukkappaṭuvatu.
(6) vaḻūvup pakuppuc colliyal (Malanalytic Etymelogy) 
e-kā :	cāppāṭu - cāvataṟku ētuvāṉatu (cāppāṭu)
(7) nakiyāṭṭuc colliyal. (Playful Etymology)
e-kā :	tōci irumuṟi `ci' eṉṟu olippatu
	tō (urutu) = iraṇṭu.
iṅku kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa eṭuttukāṭṭukkaḷiyellām maṟukkavum viḷakkavum pukiṉ viriyumātaliṉ avaṟṟuḷ nāṉki maṭṭum īṇ ṭārāyccik keṭuttukkoḷval,
(1) aṇil. aṇi = vari, varici,
aṇi - aṇil = mutukil mūvarikaḷiyuṭiyatu.
(2) vēntaṉ : vēytal = mēlaṇital, muṭicūṭutal. vēy = vēyn tōṉ - vēntaṉ = muṭiyaṇiyum urimiyuḷḷa cēra, cōḻa pāṇṭiyaruḷ oruvaṉ, vēḷ, maṉṉaṉ, kō, vēntaṉ eṉṉum nālvakiyaracar peyaruḷ, vēntaṉ eṉpatu muṭiyaṇiyum urimiyuḷḷa mūvēntarkkē potuvāka vuriyatām. ivvurimi kiṟittuvukku muṟpaṭṭa kālamellām kiyāḷap peṟṟu vantatu.
koṉṟi vēntaṉ (civaṉ) eṉpatu koṉṟi vēyntōṉ eṉṟē poruḷpaṭutal kāṇka.
vēntu eṉṉum pālīṟillāp paḻavaṭivamum vēntaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ paṭuvatē; ceytu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉimuṟṟum ātal pōṉṟu.
(3) kiḷi kiḷḷi :- kiḷ - kiḷḷi - kiḷi, kiḷ - kiḷḷi, kiḷḷutal = kūṟiya mūkkāl kottutal. maraṅkaḷiluḷḷa kāykaṉikaḷik kiḷḷi vippatu kiḷiyiṉ iyalpu. paḻakāta kiḷiyi oruvaṉ piṭippāṉāyiṉ; uṭaṉē akappaṭṭa uṟuppik kāyappaṭa valitāyk kottiviṭum. `kiḷi kottiṉa paḻam' eṉpatu vaḻakku pēcum iyalpu kiḷikkilli. collik koṭuttatic collum iyalpē ataṟkuṇṭu. ``coṉṉatic collum kiḷippiḷḷi'' eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. coṉṉatic collum tiṟattil,  kiḷiyiṉuñ ciṟantatu pūviyeṉa aṟika.
(4) accaṉ :- attaṉ - accaṉ (āṇpāl) tanti.
atti - acci (peṇpāl) tāy, akki.
ivi tūya tamiḻc coṟkaḷ. ta - ca : pōli.
o.nō : pittaṉ - piccaṉ.
attaṉ, accaṉ eṉpaṉa muṟippeyar.
āriyaṉ eṉpatu oru makkaḷiṉap peyar; attuṭaṉ ōr āriyac col.
attaṉ, accaṉ eṉpaṉavum atti acci eṉpaṉavum; muṟiyē āṇpālīṟākavum eṇṇiṟanta tamiḻp peyarkaḷil toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
e-kā :	taṭṭātti, vaṇṇātti
	kiḷḷicci, vēṭṭuvacci	}	peṇpāl
aracaṉ eṉṉum collē vaṭanāṭṭil ajja eṉṉum pirākirutac collāka varukiṉṟateṉka.
(1)	palamoḻikaḷ tammuḷ toṭarpu koṇṭiruppatāl col lārāyccikku moḻiyārāycciyum vēṇṭum.
(2)	coṟkaḷ kuṭumpam kuṭumpamāy iyalvatāl, avaṟṟit tokuttu nōkkiyē mūlaṅkāṇutal vēṇṭum; eṉṉum iru neṟi muṟikaḷiyuṇarttutaṟku, oru coṟṟokutiyi īṇṭeṭuttuk kāṭṭuvām.
mēliyāriya moḻikaḷiluḷḷa tamiḻc coṟkaḷuḷ oru cila : 
kulavu -	L. Curuo, to bend, E. Curve 
kuraṅku (vaḷivu kokki) kuṟaṅku (kokki) D. King, Krink, a curl bend, crook
E. Crank to bend, wind and turn
E. Cringe A. S. Cringan, Crincan to bend with servility.
kaṟaṅku (vaḷivu, vaṭṭam,kāṟṟāṭi, cuḻal) - A. S. hring O. H. G. hing Icel, hringr, G. ring, D. King, Sw. ring, E ring.
sw. Kring, about, around; Icel, Kringer, acircle,
prov G. Krink, Kring, a ring, circle.
kuravi - Gr. choros, a dance in a ring. L. chorus E. Chorus
kuruḷ - D. Krullen, Dan Krolle, E. Crull, Curl,
kuruku - A. S. Cran, D. Kraan, G. Krahu, Kranich; Icel, Trans, Dan trane. Armor. Karan, w. Garan, Gr. Geranos, L. Grus, E. Crane.
kokki - A. S. hoc. E. hook. D. hock, Icel. haki, G. haken, O. H. G. hako, L. G. hake.
koṭukku - Icel. Crokr, sw. krok, Don Crog, O. E. Crok, E. Croog, Dan Crog, D. Kruk, W. crwg, Gael, Crocan.
O. F. Croc. ṭa.ra. pō o.nō :
kuṭaku - coorg, aṭikkāy areca
kōṇam - Gr. Gonia, an angle.
kōṭṭi - L. Castrum. E. caster.
mēṟkāṭṭiya teṉ coṟkaḷum, mēliyāriyac coṟkaḷum, collaḷavil maṭṭumaṉṟi aṭippakutiyilum oliyum poruḷum ottiruppatik kāṇalām. avaṟṟuḷ mūlam evi, tiripu evi eṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭittaṟku, accoṟkaḷellām emmoḻiyil oruṅkē vaḻimuṟit toṭarpuṟṟu oru kuṭumpamāka vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kāṇutal vēṇṭum. avi emmoḻiyil eṅṅaṉam vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟavō ammoḻiyē avaṟṟukkellām mūlam eṉpatu muṭinta muṭipākum. pala iṭaṅkaḷil oru marattiṉ iliyum pūvum kāyum kaṉiyumākiya ciṉikaḷē citaṟik kiṭakku māyiṉ, avi aṇmiyilō, cēymiyilō uḷḷa oru marattiṉiṉṟē paṟikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. itaṟku māṟāka, acciṉikaḷiliruntē ammaram vantateṉṟu valippatu uttikkum uṇmikkum poruntāta iṭumpuk kūṟṟākum. ōr ūriluḷḷa makkaḷ vēṟṟūr ceṉṟu vāḻiṉ, toṉṟu toṭṭu avvūrārē eṉattammic collikkoḷḷa muṭiyātu. aṅṅaṉam collutaṟkut toṉṟutoṭṭu varum uṟiyuḷ uṟavu, naṭpu, ayal mutaliyavaṟṟik kāṭṭutal vēṇṭum. mēṟkāṭṭiya āriyac coṟkaḷ mēli moḻikaḷil āṅkāṅkē oṉṟiraṇṭākac citaṟik kiṭappaṉavē yaṉṟi, tamiḻiṟpōl maraput toṭarpuṟṟa oru kuṭumpamāka allatu kulamāka vaḻaṅki varuvaṉavalla. āriya moḻikaḷil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷ collālum, poruḷālum teṉ coṟkaḷi ottiruppatāl, avaṟṟit taṟceyalāka nērnta oppumikaḷeṉat taḷḷiviṭavummuṭiyātu. avaṟṟiṉ uṇmit toṭarpik kāṭṭutaṟku moḻi nūloṭu varalāṟu mutaliya piṟa cāṉṟukaḷumuḷa.
mēṟkāṭṭiya coṟkaṭkellām mūlam teṉmoḻiyē eṉpatu, piṉvarum coṟṟokutiyāl aṟiyalākum.
kul eṉpatu vaḷivup poruḷ tarum oru vērccol. ataṉiṉṟu, vaḷivu kōṇal vaṭṭam vaḷiyam uruṭci tiraṭci, kuḻaṟci, cuḻaṟci, cuṟṟal cūḻal mutaliya pala uḷaviyaṟkaruttuḷḷa paṟpala coṟkaḷ toṭarntu kiḷittuḷḷaṉa.
avi varumāṟu :-
kul-kulā-kulavu-kulāvu. kulavutal - vaḷital.
kul-kur-kura-kuram-vaṭṭamāṉa kutirikkuḷampu. kural - vaḷinta tiṉikkatir. kurakam - vaḷinta kaḻuttuḷḷa nīrppaṟavi kuraṅku-vaḷivu kokki kuraṅkutal-vaḷital, kuravi-vaṭṭamāka niṉṟāṭuṅ kūttu-kuraṅku. kuṟaṅku-kokki. kuṟaṅku-kaṟaṅku, kaṟaṅkutal-cuḻalutal, kaṟaṅku-kāṟṟāṭi. kaṟaṅkal-vaḷitaṭi, kū. a, tiripu, oppunōkka, muṭaṅku. maṭaṅku, kuṭumpu-kaṭumpu, kuṟaku-vaḷinta kaḻuttuḷḷa karuvi.
kul-kuṉ-kuṉṉā. kuṉṉāttal-uṭampu kūṉippōtal. kuṉi, kuṉital-vaḷital, vaṇaṅkutal. kuṉippu-vaḷintāṭuṅkūttu. kuṉuku, kuṉukutal-kaṭuñ cirippil uṭampu vaḷital kūṉal-vaḷivu mutukuvaḷivu. kūṉi-vaḷintaciṟṟiṟāl kūṉi-kūṉuḷḷa tōṟcāl.
kul kuḷi-kuḷavi-koṭukkuḷḷa tēṉī. kuḷiki-marunturuṇṭi, vaḷitaṭi, tiraṭci-kuḻal, kuḻalutal-curuḷtal.
kul kuṇ-kuṇṭu-uruṇṭi, uruṭci, tiraṭci. kuṇṭam-uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa paṉṟi, kuṇṭaṉ-uruṇṭu tiraṇṭavaṉ, vaḷintatu. kuṇṭucaṭṭi-uruṇṭa caṭṭi. kuṇṭā-kuṇṭucaṭṭi, kuṇṭi. uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa kāḷi. kuṇṭuccaṭṭi. kuṇṭalam-vaṭṭam, cuṉṉam kuṇṭāṉa kātaṇi.
kuṇ-kuṇa-kuṇaku. kuṇakutal-vaḷital, kuṇaṅku. kuṇaṅkutal, vaḷital. kuṇakku-vaḷivu. kuṇali-uṭalvaḷivu, vaḷintāṭuṅ kūttu kuṇi-muṭamāṉatu. kaṇukku-katalaṇiyum ulōka vaḷiyam. kuṇukkutal vaḷittal.
kuḷ-kuṭṭu-muṭṭi (teluṅkuc collāṉa ticiccol) kuṭam-uruṭciyāṉatu, vaḷivu, kuṭa-vaḷinta. kuṭavu, kuṭavutal-vaḷital. kuṭakkam vaḷivu, kuṭakki-vaḷivāṉatu. kuṭakkiyaṉ-kūṉaṉ. kuṭaṅku-kuṭaṅkutal-vaḷital. kuṭantam-vaḷivu, vaṇakkam, vaḻipāṭu. kuṭanti-vaḷivu. kuṭā-vaḷivāṉatu kuṭi-(vaḷinta) puruvam.
kuḷ-kuy-kuyam-vaḷinta arivāḷ.
kuḷ-kūḷ-kūḷi-vaḷinta vāḻippaḻam.
kūḷ-kūṭu-vaṭṭamāṉa neṟ kaḷañciyam.
kaḷ-koḷ-vaḷinta kāyiluḷḷa karaṇam. koḷ-koṭpu-cuḻaṟci cuṟṟutal. koṭṭu-koṭkutal-cuṟṟutal. (koṭki)-kokki-vaḷinta māṭṭuṟuppu. (koṭku)-kokku-vaḷinta kaḻuttuḷḷa oru nīrppaṟavi vaki. kokki-kokki. koṭṭu-neṟkūṭu. koṭṭārām-kaḷañciyamuḷḷa nilam.
koṭṭi-uruṇṭi vaṭivam, uruṇṭu tiraṇṭaviti, uruṇṭit taliyaṇi.
koṭu-vaḷinta. koṭumaram-vil.
koṭukku-vaḷinta muḷḷuṟuppu. koṭukkaṉ-tēḷ.
koṭukki- tēṭkoṭukki (oruceṭi)
koṭi-vaḷinta taṇṭuḷḷa ceṭi
koṭiṟu-kuṟaṭu, kuṟaṭu pōṉṟa vāyalaku.
kul-(kūl)-(kol)-kōl-uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa kompu, kōṟṟoṭi-tiraṇṭa vaḷiyal, kōli-uruṇṭi.
koḷ-kōḷ-uruṇṭiyāṉa (allatu cuṟṟivarum) viṇmīṉ. kōḷam-uruṇṭi. kōḷā-uruṇṭiyāṉa ciṟṟuṇṭi vaki. kōḷam-kōram-vaṭṭil (vaṭṭamāṉa kalam)
kōḷ-kōṇ-kōṇutal-vaḷital cāytal, kōṇam-cāyvu, cāynta mūli. kōṇaṉ-kūṉaṉ. kōṇal-cāyvu, neḷivu. kōṇi-cāyvu, piṟaḻvu. kōṇiyaṉ-matipiṟaḻntavaṉ, kutarukkavāti. kōṇ-kōṭu-vaḷivu, kōṭutal-vaḷital. kōṭal-vaḷivu, vaḷinta itaḻuḷḷa kāntaṭpū. kōṭi-vaḷivu. kōṭi-kāntaḷ. kōṭṭam-vaḷivu, matilāṟ cūḻappaṭṭa kōyil, nilāvic cūḻnta ūrkōḷ kōṭṭam-kōṭṭakam. kōṭṭi-vaṭṭamāṉa neṟkaḷañciyam, matilāṟ cūḻappaṭṭa iṭam, nilāvic cūḻnta oḷivaṭṭam.
ikkuṭumpattic cērnta piṟa coṟkaḷumuḷa. virivañci immaṭṭil niṟuttap peṟṟatu.
tamiḻppoḻil - tuṇar 32 malar 1
pa-9-18. (1956, ēppiral, mē)
7
vaṭacol teṉcol kāṇumvaḻikaḷ
vēta āriyaruḷ viraliṭṭeṇṇattakka orucilar kimu 2000 pōṉṟa paṇṭik kālattē tamiḻakattu vantamarntatākat teriyiṉum, kaṭikkaḻakak kālattilēyē vaṭamoḻiyāl tamiḻ tākkuṇṭa niliviḷaṅkit tōṉṟiya tiṉ viḷivāka vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻip pōrāṭṭam allatu vaṭacol teṉcol vēṟupāṭṭuṇarcci toṭaṅkiyirukkiṉṟatu.
``iyaṟcol tiricol ticiccol vaṭacolleṉ
naṉittē ceyyuḷ īṭṭac collē''	(tol.)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā (eccaviyal) tolkāppiyar kālattil (ki.mu 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) vaṭacol vēṟupaṭuttiyuṇarappaṭṭamiyi uṇarttumē yaṉṟi tūya tamiḻarāl veṟuttotukkappaṭṭamiyi - uṇarttātu.
ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutivari vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa coṟka ḷellām perumpālum vaṭacollē eṉṉum tavaṟāṉa karuttu, tamiḻa ruḷḷattilum nilittiruntatu. vaṭamoḻiyilum teṉcoṟkaḷuṇmi kaṇṭu avvuṇmiyi mutaṉmutal veḷippaṭuttiyavar paṇṭārakar (Dr.) kuṇṭarṭṭu, kālṭuvel, pōppu eṉṉum mēliyaṟiñarāvar. ivaruḷ iṭiñar, taliciṟantavar. avarum, teṉ colli vaṭacolliṉiṉṟu pirittuṇarum vaḻikaḷik kūṟiṉarēyaṉṟi, vaṭacollit teṉcolli ṉiṉṟu pirittuṇarum vaḻikaḷi kūṟiṉarallar. ēṉeṉiṉ atu avaratu tiraviṭa oppiyal ilakkaṇattiṟkut tēviyāṉataṉṟu. āyiṉum, avar teṉcoṟkuk kūṟiya vitikaḷit tuṇikkoṇṭē vaṭacoṟkum cilavaṟṟi vakuttuk koḷḷalām. avi varumāṟu.
vaṭacoṟkaḷ:
1.	mūlavaṭivil āriyaveḻuttum āriya meymmayakkamum amintiruppaṉa.
2.	paṇṭit tamiḻil vaḻaṅkātu piṟkālat tamiḻil vaṭamoḻiyi ṉiṉṟu vantu vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.
3.	tamiḻil vēriṉṟu āriyattilēyē vēr koṇṭiruppaṉa.
4.	tamiḻum tamiḻiṉiṉṟu tirinta tiraviṭamumāṉa ellā moḻikaḷilum vaḻaṅkātu, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟālmaṭṭum vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.
5.	mēliyāriya moḻikaḷilum vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.
6.	āriyattiṟ ciṟappup poruḷ allatu ciṟappu viṉit toṭarpu koṇṭiruppaṉa.
cila teṉ coṟkaḷ tamiḻiṉum vaṭa moḻiyiṟ peruvāriyāy vaḻaṅku vatāl perumpāṉmi vaḻakku vaṭacoṉmikkuc cāṉṟākāteṉa vaṟika.
mēṟkūṟiya vitikaḷ allatu nilippāṭukaḷ oṉṟiṟku mēṟ poruntiyiruppiṉ, avaṟṟilēmikutikkut takkavāṟu vaṭacoṉmi tēṟṟamām āyiṉum, avi kaṇita muṟippaṭṭa tullipa vitikaḷalla. ātalāl, parantamoḻi nūṟkalviyum neṭunāṭ collārāycciyum ciṟanta naṭuniliyumilavēl, avi peritum payaṉpaṭā, uṇmiyāṉa teṉ collit teṉcolleṉṟu kūṟuvatum naṭuviṉmiyil etirikaḷ kūṟalām. āyiṉ āḻnta ārāycciyum tūyamaṉac cāṉṟum valiyuṟutti evarukkum eḷḷaḷavum añcavēṇṭuvatiṉṟu cilar aṟivārāycci valimiyiṉṟit tam paṭṭam patavic ciṟappāl oru muṭipiṟku varukiṉṟaṉar, atu mikat tavaṟām.
paṇṭit tamiḻil vaḻaṅkātu piṟkālat tamiḻil iruvaki vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅkum vaṭacoṟkaḷuḷ `pacu' eṉpatu oṉṟākum. itu vaṭacolleṉpatu veḷippaṭiyāyiṉum, oru cār tamiḻp pērāciriyar itit teṉcol leṉpatāl, māṇavarum ārāycciyillātārum mayaṅvataṟku, iṭaṉā vatuṭaṉ, avaravar virumpiyavāṟē pala vaṭa col teṉcoleṉavum pala teṉ col vaṭacoleṉavum paṭṭu, uṇmiyāṉa ārāycci valiyaṟṟup pōy viṭukiṉṟatu. pala aṭippaṭit teṉcolli vaṭa colleṉṟu kūṟum vaṭamoḻiyāḷar kūṟṟum valiyuṟṟuviṭukiṉṟatu.
pacuvik kuṟikkum tūyateṉcoṟkaḷ, ā (āṉ) kaṟavi. kuṭañcuṭṭu, kurāl, kō, curi peṟṟam (irupāṟpotu) eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ, kaṟavi eṉpatu pāl kaṟakkappaṭuvatu. curi eṉpatu maṭi poruttatu; kuṭañcuṭṭu eṉpatu orukuṭam pālkaṟappatu; kurāl eṉpatu pukar (kapila) niṟattatu. peṟṟam eṉpatu mikap paruttuyarntatu; ā, kō eṉpaṉa potuppeyar. ivaṟṟuḷ ā eṉpatu tamiḻilum tiraviṭattilum maṭṭum vaḻaṅkum; kō eṉpatu āriyattilum vaḻaṅkum.
E. cow, A, sax, cu, G. kub, D. and Dam koe, Ice, ku, Sc, kye, skr. go, gus.
kōvi mēyppavaṉ allatu vaḷarppavaṉ kōvaṉ, kōvaṉ - kōṉ - kōṉāṉ. oppunōkka ; āyaṉ - āvi vaḷarkkum iṭiyaṉ
pacu eṉṉum vaṭacol, pācattiṉāṟ kaṭṭappaṭuvatu eṉṉum vērp poruḷatu. pācam kayiṟu, āriya vēḷviyiṟ kaṭṭappaṭum uyirikaḷ, māntaṉ, āṉ, kutiri, veḷḷāṭu, cemmaṟiyāṭu eṉṉum ainteṉṟu atarvavētam (xi, 2, 9 etc) kūṟuvatāka, māṉiyar villiyam tam vaṭa moḻiyāṅkila vakarātiyiṟ kāṭṭuvar. (pak-611)
pacu eṉpatu mutaṟkālattil, potuvāka, kaṭṭappaṭṭa oru vilaṅki maṭṭum taṉippaṭavum tokutiyākavum (irupāṟ potup peyarākak) kuṟittu, piṉpu vēḷviyuyirip peyarāka vaḻaṅki, ataṉpiṉ āviṟkuc ciṟappāka vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟatu.
āriya vaḻakkit taḻuviyē, pacu eṉṉum col tamiḻilum, ceyyuḷ vaḻakkil vilaṅku, kāḷi eṉṉum poruḷkaḷil vaḻaṅkiyirukkiṉṟatu.
``pacuvēṟit tirivāṉōr pavaṉ'' (tēvā. 760, 4) eṉṉumiṭattiṟ kāḷiyiyum, ``pacukkaḷip pōlac cellum naṭiyāl'' (patiṉo. paṭṭiṉat. tiruvēkam. 32) eṉṉumiṭattil vilaṅkiyum, pacu eṉṉum col kuṟittal kāṇka. āṉ eṉṉum tūyapeṇpāṟ col, tamiḻ marapiṟku māṟāka,
``āṉmukattaṉ aṭaṟkaṇnāyakaṉ''	(kantapu. pāṉukō. 95)
eṉṉumiṭattiṟ kāḷiyik kuṟippatum āriya vaḻakkit taḻuviyē.
pacu eṉpatu āvi maṭṭuṅ kuṟippatu ulaka vaḻakkākum.
vēta āriyar tamiḻarōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭu civaneṟikkoṇ muṭipu (civacittāntam) kaṟṟa piṉṉarē, pācam, pacu, pacupati eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, muṟiyē, taḷi (malam), pulampaṉ (āṉmā). iṟi eṉṉum poruḷkaḷil aṇiyiyaṉ muṟiyil āḷappeṟṟaṉa.
pacu eṉṉum coṟku iṉamāka, ilattīṉil, Peck (Cattle) bous eṉṉum coṟkaḷum, kirēkkattil bous eṉṉum collum uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉiraṇṭiyum Gaus eṉṉum vaṭa collōṭiṇippar. mēli moḻinūlarum akarātiyāḷarum, itu attuṇip poruttamākat tōṉṟavilli. āyiṉum kakarattiṟkum pakarattiṟkum pōlit toṭarpiruppatāl, ati muṟṟum maṟuttaṟkilli.
Pecu eṉṉum iyaṟ colliṉiṉṟu tirintu paṇattik kuṟikkum Pecunia (money) eṉṉum ilattiṉ peyarc collum, Pecuniary eṉṉum āṅkilap peyareccamum, celvattik kuṟikkum māṭu eṉṉum colloṭu oppu nōkkaṟkuriyaṉa.
``kēṭil viḻuccelvam kalvi yoruvaṟku
māṭalla maṟṟi yavi''	(340)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
kō (āṉ) eṉṉum teṉcol mēliyāriya moḻikaḷilum ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatum, pecunia eṉṟum ilattīṉ col māṭu eṉṟum teṉcol lottuc celvatti yuṇarttuvatum, pērāciriyar pi.ṭi.cīnivāca aiyaṅkārum, paṇṭārakar irāmaccantira tīṭcitarum kūṟiyuḷḷavāṟu, kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻaruḷ oru kūṭṭattārē mēli nāṭu ceṉṟu āriyarāka māṟi yuḷḷamiyi uṇarttum.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, pacu eṉpatu vaṭacollēyeṉṟum, atitteṉ colleṉpatāl poyyoṭu kalanta meyyum poyyākip palatūya teṉ collum vaṭacollākat tōṉṟu meṉṟum, ātalāl ataṉāl tīmiyē yaṉṟi naṉmiyilli yeṉṟum, pacumpullittiṉpatu pacuveṉpatu caṟṟum poruntāteṉṟum, aṟintukoḷka.
iṉi, pācam eṉṉum vaṭacol, pacumi, paci, pāci eṉṉum teṉ coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṉ tiripeṉak koḷḷa iṭamiruppiṉum, intiyat tōlāṟ ceyyappaṭum pātakkūṭu (boot) intiyak kālaṇiyākāmi pōṉṟē, teṉ colliṉiṉṟu tirintu oru ciṟappup poruḷi yuṇarttum vaṭamoḻit tiricollum teṉ collākāmi kaṇṭukoḷka.
tamiḻppāvi 4 āṇṭuc ciṟappumalar
(maturi eḻuttāḷar maṉṟam)
8
pāvi eṉṉuñ col varalāṟu
tamiḻiluḷḷa iḷamippeyarkaḷuḷ pārppu eṉpatu oṉṟākum.
``māṟṟaruñ ciṟappiṉ marapiyal kiḷappiṉ
pārppum paṟaḻum kuṭṭiyum kuruḷiyum
kaṉṟum piḷḷiyum makavum maṟiyum eṉ(ṟu)
oṉpatum kuḻaviyō ṭiḷamip peyarē,''	(1)
``avaṟṟuḷ,
pārppum piḷḷiyum paṟap pavaṟṟiḷami,''	(4)
``tavaḻ pavi tāmum avaṟṟō raṉṉa,''	(5)
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiya marapiyal nūṟpākkaḷ.
paṟavik kuñcum cila ūruyirikaḷiṉ (Reptiles) iḷamiyum pārppeṉappaṭum eṉpatu ivaṟṟāl teriyavarum. cila vilaṅkukaḷiṉ kuṭṭiyum pārppeṉappaṭum eṉṟu piṅkala uriccoṟṟokuti kūṟum. itu attuṉic ciṟappuṭittaṉṟu, tāyiṉāl mikak kavaṉittup pārkkappaṭu vatiṉāl, paṟavik kuñcu pārppu eṉappaṭṭatu. pārttal - pēṇutal, pēṇivaḷarttal. pārppu eṉṉuñ col poruṭkaraṇiyam paṟṟi, Nurs(e)ling eṉṟu āṅkilac colli ottatākum. cila ūruyirikaḷiṉ iḷamikkum pārppu eṉṉum collit tolkāppiyar māṭṭeṟintā rēṉum, atu ciṟappākap paṟaviyiṉ iḷamikkē uriyateṉpati, `ciṟappuṭip poruḷi muṟpaṭakkiḷatal' eṉṉum utti paṟṟi ati mutaṟkaṇ taṉippaṭak kūṟiyatāṟ peṟavittār.
pārppu eṉṉum col poruḷ viripu muṟiyil makkaṭkuḻavi yiyum kuḻavi pōṉṟa pommiyiyum kuṟitta pōtu rakaraṅ keṭṭup pāppu, pāppā eṉat tirintatu.
o.nō: kōr - kō, kōrvi - kōvi.
pāppā =	1. paṟavik kuñcu
	2. ūruyiriyiḷami
``cilampikkut taṉciṉi kūṟṟam nīḷ kōṭu
vilaṅkiṟkuk kūṟṟam mayirtāṉ - valampaṭā
māviṟkuk kūṟṟamām ñeṇṭiṟkut taṉ pārppu
nāviṟku naṉṟal vaci''	(ciṟupañcamūlam. 9)
E. Pupa = chrysalis.
3.	makkaṭ kuḻavi.
	E. beb, baby.
4.	ciṟumi
	L. pup=Girl, pupa = girl.
5.	vilaṅkiṉ kuṭṭi,
	E. pup puppy = Young dog.
6.	pommi.
	L. Pupa= doll. of. Poupee = doll, playtning, toy; 
	F. Poupette = doll, dim of pupa (girl).
	E. Puppet = Small figure representing human being
	E. Poppet = Small person.
iṭṭu eṉpatu, tamiḻil oru ciṟumip poruḷ muṉṉoṭṭu, iṭṭiṭi=ciṟṟiṭi.
iṭṭēṟi = ciṟu vaṇṭippāti. iṭṭu - of. ette, E. et, pāppā eṉṉuñ col nāḷaṭiviṟ pāvi eṉṟu tirintatu. 
te. pāppa, ka. pāppe. ma.pāva
pāvi = 1. paṭimi, pommi
``marappāvi nāṇā luyir maruṭṭi yaṟṟu''	(kuṟaḷ. 1020)
polampā likikaḷum pāvi viḷakkum	(maṇi 1:45) 
2. aḻakiya uruvam.
``cittirap pāviyiṉ attaka vaṭaṅki''	(naṉ. 40)
3. karuviḻiyiṟ pāvipōl teriyum uruvam.
``karumaṇiyiṟ pāvāy nī pōtāy''	(kuṟaḷ. 1125)
4. karuviḻi,
L. pupillus, of. pupille, E. pupil. dim. of L. pupa.
5. pāvi pōṟ pūkku ṅ kuravampū.
``kuravam payanta ceyyāp pāvi''	(aiṅkuṟu, 344)
6. pāvi pōṉṟa iñcik kiḻaṅku.
``ceyyāp pāvi vaḷarntu kaviṉ muṟṟi'' (malipaṭu. 125)
``pāvi yiñciyuṅ kaṭavic cuṇṇamum'' (peruṅ, uñcik. 53:21
7. pāvi pōl aḻakiya peṇ.
``pāṭaka mellaṭip pāvi'' (tēvā. 538:1.)
8. ciṟumi.
9. pāvi naṭam.
``tiruviṉ ceyyō ḷāṭiya pāviyum'' (cilap. 6:61).
10. pāvi nōṉpu.
``nam pāvikkuc ceyyuṅ kiricikaḷ'' (tiṅ. tiruppā.2)
11. tiruvem pāvi.
12. tirup pāvi.
``tolpāvi pāṭiyaruḷa valla palvaḷiyāy'' (tiṅtiruppā. taṉi)
13. tōṟ pāvi.
pāvi eṉṉum nūl ulaka vaḻakkiṟ pāvāy eṉṟuntirintu peṇṇiyaṟ peyarākum.
itu kāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl pāvi eṉṉuñ colliṉ ciṟappiyum, atu mēliyāriya moḻikaḷiṉuñ ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkum parappiyum kaṇṭukoḷka.

9
tiru eṉṉum col teṉcollā, vaṭacollā?
tamiḻil taṟpavamāka, atāvatu vaṭacol tiripāka allatu citivāka oru cārārāl karutappaṭum coṟkaḷil tiru eṉpatum oṉṟu.
``nilantarutiruviṟ pāṇṭiyaṉ aviyattu'' 
eṉṟu tolkāppiyac ciṟappuppāyirattilum.
``tiruvuṭit tirumaṉiṉayatu tōṉṟu kamaḻpuki''	(379)
``viṭṭōri viṭā aḷ tiruvē''	(358)
``tiruḷi lallatu tolivil laṟiyār''	(20)
eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟilum.
``tiruc ceṟṟut tīyuḻi yurittu viṭum''	(168)
``cērun tiṟaṉaṟin tāṅkē tiru''	(179)
``tiru nutal nallavar nāṇuppiṟa''	(1011)
``naṉṟaṟi vāriṟ kayavar tiruvuṭiyar''	(1072)
eṉṟu tirukkuṟaḷilum.
``irunilaṅ kaṭanta tirumaṟu mārpiṉ''	(20)
eṉṟu perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭiyilum
tirunutal (2:41), tiruttakkīr (11:111), tiruñemira kalam (28:157), tirumoḻi (10:12), tirumakaḷ (5:213), tirumāl (17) eṉṟu cilappatikārattilum, iṅṅaṉamē piṟa nūlkaḷilum tiru eṉṉum col palvēṟu poruḷkaḷil vaḻaṅkappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.
ulaka vaḻakkil, tirukkaliyāṇam tirukkārttiki tirukkuṟaḷ tirukkūttu tirunāḷ tirunīṟu tiruppaṉi tiruppati tiruppatikam tiruppāṟ kaṭal tiruppukaḻ tirumakaḷ tirumāṉ tirumaṅkalam, tirumaṅkaliyam tirumaṇam tirumali tirumāl tirumukam tirumuṭi tirumuṟi tirumuṉ tirumēṉi tiruvaṭi tiruvaraṅkam tiruvaruḷ tiruvaḷḷuvar tiruvākku tiruvāciki tiruvāṭutaṇṭu tiruvāyamalartal tiruviḻā tiruviḷakku tiruviḷiyāṭal tiruvuḷam tiruvēṅkaṭam tiruvōṭu mutaliya nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷ toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa.
teyvap peyar aṭiyār peyar teyvattiṉ allatu, teyvat toṭarpuḷḷa ciṉippeyar āṭiyaṉip peyar uḷavup peyar viṉip peyar iṭappeyar nūṟpeyar mutaliyaṉa, tam tūymiyik kuṟittaṟkut tiru eṉṉum aṭipeṟuvatu toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakkākum.
e-kā :-

aracar teyvat taṉmiyuḷḷavarākak karutappaṭṭatiṉāl, avar toṭarpuḷḷa coṟkaḷum tiru eṉṉum aṭipeṟṟaṉa.
e-kā : tiruvāyk kēḷvi, tirumantiravōli, tirumāṭam, tirumāṭam, tirumukakkaṇakku, tirumukakkāṇam, tiruvāṇi, tirumūppu, tiruvāṇ ṭeḻuttiṭutal, tirunalliyāṇṭu, tirukkic ciṟappu. 
aṭiyārkaḷ teyvattaṉmiyuḷḷavarātaliṉ, avarkaḷoṭu toṭarpuṟṟavaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷum tiru eṉṟa aṭipeṟum.
e-kā : tirukkuki, tirukkūṭṭam, tirukkōvi, tirumaṭam, tiruttoṇṭar, tirunāḷ, (tirunaṭcattiram), tiruvēṭam.
tirumakaṉ, tirumakaḷ mutaliya peyarkaḷil tiru eṉṉum aṭimoḻi pirikka muṭiyātavāṟu piṇintuḷḷatu.
tiru eṉṉuñ col mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu aṭimoḻiyāy maṭṭumaṉṟi, taṉic collākavum vantu, muṟiyē, celvam, ciṟappu aḻaku oḷi ceḻippu pēṟu (pākkiyam) teyvattaṉmi tūymi naṉmi nalviṉi kaṇiyaṉ (cōtiṭaṉ) maṅkalam maṅkiliyam oruvakit taliyaṇi mārpil tōṉṟum vīṟṟut teyvam mutaliya poruḷkaḷit tarum.
ivaṟṟuḷ, celvam eṉṉum poruḷē mutaltōṉṟiyatum piṟavaṟṟukkellām aṭippaṭiyumākum.
``poruḷallā tārip poruḷākac ceyyum
poruḷ tilli poruḷ''
``illāḷi illāṉuma vēṇṭāḷmaṟ ṟīṉṟeṭutta
cellā tavaṉvāyiṟ col.'' 
(tāy vēṇṭāḷ eṉpavaṟṟāl kiṟappum)
`aṉṉamoṭuṅkiṉāl aintu moṭuṅkum' eṉṟum eḷiya iṉattārik kārokkal (kariya cuṟṟattār) eṉṟum,
``tarittiram mikka vaṉappiṉi yoṭukkic carīratti yularttā vāṭṭum'' eṉṟum kūṟuvatāl aḻakum oḷiyum;
``tiruvuṭi maṉṉarikkāṇil tirumālikkaṇṭēṉē yeṉṉum'' eṉpatāl tūymiyum celvattāluṇṭātal kāṇka. iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum.
tiru eṉṉum col, mutalāvatu poruṭ peyarāyp piṉpu paṇpup peyarāyum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. iccolliṉṟu tirumi (kanta. mutaṉāṭ.35) tiruvam (cilap.12) mutaliya paṇpup peyarkaḷum, tiruvaṉ (tiv. iyaṟ. 2, 84)  tiruvāḷaṉ (tiv. periyati. 5, 5, 1) tiruvāṭṭi (tirukkō. 294 uri) mutaliya uyartiṇip peyarkaḷum tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
tiruvaṉ 0 1. celvaṉ, perumakaṉ
tiruvāḷaṉ 0=2. tirumāl
tiruvāṭṭi = 1. celvi, perumakaḷ; 2. tirumakaḷ; 3. tēvi.
tiruvaṉ eṉṉum peyar peṇpālil tiruvi eṉṟākum.
tiru eṉṉum `colliṉ vēr'
uyireḻuttukaḷil īkāram allatu ikaram aṉmic cuṭṭum kīḻmic cuṭṭum piṉmic cuṭṭumākum.
aṇmic cuṭṭi, ivaṉ itu iṅku ivaṉ mutaliya coṟkaḷālum; kīḻmic cuṭṭi, ikam iku iḻi iḷi iṟaṅku mutaliya coṟkaḷālum. piṉmiccuṭṭi iṭi iṭaṟu iṇuṅku iḻu iri iṟi mutaliya coṟkaḷālum aṟiyalām. (itaṉ virivi eṉatu cuṭṭuviḷakkam eṉṉum cuvaṭiyiṟ kaṇṭu koḷka.)
iri eṉṉuñcollukku, olittal cīṟutal mūccuvāṅkutal vīṅkutal mutaliya viṉipporuḷkaḷum oli aḵṟiṇiyuyirikaḷiṉ uṇavu nākappūcci mutaliya peyarp poruḷkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ, mūccuvāṅkutal vīṅkutal uṇavu nākappūcci eṉṉum poruḷkaḷ, iḻuttal eṉṉuṅkarutti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭaṉa.
mūccuḻuttalukkum, mūcciḻukkum īḷi (kāca) nōykkum irippu eṉṟum iḻuppuḷḷa mānta nōykku irippumāntam eṉṟum, iḻuppi yuṇṭākkum elikku irippeli eṉṟum peyar.
ikarac cuṭṭaṭippiṟappiṉālum rakaraḻakarap pōliyālum iḻuppup poruḷālum iri eṉṉuñcoṟku iṉamāṉatākum. iri eṉṉuñcol mutalil takarameypeṟṟut tiri eṉṟākum. tirittal iḻuttal. vēṭṭiyi mēl iḻuttuk kaṭṭutalit tirittuk kaṭṭutal eṉpatu teṉṉāṭṭu vaḻakku. āṭiyi iḻuttiḻuttu vittāṟpōṉṟa alikkum, tōṟcuruṅkaṟkum, orupuṟamākavum kīḻākavum iḻukkappaṭukiṉṟa paṭutāvukkum, tiri yeṉṉum peyariruttali nōkkuka.
ïiu>âiu, oppu nōkkuka: v©>nr‹.
āṭiyāvatu nīrāvatu tiriyum pōtu, tirintaviṭattil tiraṭci yuṇṭāvatāl, tiritalukkut tiruñatal eṉṉum poruḷum uṇṭāyiṟṟu. pāl tirañatalit tiritaleṉṟu kūṟuvati nōkkuka. tiraḷ eṉṉuñ collum tiri eṉpatiṉiṉṟu tirintatē. aliyāṉatu ōriṭattilillāmal iṭiviṭātu alintu koṇṭiruppatāl, alital viṉi aliyik kuṟikkum tiri eṉṉum colliṉ tiripāṉa tiri eṉṉuñ collāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu.
tiru eṉṉuñcol mēṟkūṟiya tiri tiraḷ tiri eṉṉum mūṉṟu coṟkum collālum poruḷālum iṉamākum. tiri pōl ōriṭatti lillāmal eṉṟum alintukoṇṭiruppatu eṉṉum poruḷilāvatu, makkaḷāl tiraṭṭappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruḷilāvatu, celvattiṟkut tiru eṉṉum peyar tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
``āṟiṭu mēṭu maṭuvumpō lāñcelvam''
``akaṭuṟayārmāṭṭu nillātu celvañ
cakaṭakkāl pōlvarum''
eṉṟu kūṟiyataṉālum, celvam eṉṉum peyarukkuc celvatu eṉpatu poruḷātalālum, celvam niliyaṟṟatu eṉṟum, ākkappaṭuvatāl ākkam eṉṟum tēṭappaṭuvatāl tēṭṭu eṉṟum ceṟintiruppatāl veṟukki yeṉṟum peyar peṟṟiruppatiṉāl celvam tiraṭṭappaṭuvatu eṉṟum, iru karuttukaḷ nam muṉṉōrkkut tōṉṟiyiruntami pulaṉām. ivviraṇṭiloṉṟu tiru eṉṉuñ col tōṉṟuvataṟkuk kāraṇamā yiruntirukkalām.
tiri eṉṉuñ collē celvattik kuṟiyāmal, ataṉ tiripāṉa tiru eṉṉuñ col kuṟippatēṉ eṉiṉ, colliṉ poruḷ māṟumpōtu collum māṟavēṇṭum eṉṉum collākkap potuviti paṟṟi yeṉka. allākkāl pala poruḷkaḷ uṭaṉmayaṅkik karuttuṇarvu taṭippaṭu meṉka.
tiri eṉpatiliruntu tiru eṉpatu tōṉṟiyiruttal tiruppāṟ kaṭaliliruntu tirumakaṉ tōṉṟiṉāṉ eṉṉuṅ katiyi niliyuṟuttum. tiri = kaṭal. tiri = tirumakaḷ.
``tirikaṭa lōṭiyun tiraviyan tēṭu''
``nīrpōyum - oṉṟiraṇṭām vāṇikam il''
eṉpavaṟṟālum, paṭṭiṉappāli cilappatikāram maṇimēkali mutaliya vaṟṟālum, paṇṭikkālattilum nīr vāṇikattālēyē perum poru ḷīṭṭappaṭṭami aṟiyappaṭum. kaṭal pōy vanta celvatti kaṭal tanta teṉṟu kūṟutal poruttamāṉatē. mēlum celvattiṉ ciṟanta vakiyāṉa muttē perumpālum kaṭalilirunteṭukkappaṭuvatu itaṉālum celvam kaṭalil piṟantatākak kūṟalām. celvattiṉ uruvakamē allatu āṭpaṭiyē (personification) tirumakaḷātaliṉ, avaḷ tirumāliṉ iruppiṭamākak kūṟappaṭum tiruppāṟkaṭaliṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiṉatākak kūṟappaṭṭāḷ eṉka. (vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa tiraviyam eṉṉum col tiravam eṉpatiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiyatu pōlum!)
ākavē, tiru eṉṉuñ col, taṉimoḻiyākavum aṭimoḻi yākavum mutalāvatu celvapporuḷilum piṉpu ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa pala poruḷkaḷilum toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarum taṉit tamiḻc collātal peṟappaṭum. ilattīṉ kirēkku jermāṉiyam mutaliya mēliyāriya moḻikaḷil iccollillāmiyāl, itu vaṭacollaṉṟu eṉpatum veḷiyām. āriyar nāvalantēyattiṟku (intiyāviṟku) vantapiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kalantupōṉa nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa tirāviṭa allatu teṉcoṟkaḷil tiru eṉpatum oṉṟākum.
tiru eṉṉum teṉcollē vaṭamoḻiyil srī eṉṟāki, piṉpu cī eṉat tamiḻiṟ citintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟateṉka. ippōtu tamiḻnāṭṭil kaḷḷikkōṭṭiyeṉa mēlkarit tuṟinakarākiya kōḻikkōṭṭiliruntu iṅkilāntiṟku ēṟṟumatiyāṉa tuṇi āṅkilattil kalikkō (Calico) eṉṟum piṉpu ataṉ vaḻiyāyt tamiḻil 
kalikkā eṉṟum vaṅkāḷattil kāḷikkōṭṭam eṉṉum nakarp peyar āṅkilattil kalkuṟṟā eṉṟum piṉpu ataṉ vaḻiyāyt tamiḻil kalkattā eṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvati nōkkuka. kōḻikkōṭu kāḷikkōṭṭam eṉṉumiraṇṭum taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷākum. fŸ>fhs«>fhË. kaḷ=karuppu. kōṭiya matil kōṭṭam. kōṭutal kōṇutal allatu vaḷital.

kāḷikkōṭṭam > Calcutta > kalkattā.
tiru eṉpatu srī eṉṟu tiriyavē, tirumāṉ eṉpati srīmāṉ cīmāṉ eṉṟum tirumāṭṭi eṉpati srīmāṭṭi cīmāṭṭi eṉṟum collavum eḻutavum talippaṭṭaṉar. tirumāṉ eṉpatu tirumakaṉ eṉpataṉ marūu.

St. (Saint) eṉpataṟkuc camamāyt tūymi kuṟippatāyiruppatāl, aṭiyārallāta piṟa makkaḷik kuṟikkumpōtu tiruvāḷar tiruvāṭṭiyār eṉṟa aṭikaḷiyē muṟiyē āṇpāṟkum peṇpāṟkum vaḻaṅkuvatu takkatākum. tiruvāḷaṉmār tiruvāṭṭimār eṉpaṉa palarpāl aṭikaḷ.
