10
`uttaram' `takkaṇam' emmoḻic coṟkaḷ?
(namatu pāvāṇar avarkaḷ taṉittamiḻ iyakkak kāvalar. tamiḻukkut taṉit tamiḻil akaramutaliyi (ṭikcaṉari) tokuttaḷikkum poṟuppi namatu aracu ivariṭam tāṉ oppaṭittuḷḷatu. ivaraṉṟi appaṇikku aṟiñar yāruḷar? maṉṟam 1968 il ipperumakaṉāriṉ paṭippukkaḷi veḷikkoṇara urupā 7500 koṇṭa tokiyi māṇpumiku kaliñar karuṇāniti avarkaḷikkoṇṭu aḷittup perumi koṇṭatu)
tamiḻiṉ colvaḷatti aṟiyātār, vaṭamoḻi kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷa āyirakkaṇakkāṉa teṉ coṟkaḷiyum vaṭacoṟkaḷeṉṟē mayaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. attakiya coṟkaḷuḷ, uttaram, takkaṇam eṉpaṉa iraṇṭām.
tiraviṭaṉiṉ muṉṉōṉāṉa tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamākiya kumarināṭṭil, mutaṟkaṇ tōṉṟiya nāṟṟicip peyarkaḷ, teṉ, vaṭam, kīḻ, mēl eṉpaṉa. avi 4ām vēṟṟumikkuriya kuvvuru pēṟṟut teṟku, vaṭakku, kiḻakku (kiḻkku) mēṟku eṉṟāki, piṉṉar āṭci paṟṟi mutal vēṟṟumiyākavē vaḻakkūṉṟiviṭṭaṉa.
avaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉiraṇṭum nilittiṇic ciṟappum, piṉṉiraṇṭum nilamaṭṭamum paṟṟiyaṉa.
teṉ eṉpatu teṉṉi. teṉṉutal kōṇutal, perumpālun teṉṉik koṇṭiruppatāl teṉṉi appeyar peṟṟatu. teṉ-teṉṉi, teṉ-teṉku-teṟku.
teṉṉi iyaṟkiyākat tōṉṟiyatu kumarikkaṇṭam. ikkālattil teṉṉiyiṉ pala vakikaḷ intō maliyāviṉ iyaṟkiyāka vaḷarvatāl annilap pakutiyē teṉṉiyiṉ piṟantakamāyirukkalāmeṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ appakutiyum kumarik kaṇṭattic cērntatē yeṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum. paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiṟkum kumariyāṟṟiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa eḻu nūṟṟukkāvata nilapparappilirunta palvēṟu nāṭukaḷik kuṟikkumiṭattu, aṭiyārkku nallār ēḻteṅka nāṭṭiyē muṟpaṭak kuṟittiruttalāl, annāṭukaḷ kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ teṉkōṭiyaṭut tiruntami peṟappaṭum. teṉkōṭiyil teṉṉi ciṟappāka vaḷarntata ṉālēyē, teṉṟici appeyar peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum.
iṅṅaṉamē nāvalantēyattiṉ vaṭakōṭiyilum vaṭamaram (ālamaram) ciṟantu vaḷarntataṉāl. vaṭatici appeyar peṟṟatu. vaṭanāvalap pakutiyāṉa vaṅkattil atu ciṟappāka vaḷarvataṉālēyē, mēlip payir nūlārum ataṟku ``vaṅkāḷa atti'' (Ficushem-gllensis) eṉa ilattīṉap peyariṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.
ālamaram cūḻa viḻutuviṭṭu vaṭṭamāka akaṉṟu vaḷarvatāl, vaṭam eṉappeyar peṟṟatu. val-vaḷ-vaṭṭu-vaṭṭam-vaṭam.
ō.nō. paṭṭam-paṭam=tuṇi. māṉiyar vilyamcu camaṟkiruta-āṅkila akara mutaliyum, vaṭa (vata) eṉṉum ālamarap peyarukku vaṭṭam eṉpatiyē poruṭ karaṇiyamākak kūṟukiṉṟatu.
vaṭṭam - va vaṭa (vaṭṭa)
vaṭamoḻiyeṉṉum camaṟkirutattil, vaṭṭam eṉpatu varutta eṉṟēyirukkummeṉpatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
vaṭṭam (tamiḻ) - vaṭṭa (pirākirutam) - vrutta (camaṟkirutam)
nila maṭṭam iṟṟit tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ pōṉṟē kumarik kaṇṭattilum, mēṟkil uyarntum kiḻakkil tāḻntum iruntataṉāl, avviru ticikaḷum muṟiyē mēl kīḻ eṉappeyar peṟṟaṉa.
ulakil uyarnta paṉimali, oru kālattil kaṭaṟkariyāy iruntatu. aṉṟu vaṭatici kaṭalāl tāḻntum, teṉtici kumarimaliyāl uyarntum, iruntaṉa. piṉṉar paṉimaliyeḻunta piṉ iruticiyum camamāyiṉa. ataṉ piṉ kumarimali mūḻkiviṭavē, vaṭaticiyuyarntu teṉtici tāḻntum pōyiṉa. ātalāl, vaṭatici uttaram eṉṉum peyarum teṉticikkut takkāṇam eṉṉum peyarum tōṉṟiṉa. avaṟṟoṭu ēṉiyiru ticikkum, kīḻ mēl eṉṉum paḻiya karaṇiyam paṟṟiyē kuṇam kuṭam eṉṉum iru peyarkaḷum uṭaṉ tōṉṟiṉa.
kuḷ-kuḻi. kuḷ-kuṇṭu=kuḻi, paḷḷam kuḷ-kuṇ-kuṇam=paḷḷam kuṇṭuṅkuḻiyumāyirukkiṟatu eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
kuṭu-kuṭumi=ucci, māṭavucci, taliyucci uccikkoṇṭi, uccimuṭi, makuṭam, maliyucci, koṭumuṭi. kuṭu-kuṭam =uyarcci, mēṭu, kuṭanāṭu=uyarnta mali nāṭāṉa cēranāṭu, mēlnāṭu. 
kiḻakku mēṟku eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiyotta, kuṇam kuṭam eṉpaṉavum kuṇakku kuṭakku eṉak kuvvurupēṟṟaṉa.
tamiḻ kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya ulaka mutaṉmoḻi yātalāl, cēymi aṇmi muṉmi ākiya mūviṭattiyuñ cuṭṭum ā (a) ī (i) ū (u) eṉṉum muccuṭṭum mutaṉ mutal māntaṉ vāyil tōṉṟiya cuṭṭolikaḷāka maṭṭumaṉṟi uyirolikaḷākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉālēyē, avi tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkil mutalil vikkappaṭṭiruppatuṭaṉ, iṉṟum āriyamoḻi kaḷiṟpōl maṟiyātum tiriyātum iyalpākavē tamiḻil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. cuṭṭuvakiyilum ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa mūviṭap peyar vaki yilum, tamiḻ (tiraviṭam) āriyattiṟku (camaṟkirutattiṟku) muntiya teṉṟum, ulaka mutaṉ moḻiyoṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiya teṉṟum, kālṭuvelār tam oppiyalilakkaṇattil āṅkāṅku mikaveṭutturittuc cellutal kāṇka.
muṉṉilip peyar: ūṉ - uṉ (orumi). ūm-um (paṉmi)
uvaṉ uvaḷ uvar utu uvi uṅku eṉpaṉa muṉṉilip poruḷ kaḷiyum iṭattiyumē kuṟittalāl, ukara allatu ūkāraccuṭṭu muṉmic cuṭṭē. muṉmi cēymikkum aṇmikkum iṭippaṭṭa tātalāl, iṭimic cuṭṭeṉpatu oru maruṅku poruntum. āyiṉ, cēymikku varampilliyātalāl, muṉmi iṭimiyākalām. iṭimi eṉṟum muṉmiyākātu.
muṉmi aṇmikkuc caṟṟuc cēymiyātalāl muṉmiccuṭṭu, tamiḻiṟciṟupāṉmi ciṟu cēymiyiyum vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷiṟ cēymiyiyum uṇarttum.
e-kā :	uṅku = nī (nīṅkaḷ) irukkumiṭattu taṉayarik kaṇṭirō  vuṅkaṇeṉṉa'' (cētupu 6:39)
utōḷ - utar (inti) = aṅku. muṉmik karuttiṉiṉṟu muṟcelavuk karuttuttōṉṟum.
e-kā :	uy = muṉcel, muṟceluttu, untu = muṉtaḷ. uki = muṟceluttu.
nilamaṭṭattiṟkum nīr maṭṭattiṟkum mēṟpaṭṭa muṟcelavu pakkavāṭṭi lum mēḷōkkiyumirukkumātalāl muṉmiccuṭṭu uyarccik karuttiyun taḻuvum. e-ṭu: ukkam = kaṭṭit tūkkuṅkayiṟu ukappu = uyarvu. ukaḷtal = kutittal. ukaḷittal = kutittal. uḷakattal = eḻutal. uccam = uyarnili. ucci=uyariṭam.
uttaram = (1) mukaṭṭuk kuṟukkup perumaram allatu iruppuviṭṭam. (2) mēṟmaṭaṅkal = kūṟṟuvaṉ, kūṟṟuvaṉ pōl aḻikkun tī paṭṭatu. uttaramantiri = talimiyamiccaṉ.
upputal = parutteḻutal
umpal = uyarntayāṉi
umpar = mēl, mēlulakam, tēvar
umpaṉ = uyarntōṉ
uyartal = mēleḻutal, mēṟpaṭutal
uvaṇ = mēliṭam uvaṇam = uyarap paṟakkum kaluḻaṉ. uvaṇi = tēvarulakam.
uṟi = uyarak kaṭṭit tūkkuṅkayiṟu allatu toṭari.
uṉṉutal = uyarakkutittal
ūrtal = ēṟic cellutal
ūṅku = uyarvu, mēṉmi 
uttaram = 1. paṉimaliyāl uyarnta ticiyāṉa vaṭakku (tivā)
Uttara, mfn (compar fr. 1. ud; opposed to adbara. upper, higher, superior (e.g. ultare dantas, the upper  teeth), RV; AV, TS: CHUP; Ragh, and c.; northern (becuse the northern part of India is high), AV; Mn; Susr.; pancat & c.'' eṉṟu māṉiyar villiyamcu camaṟkiruta - āṅkila akara mutali kūṟutal kāṇka.
2. vaṭakkil tōṉṟum ūḻittī (piṅ)
uttara maṭaṅkal = vaṭavaṉal eṉṉum ūḻittī (tivā).
kumarināṭṭuk kālattil tamiḻar (neytal nilapparavar) kaṟṟuk kaṭalōṭikaḷāyiruntataṉāl, vaṭamuṉiyil avvappōtu tōṉṟum oru palvaṇṇavoḷiyik kaṇṭu, ataṟku allatu vaṭavaṉal eṉṟu peyariṭṭaṉar; atiyē ūḻiyiṟutiyil ulakiyaḻikkum tīyākavum karutiṉar.
``vaṭavā vaṉalu maṭanaṭip piṭiyum
arumip peyaruṅ kutiriyum vaṭali''(piṅ. 988)
``vaṭavik kaṉalip piḻinteṭuttu'' (antakak. taṉip) 
``veḷḷat tiṭivāḻ vaṭavaṉali'' (kamparā. tilamā. 86)
``kaṭukiya vaṭavaṉalit tiṉivittatu'' 
aṉal - aṉalam (kaliṅ-402)
``akkaṭaliṉ mītuvaṭa vaṉalniṟkavilliyō'' (tāyu, paripūr .9) 
mēliyar vaṭavaṉali, Aurora (down) Borealies (northern) eṉṟu āṅkilattilum, vaḻaṅkuvatu muṟṟum poruḷottiruttal kāṇka.
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar vaṭavi eṉṉum teṉcolli, vaṭavā, vaṭavā mukam, vaṭavāmukākkiṉi, paṭapā, paṭapāmukam, paṭapā mukākkiṉi eṉṟu tirittum virittum, peṭṭikkutiri mukattil tōṉṟiya neruppeṉṟu, poruntāp poyttalākap poruḷ kūṟuvar. atit tamiḻar nampiya piṉṉarē ``vaṭavik kaṉaṉmāvum'' (takkayākap 694) eṉṟu oṭṭakkūttarum pāṭanērntatu. itaṉāl, tamiḻccol tirikkappaṭṭatoṭu tamiḻar matiyum tirikkappaṭṭami kāṇka.
j¡F=jhœî bj., ka. takku (taggu) avarkkut takkut toṇṭi, avar takkilē pāṭukiṟār. eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
takku - takkaṇam = nilamaṭṭattil tāḻnta teṉṟici, teṉṉāṭu (decan)
takkaṇam - va. taṣiṇam
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar, taṣiṇam eṉṉum collit teṟku eṉṉum poruḷil, taṣiṇākkiṉi, takṣiṇāyaṉam, takṣiṇācalam (potiyam), takṣiṇa pāñcālam, takṣiṇāmūrtti eṉa āḷuvar āyiṉ, takṣiṇam eṉṉum colliṟkut takṣ eṉpati mūlamākak koṇṭu ataṟkuttiṟami yāyirattal allatu valamiyōṭiruttal eṉṟu poruḷkūṟi, kiḻakku nōkkumpōtu teṉṟici valappuṟamum vaṭatici iṭappuṟamum iruppatāl takṣiṇam eṉṉum coṟkuvalam eṉṟum uttaram eṉṉum coṟku iṭam eṉṟum poruḷ tōṉṟiṉaveṉṟu karaṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvar.
takṣiṇam pari = valam vā, valañcuṟṟu.
pratakṣiṇa = valam varuki, valar cuṟṟuki.
takutiyeṉṉuñ coṟku vallamiyeṉṟum poruḷiruppatāl takṣ eṉṉum vaṭacol taku eṉṉunteṉ colliṉ tiripākavumirukkalām.
o. nō. L. dexter = of or on the right-hand side. 
E. dexterous = deft, adroit, clever, using right hand by preference
E; ME. deft = dexterous skilful.
E. daft. ME. daffte = OE ged & fte (Goth gadaban to be fit) orig. Sense, fitting, suitable.
takṣ eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkum, (dextes) eṉṉum ilattīṉac coṟkum, mūlam etuvāyiṉum, uttaram, takkaṇam eṉṉum iru ticiccollum teṉcollēyeṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷāvaṉa:-
1. uttaram takkaṇam eṉpaṉavaṟṟoṭu cērntu vaḻaṅkappeṟum kuṇam kuṭam eṉṉum ticippeyarkaḷ, tūya teṉcol. avi vaṭamoḻiyil illi. avaṟṟōṭu cērnta ēṉiyiraṇṭum teṉcollākavēyiruttal vēṇṭum.
2. uyarntōṉ tāḻntōṉ eṉpaṉa pōla, uttaram takkaṇam eṉṉum iraṇṭum oṉṟiṟkoṉṟu etirāṉa uṟaviyaṟ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ (Relative terms).
camaṟkirutattil, uttaram eṉpataṟku nilavuyarcciyiyum takṣiṇam eṉpataṟku valappuṟattiyum, karaṇiyamākak kāṭṭuvatu poruntātu.
3. uttaram eṉṉum colliṟku aṭiyāṉa ukarac cuṭṭum, takkaṇam eṉṉum colliṟku mūlamāṉa takku eṉṉum mutaṉiliyum tūya tamiḻ.
4. kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya tamiḻ camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiyatu.
5. uttaram takkaṇam eṉṉum nāṭṭuppeyarkaḷ allatu ticippeyarkaḷ, tamiḻarum avar vaḻiyiṉarāṉa tiraviṭarum toṉṟutoṭṭu vāḻum. nāvalan tēyattilaṉṟi, vēṟevvāriya nāṭṭilum eṉṟēṉum vaḻaṅkiyatilli.
kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭiṉṟik katiravaṉ vaṇakkattiyē ciṟappākakkoṇṭa vētāriyar, intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntapiṉṉarē, kālik katirava vaṇakkattiṉ pōtu takkaṇam valappuṟamum uttaram iṭappuṟamum iruttal kaṇṭu, avviruticip peyarkaṭkum muṟiyē valam iṭam eṉap poruḷ koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
``kiḻakku'' mutaliya muṉṟōṉṟiya nāṟṟicip peyarum ulaka vaḻakkil āḻa ūṉṟiviṭṭataṉāl, `kuṇakku' (kuṇam) mutaliya piṟṟōṉṟiya nāṟṟicip peyarum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkeṉṉum ilakkiya vaḻakkilēyē iṭampeṟalāyiṉa.
ulaka vaḻakkiliṉmiyālum poyyuṭi vaṭavar col vaṉmi yiṉālumē, uttaram takkaṇam eṉṉum iru teṉ coṟkaḷum vaṭacol leṉum mayakkattiṟku iṭamāyiṉaveṉa, ikkaṭṭuriyiṉiṉṟum teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka.
mutaltaram, kaṭittaram, mēltaram, kīḻttaram, naṭuttaram, iṭittaram, ciṟutaram, peruntaram, eṉpaṉa pōṉṟatē uttaram (uyarnta nili) eṉṉum vaḻakkum puṉka vaṭamoḻiyāḷar ivvaṭivi uṟaḻtaram (comparative degree) ākkik koṇṭaṉar. ataṉāl ut+tara (led + tara) eṉṟu pakuppar. atu kaṇṭu mayaṅkaṟka.
muraṭṭuk kuṟukkup perumaram uttaram eṉṟu peyar peṟṟirut taliyum nōkkuka.
11
`mati' viḷakkam
colliyal (Etymology) moḻi nūluḷ (Philology) aṭaṅkumēṉum, pala coṟkaḷ pala moḻikaṭkup potuvāyiruttalāṉum, oru moḻic col vevvēṟu vakiyil piṟa moḻic ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkutalāṉum, moḻiyārāycci ceyyātār collārāycciyil iṟaṅkutal takātu. moḻiyārāyccikku moḻi nūlilakkiyak kalvi maṭṭumaṉṟi palvēṟu moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa aṟivum coṟṟokutiyaṟivum vēṇṭum.
poruṭpāṭu nōkkic coṟkaḷi,
(1) oru poruṭ pala col (Synonyms)
(2) pala poruḷoru col (Homonyms)
eṉa iru vakiyākap pakuttaṉar paṇṭit tamiḻ ilakkaṇiyar. ivaṟṟi, muṟiyē, oru poruṭcol, pala poruṭ col eṉak kuṟukkiyum vaḻaṅka lām. oru poruṭ col eṉpatu orēyoru poruḷuḷḷa col eṉṟum poruḷ paṭiṉum oru poruṭ palacol eṉpatu orumikku ēṟkāmi yāṉum, iraṭṭuṟaṟ coṟkaḷ pala iṭam nōkkip poruḷ vēṟupā ṭuṇarttalāṉum, oru poruṭcol eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭṭiṟku iraṭṭuṟaṉmi iḻukkākāteṉka.
iṉi, pala poruṭ collum,
(1) oru pērp pala poruṭcol (Homonymy) 
(2) pala pērp pala poruṭcol (Polysemy)
eṉa iru tiṟappaṭum. kaṭu eṉṉum col, mikuti, vaṉmi, vali, virivu, kāram, koṭumi mutaliya pala poruḷi orē pērp poruḷaṭiyil uṇarttutalāl oru vērp pala poruṭ collum; māṇṭāṉ eṉṉum col, māṇ, māḷ eṉṉum iru vēṟu viṉip pakutikaḷiṉ paṭarkki āṇpāl iṟantakāla muṟṟātaliṉ pala vērp pala poruṭ collum; ākum.
oru poruṭ collum iṅṅaṉamē oru vēruṭimiyum pala vēruṭimiyumpaṟṟi iru tiṟappaṭum. āyiṉ, atu ikkaṭṭurikkup payaṉpaṭāmiyiṉ viḷakkappaṭṭilatu.
mati eṉṉum col, paruttal, kaṭital, koḻuttal, mataṅkoḷḷutal, mayakkam, kaḷ, yāṉi, nilā, iṉimi, karuvāppaṭṭi, aḷavu, matippu, aṟivu, aciccol mutaliya poruḷkaḷil oru vēli pala poruṭ collāṉa teṉ collum; peṇpāl aṭi moḻiyīṟākavariṉ, `māṉ' eṉṉum tam īṟṟiṉiṉṟu tirinta vaṭa collum ākum. ivviraṇṭiṟkum evvaki toṭarpumilli. ivaṟṟiṉ varalāṟu varumāṟu:-
muttu-mattu matti-mati. u (aḷḷit tiripu),
muttutal=cērtal. (cīva 504), mukattoṭu mukam cērtal.
pala poruḷkaḷ allatu kūṟukaḷ oṉṟu cēriṉ tiraṭciyuṇṭākumata lāl, cērtaṟ karuttil tiraṭci karuttut tōṉṟum.
``cērē tiraṭci'' (tol. col 363).
muttu-mutti=tiraṭci (cūṭā cōṟṟuruṇṭi).
mutti - motti=paruma uruṇṭi.
motti - mōtti=parutta veḷḷāṭṭukkaṭā.
muttu - mottu - mottam tiraṭci, muḻumi.
muttu=tiraṇṭa viti, mala ḷiñcilil viḷiyuṅ kal, koppaḷam.
muttu-muttam=periya muttu, iccol vaṭamoḻiyil muktayeṉṟu tiriyum. `am' perumip poruḷ īṟu.
muttu-mattu=tiraṇṭa tayir mutaliyaṉa kaṭi karuvi. mattu-mattam āymakaḷ mattam piṇitta kayiṟu'' (kalit. 110)
mattu-matti. mattittal=kaṭiyil.
matti-mati. matittal=kaṭital, viralāl allatu kiyāl nacukkip pacipōlākkutal.
mati-maci. macital=pacipōlātal (ta. vi.) macittal = paci pōlākku tal (pi.vi.)
mattu, mattam eṉpaṉa manta (Mantha) eṉṟum; matti, mati eṉpaṉa mat (Math) eṉṟum, maci eṉpatu maṣ eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
mattam = erumikkaṭā. iccol vaṭamoḻiyil matta eṉat tiriyum. potuvāka, ōr iyaṅkutiṇi uyiriyiṉ uṭampu paruttalāl koḻuttalum mataṅkoḷḷutalum uṇṭākum.
matittal=	1. paruttal, upputal.
	2. koḻuttal, cerukkutal.
	'' matittetir tevvā'' (iraku. mīṭci. 37).
3. mataṅkoḷḷutal.
'' matitta kaḷiṟṟiṉiṉ'' (kamparā. pañcakē. 56)
matayāṉi. ``kōṇ matittiṭar kiṭartaṉa'', (kamparā. nākapāca 136)
mati- matam = mikuti, perumi, valimi, vaḷam, cerukku, matanīr.
matam - mataṉ = mikuti, valimi, cerukku.
mattam = matam. mattamā = yāṉi. mattam - matam eṉṟumām.
mattaṉ = koḻuttavaṉ. ``mattaṉirāvaṇaṉ kotittāṉ'' (irāmanā. uyut. 44).
mattam, mattaṉ eṉpaṉa matta eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
matam-matar. matarttal=mikutal, ceḻittal, koḻuttal, mataṅ koḷḷutal, cerukkutal.
pala poruḷkaḷ allatu vaḻikaḷ allatu karuttukkaḷ kalattalāl, kalakkamum mayakkamum uṇṭākum.
mattittal=kalattal, maruntu kalattal.
mattam = mayakkam. mattam-matam-mataṉ=kalakkam.
o.nō:	kula-kulavu-kalavu, kalavutal = kalattal. kulavutal=kūṭutal. kula-kala-kalaṅku-kalakku-kalakkam. muya-muyaṅku-muyakkam. muyaṅkutal=taḻuvutal, kalattal. muya-maya-mayaṅku-mayakku-mayakkam=kūṭṭam, kalakkam. mayattal=mayaṅkutal. ``mayantuḷē ṉulaka vāḻkkiyi'' (aruṭpā, apayattiṟaṉ. 14).
mayakkak karuttiṉiṉṟu, uṇarviṉmi (mūrcci), veṟi, kōṭṭi (pittiyam), kāmam ākiyakaruttukkaḷ tōṉṟum. 
mattam=mayakkam, kuṭi veṟi, kōṭṭi, ūmatti.
mattam - mattaṉ = matimayaṅkiyavaṉ, kōṭṭipiṭittavaṉ. mattam-matam=kuṭiveṟi, kōṭṭi.
mattu=ūmatti. matattal=mayaṅkutal.
mattu, mattam, mattaṉ eṉṉum immuccollum vaṭa moḻiyil matta eṉat tiriyum; mata eṉpatu Mada eṉat tiriyum.
cerukku, yāṉi matam ākiya iru karuttum mayakkak karuttiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākavuṅ koḷḷalām. ō-nō: kaḷi-kaḷiṟu. kaḷittal=kaḷḷuṇṭu veṟittal, mayaṅkutal.
nila voḷiyāl kōṭṭiyuṇṭākumeṉṟu paṇṭik kālattil kīḻnāṭṭilum mēl nāṭṭilum oru karuttiruntatāl, tiṅkaḷukku mayakkan taruvatu eṉṉum poruḷpaṟṟi oru peyaruṇṭāyiṟṟu.
mattu-(matti)-mati: allatu matam-mati=tiṅkaḷ.
Luna (Latin) = tiṅkaḷ, Lunacy (Eng.) = kōṭṭi.
mati=mātam (matiyāl aḷakkappaṭum kāla aḷavu). 
mati-mātam-māsa (va).
matip peyariṉiṉṟē mātap peyar uṇṭākum. tiṅkaḷ=mati, mātam. (Moon-month). mati eṉṉum coṟku vaṭamoḻiyil tiṅkaṭ poruḷ illi. iyalpākat takaram cakaramākat tiriyumēyaṉṟic cakaram takaramākat tiriyātu. ātalāl, mātam eṉpatu teṉ collātal teḷivu. āyiṉum, āriya mayakkāl tamiḻar pētuṟuvatu avar pētimiyē. ēṟkeṉavē tiṅkaṭ col matiyiyum oru kiḻamiyiyum kuṟittalāl, ati mēlumoru poruḷil vaḻaṅki mayakkutal vaḻuvākum. mayakkan tarum kaḷ allatu tēṉ iṉittalāl, mayakkak karuttil matuk karuttum tōṉṟum.
mattu-maṭṭu=tēṉ, kaḷ.
mattu-matu-matur-maturi-maturam.
matu-matam=tēṉ. ``mataṅkamaḻ kōti'' (cīvaka. 2584).
matu=tēṉ, kaḷ, iṉimi, atimaturam.
matu-mati=atimaturam.
maṭṭu eṉpatu Madya eṉṟum, matu eṉpatu Madhu eṉṟum vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
iṉi, muttu eṉpataṉ valvaṭivāṉa muṭṭu eṉṉum col, oru poruḷ iṉṉoru poruḷi muṭṭutalāl aṟiyappaṭum iyakka muṭivi allatu vaḻi elliyi uṇarttutalāl, cērtal allatu poruntutaṟ karuttil elli allatu aḷavukkaruttum tōṉṟum. mukattāṟ poruntutal muttutalum taliyāṟ poruntutal muṭṭutalum ātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṉ, meṉmi vaṉmi kuṟikka, muṟiyē takara ṭakaram vantaṉaveṉa aṟika. iṅṅaṉamē muṭṭiyāl mutukil aṟital kuttutalum maṇṭiyil aṟital kuṭṭutalum ātal kāṇka. āyiṉum, mutaṟkālattil takarac collē ṭakaraccoṟ poruḷiyuṅ kuṟittatu. 
muṭṭutal=muṭital.
muṭṭu-maṭṭu=muṭivu, elli, aḷavu maṭṭu-maṭṭam.
(mattu)-(matti)-mati.
matittal=aḷaviṭutal, uḷḷattāl aḷaviṭutal, uyarvākak karututal, karuttākak koḷḷutal. 
matippu = ēṟattāḻak kaṇippu (kaṇicam), uyarvu.
mati=matippu, aḷaviṭappaṭṭa paṇṭam (ēṟṟumati, iṟakkumati), aḷantaṟiyum pakuttaṟivu.
aṟivuppulaṉ. mati - matam = koḷki, camayam, nūṟkoḷki. mati eṉṉum col muṉṉili paṭarkki yaciniliyāy varuṅkāl, aḷavu eṉṉuṅ karuttil `atu pōtum' eṉṟēṉum, aṟivu eṉṉum karuttil `atu aṟivākum' eṉṟēṉum poruḷpaṭum. paḻañcēra nāṭākiya kēraḷattil, mati eṉpatu `pōtum' eṉṉum poruḷil iṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
matam (camayam) eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyil Mata eṉa vaḻaṅkum. aḷavīṭu eṉṉum karuttoḻittu, itukāṟuṅ kūṟiya ellāp poruḷkaḷilum, mati eṉpataṉoṭu toṭarpuṭiya mata, matam, mataṉ, matar mutaliya coṟkaḷ - tamiḻil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. muḻukip pōṉa kumarikkaṇṭattoṭu pala ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, iṟantupaṭṭa paṇṭiyilakkiyattoṭu pala ceyyuḷ vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, maṟintu pōṉamiyāl, pala coṟ kōvikaḷiṉ iṭiyilirunta aṇṭukaḷi iṉṟu kāṭṭa muṭiyavilli. āyiṉum, tamiḻ kumari nāṭṭil tōṉṟi vaṭakkē ceṉṟu muṉṉart tiraviṭamākavum piṉṉar āriyamākavum māṟiṟṟeṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi āyirakkaṇakkāṉa teṉ coṟkaḷik kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷateṉṟum, aṟiya vallārkku nāṉ kūṟiya yāvum teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkutal tēṟṟam.
moḻiyākavum īṟākavum varuṅkāmāṉ eṉṟu maruvum. 
e-kā : marumakaṉ marumāṉ, perumakaṉ - perumāṉ iṅṅaṉamē tirumakaṉ eṉpatu māṉ eṉṟu tiriyum.
tiru eṉpati vaṭamoḻiyar srī eṉṟu tirittukkoṇṭatāl tirumāṉ eṉpatu srīmāṉ eṉa tirintatu itaṉic cīmāṉ ``eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collākkiṉar tamiḻar. tiruvaraṅkam-srīraṅkam-cīraṅkam eṉṉum tirivi nōkkuka.
cīmāṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl `cīmāṭṭi'yātalāl, srīmāṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl srīmāṭṭi'yum tirumāṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl `tirumāṭṭi'yum ātal vēṇṭum. tiruvāṭṭi eṉpatu tiruvāḷar eṉpataṉ peṇpāl. marumakaḷ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl marumakaḷ eṉṟum perumāṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl perumāṭṭi eṉṟum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. ``kali marumāṭṭi'' (tiruviḷi, tirumaṉa 62). ``perumāṭṭi'' (tivā) perumāḷ-pirāṉ perumāṭṭi-pirāṭṭi ivi marūuc citivu.
`vāṉ' eṉṉum īṟṟiṉil `vat', `vatī' eṉṟu muṟiyē āṇ pāṟkum peṇpāṟkum tirittuk koṇṭatupōṉṟē, `māṉ' eṉṉum īṟṟiṉiṉṟum `mat', `matī' eṉṟu tirittukkoṇṭaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. ātalāl, peṇpāl peyarkkut tirumati eṉṟu aṭi koṭukkātu tirumāṭṭi eṉṟu koṭuttal vēṇṭum. srī srīmatī eṉpaṉa vaṭa col vaṭivukaḷ. srīmatī eṉpatu eḻutte puṇarnta collākumpōtu ti eṉṟākum. ivvaṭivi vilaki vēṇṭum.
12
`uvami' teṉcollē
āṅkilak kalviyum aṟiviyal ārāycciyum nam nāṭṭil pukuntu oṉṟari nūṟṟāṇṭum. tamiḻ taṉi vaḷamoḻiyeṉak kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇi yār nāṭṭi oru nūṟṟāṇṭum ākiyum iṉṟum tamiḻiṉ taṉittaṉmiyip paṟṟip pala pērāciriyarum aiyuṟṟu varuvatāl ovvoru tamiḻc colliyum, vērum ākkamum kāṭṭi iṉṉiṉṉatu teṉ collē yeṉa nāṭṭa vēṇṭum nilimi nērntuḷḷatu.
kaṇṇutaṟ peruṅ kaṭavuḷuṅ
kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇuṟat terintāyntip
pacuntamiḻ ēṉi
maṇṇiṭic cila ilakkaṇa
varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇiṭip paṭak kiṭantatā
eṇṇavum paṭumō!
eṉṟu parañcōti muṉivar pāṭumāṟu tamiḻilakkaṇam, taṉip paṭṭatāyum piṟa moḻikaṭkillāta poruḷilak kaṇattit taṉṉakattuk koṇṭu talimi vāyntatāyum ki. mu. pattāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē kumari nāṭṭil tōṉṟi vaḷarnta toṉmiyuḷḷatāyum, iruntum ataṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷil oṉṟākavum poruṭpaṭala iyaṟpeyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟākavum uḷḷa ``uvami'' eṉṉum col vaṭa collā teṉ collā eṉṉum aiyuṟaviṟkum vaṭacollē eṉṉum valippiṟkum collārāycciyum moḻiyārāycciyum mikka ikkālattu iṭantantu niṟpatu mika mika iraṅkat takka ceytiyākum.
uvami yeṉṉuñ col oppumi yeṉṉuñ poruḷatu. ip poruḷ ataṉ muḻuvaṭivil maṭṭumiṉṟi ataṉ aṭivērāṉa ukaratti lēyē karukkoṇṭuḷḷatu. ukaramākiya ōreḻut torumoḻi oppumik karuttuk koṇṭiruppati uttiyeṉṉuñ collāl uṇaralām uttal poruntutal utti poruttam matikkum aṟiviṟkum poruttamāṉa ceyti utti eṉappaṭum. avvuttiyi aṟiyum akakkaraṇattiyum uttiyeṉpatu ākupeyar. nūliṟkum urikkum payaṉpaṭum uttikaḷāṉa neṟimuṟikaḷ muppattiraṇṭākat tokukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivvuttiyi yukti eṉat tirittāḷvar vaṭanūlār.
iru kaṭciyar viḷiyāṭum ellā viḷiyāṭṭukaḷilum kaṭci yamittaṟku ivviruvarāyp porunti varuvatu utti kaṭṭutal eṉpar. kaṭṭutal eṉṉum col vaḻakkē uttiyeṉṉum coṟkup porutta poruḷuṇmiyi uṇarttap pōtiya cāṉṟākum. kaṭṭutal poruttutal, kaḷḷutal, poruntutal kaḷḷa eṉpatu porunta eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum ōr uvama urupu ``kaḷḷak kaṭuppa āṅkavi eṇucu'' eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟutal kāṇka. (1232) `kaḷ' eṉṉum pakutiyiṉiṉṟu piṟantatē kaḷam (kūṭṭam, avi) eṉṉum tokutip peyarum kaṭci (kaḷ + ci) eṉṉum peyarum ippakutiyatē.
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻaka akarāti viḷiyāṭṭu - ttic collit teluṅkuc colleṉṟu piṟaḻak koṇṭatōṭu, nūluttic colliṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭa teṉavuṅ kūṟiyuḷḷatu. utti yeṉpataṉoṭu toṭarpuṟṟa untu eṉṉum col poruntutaṟ poruḷpaṭuvatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
``tāṉan tōṟum untiṭuṅkaraṇam'' eṉpatu civañāṉa cittiyār. (4, 34)
tamiḻil ēṟattāḻa mukkāṟ pakuti ukaravaṭivac coṟkaḷ. ukaravaṭi collākkattil
(1) muṉṉit tiripu
(2) aḷḷit tiripu
(3) piṉṉit tiripu
eṉa mūvakit tiripaṭiyum ukaram ūkāra okara ōkāraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāvatu muṉṉit tiripu; ukaram akaramāvatu aḷḷit tiripu; ukaram ikaramāvatu piṉṉit tiripu; muṉṉit tiripu eṉṟum mōṉiyākavē yirukkum.
e-kā :
puḻi	-	pūḻi
kuṭṭu	-	koṭṭu	}	muṉṉit tiripu
muṭaṅku	-	maṭaṅku		aḷḷit tiripu
puraḷ	-	piṟaḻ	}	piṉṉit tiripu
ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉiraṇṭum mōṉiyākavum toṭarum aṉṟu, aḷḷi, mōṉi, piṉṉi mōṉi eṉappaṭum.
e-kā :	kuḷiyam	-	kūḷi, koḷ
	kōḷ	-	muṉṉi mōṉi
	pacumi	-	pāci
	pimmi	-	aḷḷi mōṉi
	civaṉ ceyyāṉ
	cēyōṉ	-	piṉṉi mōṉi
kuḷiyam - uruṇṭi, kuḻiyam - vaḷitaṭi, kūḷi - vaḷintatāyuḷḷa vāḻi vaki, koḷ - vaḷinta kāyuḷḷa kāṇam, koṭkutal - vaḷital, cuṟṟutal cuḻalutal, kōḷ - vaṭṭam - katiravaṉic cuṟṟum kirakam, kōḷ, kōṇ - kōṭu, kuḷ - kūḷ.
ukaraccol mutalāvatu muṉmik karuttiyum, piṉpu ataṉ vaḻiyākap piṉmi uyarcci oppumi ākiya karuttukaḷiyum koṇṭuḷḷatu.
oppumik karuttil ukarac col u - o - ō eṉa mōṉit tiripaṭi yum. ottal pōtal, ōviyam oppumi paṟṟiya varivukkali ō + tal = ottal. ō + iyam = ōviyam okaravaṭiva pala meykaḷoṭuñ cērntu pala oppumi allatu poruntaṟ karuttuc coṟkaḷi uṇṭupaṇṇum. okkal, occi, oṭṭu, oṇṭu, oṇṇu, ottu, oppu, ompu, ollu, ovvu, oṟṟu, oṉṟu, oṉṉu ākiya coṟkaḷi nōkkuka.
moḻi vaḷarcciyil, coṟkaḷ palkutaṟ poruṭṭu aṟuvakic ceyyuḷ tiripukaḷum mūvakip puṇarcci vēṟupāṭukaḷum mukkuṟikaḷum, muccērkkikaḷum muṉṉōrāl kiyāḷap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. muccērkki yāvaṉa. mutaṟ cērkki iṭic cērkki, kaṭic cērkki eṉpaṉa.
oppumik karuttuḷḷa ukarac col kaṭic cērkkiyāka lakaramey peṟṟup palvēṟu tiripaṭintu paṟpala coṟkaḷip piṟappikkum.
ul - uḷ uṭu - uṭaṉ
uṭu - oṭu - ōṭu
ōṭutal - ottal, poruntutal kūṭutal. ōṭa eṉpatu ōr uvama urupu.
``ōṭappuriya eṉṟavi eṉua'' eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟutal kāṇka. (1232)
ōṭāvi = oviyaṉ. ōṭu - ōṭṭam, āṭṭam = oppu.
kuraṅkāṭṭam ōṭukiṟāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
ōṭṭi - uṭaṉ, otta.
ul - ur - uṟu - uṟaḻ
ur uri - uriñ - uriñcu
ur - ura - uri - urāy.
`ul' aṭiyiṉ aḷḷit tiripu
ul - al. allutal - poruntutal, piṉṉutal - muṭital.
al - aḷ - aḷi
aḷ - aṇ aṇavu, aṇi
aḷ - aṭṭu. accu (otta poruḷamikkuṅ karuvi)
aṇ - aṇṭu
attu, am mutaliya coṟkaḷum `aḷ' aṭiyiṉiṉṟu amin taviyē, attutal - oṭṭutal, iṇittal. am - amar. amartal - ottal, poruntutal.
`uḷ' aṭiyaṉ piṉṉit tiripu
uḷ - iḷ. iṇ - iṇar, iṇaṅku, iṇa.
iḷ - (iy) iyi - ici
iḷ - iḻi
iy - ey - ēy.
ḷakara meyyīṟu pala coṟkaḷil yakaramey yīṟākat tiriyum.
e-kā :	toḷ - toy - poḷ - poy - vaḷ - vay. vi - kūrmi iḻital - urāytal, uḷam poruntutal, oṉṟip paḻakutal eytal - ottal ``tēṉey mārpakam'' (cīvaka 2382) ukarac col, muṉmik karuttilum uyarccik karuttilum vakarameyc cērkki peṟuvatu pōṉṟē, oppumik karuttilum peṟum
uv, uvakkāṇ, uvi - muṉmiccuṭṭu.
uvvi (tali) uva, uvaṇ, uvaṇi - uyarccik karuttu
(uv) - uva (uvar) - ivar - oppumik karuttu. ivartal - ottal.
uvami eṉṉuñ col oppumik karuttuḷḷa `uva' eṉṉum mutaṉiliyiṉiṉṟu piṟantatākum uvattal eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyarkku virumputal, piriyamātal, makiḻtal eṉṉum poruḷkaḷum uvaki eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyarkku aṉpu, kāmam, iṉpac cuvi (ciruṅkācaracam) makiḻcci eṉṉum poruḷkaḷum, uvappu eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyarkku viruppam, makiḻcci, polivu, eṉṉum poruḷkaḷum. palkalikkaḻaka akarātiyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. maṉam ottirā viṭṭāl, viruppamum aṉpum kāmamum iṉpamum makiḻcciyum eṅṅaṉam nikaḻum? ivaṟṟiṟ kellām aṭippaṭi uḷam ottal aṉṟō!
`uvaki' uvappu mutaliya coṟkaḷ pōṉṟē uvami eṉpatum oru toḻiṟ peyarākum. `uva' pakuti `mi' vikuti. oppu nōkka : iḷa + mi - iḷami poṟu + mi - poṟumi. iḷattal = meṉmiyātal.
oruviṉip pakuti pala vikutikaḷiyēṟṟup palatoḻiṟ peyarkaḷi ākkutal iyalpātalāl. `uva' eṉṉum pakuti (`tal', `ki', `pu' u, mi, mam, māṉam'' eṉṉum pala vikutikaḷi yēṟṟu muṟiyē uvattal, uvaki, uvappu. uvavu, uvami, uvamam, uvamāṉam, eṉṉum palatoḻiṟ peyarkaḷit tōṟṟuvikkum. ivaṟṟuḷ mutal nāṉkum, uvamip poruḷ iḻantuviṭṭaṉa, allatu avaṟṟi apporuḷil āḷum ilakkiyap pakutikaḷ iṟantu paṭṭaṉa.
`mam' oru toḻiṟ peyarīṟu eṉpatu, urumam, parumam, mutaliya coṟkaḷāl uṇaralām. uruttal veppamātal, urumam veppamāṉa naṇpakal.
``mam'' īṟu ``mi'' yīṟṟu tiripeṉak koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu.
o. nō : ceyyāmi - ceyyāmal.
ceyyāmi pōṉāṉ, ceyyāmē pōṉāṉ, ceyyāmaṟ pōṉāṉ, eṉṉum mut toṭarum oru poruḷaṉavātal kāṇka.
``maṉṉutiru vaṇṇā
maliccam pan tāṇ piṟkup
paṉṉu talic cavaram
paṇṇu vatēṉ - miṉṉiṉ
iḷatta viṭi mātar
ivaṉ kuṭumi paṟṟi
vaḷit tiḻuttuk kuṭṭā
malukku''
eṉṉum kāḷamēkar pāṭalil, kuṭṭāmal eṉpatu kuṭṭāmalukku eṉṟu urupēṟṟu varuvatāl `ceyyāmal' eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu etirmaṟi viṉiyecca vaṭivam, viṉi eccamāy āḷappaṭum. ceyyāmi eṉṉum etirmaṟit toḻiṟ peyariṉ tiripāyirukkalām eṉṟu uyttuṇarap peṟum.
aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷiṉ makaraviṟuti ṉakaraviṟutiyāvatu perum pāṉmiyātalāl uvamam eṉṉum peyar uvamaṉ eṉṟu tiriyum.
o. nō :	parumam	-	parumaṉ
	taṭumam	-	taṭumaṉ	}	toḻiṟ peyar
	tiṟam	-	tiṟaṉ
	kaṭam	-	kaṭaṉ	}	piṟa peyar
viḻumam eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyar viḻu + mam eṉṟum, viḻumu + am eṉṟum pirittaṟku iṭantarum. āyiṉ urumam, parumam eṉpaṉa iṭantarā, viḻuttal - ciṟattal, viḻumutal - ciṟattal.
māṉam eṉṉum col toḻiṟ peyarīṟāy varuvati, aṭimāṉam, kaṭṭumāṉam, tīrmāṉam, paṭimāṉam, piṭimāṉam, peṟumāṉam, varumāṉam mutaliya toḻiṟ peyarkaḷiṉṟu aṟika.
uvami eṉṉum peyariṉṟu uvamittal eṉṉum viṉi piṟakkum. orumi eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṟu orumi - ttal eṉṉum viṉi piṟantiruttal kāṇka. uvamittal oṉṟaṉi oṉṟiṟku oppākak kūṟutal.
iṅṅaṉamē, uvamāṉam eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṟu uvamāṉi - ttal eṉṉum viṉi piṟakkum. tīrmāṉam eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṟu tīrmāṉi - ttal eṉṉum viṉi piṟantiruttal kāṇka. tīrmāṉam muṭipu. tīrmāṉittal muṭipu ceytal uvamāṉittal uvamittal.
uvappu  uṉṉum col muṟkūṟiyavāṟu uyarccip poruḷiyun tarum.
uva - uvar. ivar - ivartal = uyartal, ēṟutal
uva - uvappu. uyaram, uva = uka - ukappu = uyarvu
uva - uvaṇ - mēliṭam. uvaṇ - uvaṇi - tēvarulakam.
``uyarntataṉ mēṟṟē uḷḷuṅkāli''
eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatāl (1224) uyarnta poruḷi oppākak koḷvatē uvamiyiṉ aṭippaṭi neṟimuṟi eṉṟu koṇṭu uvamic colliṟku uyarvupporuḷ. koḷvatu poruntātu. tolkāppiyam uyarntatu eṉṟu kuṟittatu `ciṟantatu' eṉṉum poruḷatu. ciṟappu uyarvu ciṟappu. iḻivu, ciṟappu eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. uyarnta poruḷukku mikavuyarnta poruḷiyum iḻitē poruḷukku mika iḻinta poruḷiyum uvamaṅkūṟa vēṇṭum eṉpatē tolkāppiyar karuttu.
e-kā :
``ūraṅ kaṇanīr
uravunīr cērntakkāl
pērum piṟitākit
tīrttamām''
kaṅkiyāṟṟilum cākkaṭi kalantu kaṅki nīrām eṉṉum eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvamiyil mēlōrkkuk kaṅkiyum kīḻōrkkuc cākkaṭiyum uvamamātal kāṇka. vēṟṟumi kuṟikkum colvēṟṟumi eṉṟē peyar peṟṟāṟ pōl oppumi paṟṟiya aṇiyum oppumi kuṟikkum collā lēyē aḻikkap peṟutalē muṟimiyām.
vaṭamoḻiyil ``upamā'' upamāṉa eṉṉum iruvaṭivukaḷē uḷa. ivi muṟiyē, uvami, uvamāṉam, eṉpavaṟṟiṉ tiripukaḷ, uvamam, uvamaṉ, uvami, uvamāṉi eṉṉum vaṭivukaḷum coṟkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyililli. aṟiyuṅkaruvi allatu aḷavip peyar `āṉa' eṉṟum kūṟiṉ, ataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum poruḷiṉ peyar `ēya' eṉṟu iṟuvatu vaṭamoḻi marapu.
e-kā:	ñāṉa(m) - ñēya(m)
	aṉumāṉa(m) - aṉumēya(m)
	piramāṇa(m) - piramēya(m)
immuṟippaṭi upamāṉattiṉāl aṟiyappaṭuvatu upamēya(m) eṉṟāyiṟṟu. itu piṟkāla vaḷarcci māṉam eṉpatu taṉi niliyil aḷavu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum tūya teṉ col. paṭiyi iṉṟum māṉam eṉpar vaṭārkkāṭṭār.
āriyar nāvāmpoḻiṟku varumuṉṉarē. tamiḻilakkaṇam teṉ maturiyil muḻuvaḷarcciyaṭintu viṭṭateṉṟum tolkāppiyam ki. mu. 7ām allatu 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappaṭṭatēṉum atil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa ilakkaṇam (vaṭacol nīṅkalākat) talikkaḻakak kālattatu eṉṟum atil āḷappeṟṟuḷḷa kuṟiyīṭukaḷ ellām teṉcollē eṉṟum vaṭamoḻi teṉ moḻiyiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷa coṟkaḷ āyirak kaṇakkiṉavātalāl, avi vaṭa moḻiyiluḷḷa aḷavāṉē avaṟṟi āriyam eṉa mayaṅkal kūṭāteṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum. cilar ōraḷavu vaṭamoḻikaṟṟa aḷavāṉē tammi vaṭamoḻi vallāreṉṟu paṟi cāṟṟik koṇṭu eḷḷaḷavum ārāycciyiṉṟi vaṭamoḻiyil uḷḷa coṟkaḷellām vaṭacol eṉa tām mayaṅkuvatōṭamiyātu māṇavari yum āciriyariyum atikārikaḷiyum mayakki varukiṉṟaṉar. kalvi vēṟu ārāycci vēṟu. ceṉṉiyum peṅkaḷūrum pōṉṟa paṉmoḻi vaṭṭāraṅkaḷil cila kūlik kārarum paṉmoḻi pēcukiṉṟaṉar. avarellām moḻinūlaṟiñarō!
tamiḻic cevviyāy aṟitaṟku tamiḻar teṟkiṉiṉṟu vaṭakkup pōntār eṉṉum varalāṟṟuṇmiyaṟivu iṉṟiyamiyāta aṭippaṭi yākum. iḵtillār tamiḻiṉ toṉṉili aṟital teṉmali kāṇap poṉmali celvatē yokkum.
itu kāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl `uvami' teṉ collē yeṉṟum atu uva eṉṉum mutaṉiliyaṭip piṟanta toḻiṟ peyareṉṟum ataṉ aṭivēr ukarac col eṉṟum akaṉ kaṇṇum ottaṟ karuttu koṇ ṭuḷḷateṉṟum ataṉāl atu ataṉiṉṟu mōṉi aḷḷi piṉṉit tiripākat tōṉṟiyuḷḷa ottaṟ karuttuc coṟkaḷi yellām tāṅki niṟkum taṉipperum tūṇ eṉṟum aṇikaṭkellāntāyāṉa uvami yilakkaṇattiyē aṇiyilakkaṇamākavuṅ kūṟum tolkāppiya uvama iyal vaṭamoḻiyil piṟkālattu virivākat tōṉṟiya aṇinūlkaṭ kellām mūlameṉṟum varalāṟṟu aṭippaṭiyilē collārāycciyum moḻiyārāycciyum ceytal vēṇṭumeṉṟum ārāycciyiṉṟik kuṟaṭum pētiyum pōlak koṇṭatu viṭāmi āciriyark kaḻakaṉṟeṉṟum aṟintu koḷka. tolkāppiya ilakkaṇattiṉ toṉmiyum muṉmiyum tūymiyum tāymiyum ākiya ellā ceytikaḷum eṉ ``tolkāppiyak kaṭṭurikaḷ'' eṉṉum nūlil virivāka viḷakkappeṟum.
uvami teṉ collē eṉpataṟku vēṟu cila cāṉṟukaḷumuḷa.
tamiḻil ``uvami'' eṉṉum collil ``uva'' eṉpatu viṉippakuti; `mi' eṉpatu vikuti; ākavē, `uva' eṉpatē uyirnāṭiyāṉa uṟuppu.
`mi' vikuti. vantami. varukiṉṟami, eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa coṟkaḷil toḻiṟ peyar vikutiyākavum, ciṟumi, poṟumi eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa coṟkaḷil toḻiṟ paṇpuppeyar vikutiyākavum, naṉmi, tīmi eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa coṟkaḷil paṇpup peyar vikutiyākavum irukkum.
vaṭamoḻiyil `upamā' eṉṉum collil `upa' eṉpatu iṭic collāṉa muṉṉoṭṭu (upacarkkam. Prefix) eṉṟum `mā' eṉpatu aḷavu kuṟitta colleṉṟum koṇṭu `upamā' eṉpataṟku otta aḷavu oṉṟiṉik koṇṭu, oṉṟi aḷattal, oppu nōkku, oppumi, uvami eṉa muṟiyē poruḷ kūṟappaṭum. ākavē, `mā' eṉpatē ciṟanta uṟuppām. itaṉāl tamiḻil nilimoḻi allatu piṉ moḻip poruḷ ciṟantum vaṭamoḻiyil varumoḻi allatu muṉmoḻip poruḷ ciṟantum uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum teḷivām.
uvamāṉam eṉṉum tamiḻc collil `māṉam' eṉpatu aṭimāṉam (aṭivu) paṭimāṉam (paṭivu) eṉpavaṟṟiṟ pōl oru vikuti. upamāṉa eṉṉum vaṭacollil, māṉa eṉpatu aḷavu eṉṟu poruḷ paṭuttap paṭuñ col.
iṅṅaṉam vēṟupaṭṭiruppatāl, tamiḻ `uvami' vēṟu vaṭamoḻi, `upamā' vēṟu eṉṟu karutaṟka. iraṇṭum oṉṟē. vaṭamoḻiyil uḷḷa coṟkaḷellām vaṭacollēyeṉṟu kāṭṭutaṟku, atil uḷḷa teṉ coṟkaḷellām poruntap poyttalākavum poruntāp poyttalāka vum poruḷ kūṟuvatum, atuvum iyalākkāl iṭukuṟi (rūṭam) eṉṟu muttiriyiṭṭu viṭuvatum, vaṭanūlār vaḻakkam. veḷḷiyāṉa ciṟu tavaca vaki yoṉṟik kuṟikkum cāmi eṉṉum collic `ciyāmā' eṉṟu tirittuk karuppāṉatu eṉṟum; naṉṟāy eṇṇeyil ventapiṉ tiṉṉum vaṭi vaki yoṉṟik kuṟikkum āmivaṭi eṉṉum colli `āmavaṭa' eṉattirittu `naṉṟāka vēkātatu' eṉṟum; mukam eṉpataṉ kaṭip pōliyāṉa mukaṉ eṉṉum colli `mu. kaṉ' eṉappirittu, tōṇṭap peṟṟa kiṭaṅku pōṉṟa vāyiyuṭiya uṟuppu eṉṟum, vaṭamuṉiyāy tōṉṟum oruvaki neruppik kuṟikkum vaṭavi eṉṉum collip `paṭapā' eṉṟu tirittu. ataṉoṭumukam eṉpatic cērttup `paṭapāmukam' eṉa ākki, ``peṭṭikkutirimukam pōṉṟatu eṉṟum poruḷ kūṟuvār vēṟu eṉtāṉ collār!
tolkāppiyattilum toṉṉūlkaḷilum uvami aṇiyilakkaṇa muṟiyiṟ kūṟappaṭāmal poruḷilakkaṇa muṟiyilēyē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tōḻi, talivaṉ taliviyoṭu, ciṟappākat talivaṉoṭu uriyāṭum pōtu uḷḷuṟiyuvamam ēṉiyuvamam eṉṉum iruvaki yuvami kaḷiyum āḷuvatu marapu. ivaṟṟi viḷakka eḻuntatē uvamayiyal, piṟkālattil vaṭanūlār aṇiyilakkaṇam vakutta pōtu tamiḻilakkaṇa uvamayiyali payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭatuṭaṉ uvami eṉṉum peyariyum `upamā' eṉat tirittuk koṇṭaṉar. tolkāppiyam pāṇiṉīyattiṟku muntiyateṉṉum uṇmi oppuk koḷḷappaṭāviṭiṉum, tolkāppiyam kaṭikkaḻakat toṭakkatteḻunta vaḻi nūlātalāl ataṟku muntiya akattiyam mutaliya nūlkaḷ vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇaṅkaṭku mutaṉūl eṉpati evarum maṟukka muṭiyātu. mēlum aintiram pāṇiṉīyam mutaliya vaṭamoḻi viyākaraṇaṅkaḷ ellām naṉṉūl pōl eḻuttum collum ākiya iraṇṭē kūṟuvaṉaveṉṟum tolkāppiyam mutaliya toṉṉūlkaḷ yāvum eḻuttuc coṟ poruḷ mūṉṟiyuṅ kūṟum piṇṭaṅkaḷ eṉṟum vēṟu pāṭaṟital vēṇṭum. vaṭamoḻiyil, yāppilakkaṇam cantacu allatu cantōpiciti eṉṉum nūlkaḷilum, aṇiyilakkaṇam alaṅkāram eṉṉum nūlkaḷilum vēṟākak kūṟappaṭum. tamiḻilō, yāppum aṇiyum poruḷilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷ. avi taṉi nūṟkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭaviṭattum, poruḷ ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷākavē eṉṟuṅ koḷḷap peṟum. uvami aṇikaṭkellām tāyātalāl, aṇiyilakkaṇam uvamayiyal aṭakkamām.
tolkāppiyar uvamaviyalil nālvaki ēṉiyuvamaṅkaḷum aivaki uḷḷuṟi uvamaṅkaḷum kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa; uvami uṟuppukaḷ māṇavarkku eḷitāy viḷaṅkuvaṉavātalāl kūṟappeṟavilli. āyiṉ, eccaviyalil uvamattoki kūṟappaṭṭiruppatāl ataṉ maṟutaliyākiya uvama viriyum kūṟāmal kūṟappaṭṭatākak koḷḷap peṟum.
uvamam, uvamavurupu potut taṉmi, poruḷ eṉṉum nāṉkum uvami uṟuppukkaḷām. innāṉkum amivatu viriyuvami; uvama urupu allatu uvamavurupum kotut taṉmiyum kuṟivatu tokiyuvami. vaṭamoḻiyil innāṉkiyum muṟiyē, upamāṉa, upama vācaka, cātāraṇa tarma, upamēya eṉṟum; viriyuvamattip pūrṇōpamā eṉṟum; toki yuvamatti `luptōpamā' eṉṟum aḻippar. iviyellām piṟkālattu vaḷarcciyum moḻi peyarppumākum.
itu kāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl `uvami' teṉcollē eṉṟum uvamayiyal teṉ moḻiyilakkaṇa kūṟē eṉṟum tērntu teḷika. tāḻvuṇarcci nīṅkuka! tali nimirka!
13
tiraviṭam teṉ colliṉ tiripē
aṅkam, vaṅkam, kaliṅkam, tiraviṭam - `am' īṟupeṟṟaṉa. nāṭukaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ - kālṭuvel kāṭṭiya mēṟkōḷum tāyumāṉavar pāṭiya pāṭalum tamiḻ tirinta vaḻiyum vaḻaṅkiya muṟiyum.
tiraviṭam allatu tirāviṭam eṉṉum col em moḻiyatu eṉpatu iṉṟum palarkkut teriyātiruppatāṉum, aṇmiyil atupaṟṟi ōr aiyaviṉā teṉṟalil tōṉṟiyatāṉum ammayakki aṟavē aṟuttaṟku eḻuntatik kaṭṭuri.
nāṭṭup peyarkaḷum moḻip peyarkaḷum paṇṭik kālattu `am' īṟu peṟṟē vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉpatu,
``aṅkam vaṅkam kaliṅkam kucikam
cintu cōṉakam tiraviṭam ciṅkaḷam
makatam kōcalam marāṭam koṅkaṇam
tuḷuvam cāvakam cīṉam kāmpōcam
paruṇampap parameṉap patiṉeṇpāṭi''
eṉṉum tivākara nūṟpāvum,
ciṅkaḷam cōṉakam cāvakam cīṉam tuḷukkuṭakam
koṅkaṇam kaṉṉaṭam
kollam teluṅkam kaliṅkam vaṅkam
kaṅkam makatam kaṭāram kavuṭam kaṭuṅkucalam
taṅkum pukaḻttamiḻ cūḻpati ṉēḻpuvi tāmiviyē
eṉṉum paḻañ ceyyuḷum uṇarttum. immuṟi paṟṟit tamiḻum tamiḻam eṉa vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. itaṉālēyē, īṟukeṭṭa makara vīṟṟup puṇarcciyāka tamiḻap piḷḷi, tamiḻanākaṉ, tamiḻavaṉavaṉ mutaliya puṇar moḻikaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. naṉṉūlār ivaṟṟit tamiḻ eṉṉum nilimoḻi akarac cāriyi peṟṟup puṇarntateṉṟum, tolkāppiyar ammoḻi akkuc cāriyi peṟṟup puṇarntateṉṟum, kūṟiṉar. ivar kūṟṟiṉpaṭi, koṅkavaṇṇāṉ eṉpatik koṅku eṉṉum nilimoḻi akara allatu akkuc cāriyi peṟṟup puṇarntatākavum, tuḷuva vēḷāḷaṉ eṉpatit tuḷu eṉṉum nilimoḻi avvāṟu puṇarntatākavum, koḷḷavēṇṭi varumātaliṉ, atu poruntāteṉa viṭukka. tamiḻ eṉṉum col mutalāvatu vaṭamoḻiyil tirinta vaṭivam tramiḷam eṉpatē. ḻakaram vaṭa moḻiyiliṉmiyālum, uyirmeym mutali meymmutalākki rakaratti vaḻic cerukal am moḻikkiyalpātalāṉum, accol ammoḻiyil avvaṭivi aṭinta teṉka. tiramiḷam eṉpatu tamiḻ eṉat tiritalumatu eṉṟu pirayōka vivēka nūlār (pak. 4) talikīḻākak kūṟiya kūṟṟil, avvaṭivē āḷap peṟutal kāṇka.
kālṭuvel. purāṇaṅkaḷiṉ paḻiya maliyāḷamoḻi peyarppu kaḷilellām ivvaṭivē peruvaḻakkāyiruppatākak kuṇṭarṭṭuk kūṟuvatākavum avar eṭutturippar. tiramiḷam eṉṉum colliṟku (1) pañca tiraviṭa tēcaṅkaḷ, (2) tamiḻ eṉa iruporuḷ kūṟum ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻakarātiyum.
tiramiḷam eṉpatu piṉpu tramiṭam eṉat tirintatu. timiṭam eṉpatum ciṟitu kālattiṉ piṉ traviṭam eṉat tiriyalāyiṟṟu. iv viṟuti vaṭivattiṉ nīṭciyē trāviṭam eṉpatu. itu tamiḻil tirāviṭam eṉṟākum.
tramiḷam, tramiṭam, traviṭam eṉṉuñ rakarañ cerukiya vaṭivu kaḷellām, vaṭamoḻi vaḻakkēyaṉṟip piṟamoḻi allatu piṟanāṭṭu vaḻakkalla. piṟamoḻikaḷellām tamiḻiṟ pōl rakaramaṟṟa vaṭivē vaḻaṅkum. ki. pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭil intiyāviṟku vanta iveṉ ticāṅ (Hwen Thsang) eṉṉum cīṉa vaḻippōkkar kuṟippil, cimolo (Tchimo - lo) eṉṉum vaṭivam uḷḷatu. itit timala (Dimala) allatu timara (Dimara) eṉṟum paṭikkalām eṉpar kālṭuvel. pālimoḻiyiluḷḷa makāvamicam (mahavanso) eṉṉum ilaṅki varalāṟṟil tamilo' (Damilo) eṉṉum vaṭivam uḷḷatu. airōppiyar potuvākat tamul (Tamul) eṉṟaṉar. avaruḷ tēṉiya viṭiyūḻiyar - (Danish missionaries) maṭṭum tamulikka moḻi (Lingua Darmulia) eṉa aḻittaṉar. āṅkilattil `tamiḻ (Tamil) eṉṉum vaṭivam vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. tamiḻakam eṉṉum peyar, mēṉāṭṭup paḻiya tēcappaṭaṅkaḷilum ñālanūṟ cuvaṭikaḷilum `tamirikke' (Damirice) eṉṟum, timirikka (Dimirica) eṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
muḻukippōṉa kumarikkaṇṭattil ki. mu. (ēṟattāḻa) aimpatiṉāyiram āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ muḷitteḻuntatum, tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkellān tāyumāṉa paḻantamiḻ oru kālattil paṉimali (imayam) vari paravi yiruntatālum, ki. mu. 1000 āṇṭukaṭkup piṉṉarē teluṅku mutaliya tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ ataṉiṉṟu kiḷittatālum, avaṟṟuḷḷum orumoḻiyilum ki.pi. 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉ ilakkiyan tōṉṟāmiyālum, tiraviṭam eṉṉum peyar tamiḻiyum tamiḻiṉattiyum tamiḻ nāṭṭiyumē mutaṟkālattil kuṟittu niṉṟatu. tamiḻiṉiṉṟu tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ kiḷitta piṉpum, tiraviṭam eṉṉum col tamiḻit taṉippaṭak kuṟikkum vaḻakkum kālṭuvel kālam vari toṭarntu vantiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
piḷṉi lōkāriya cīyar (500 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ)
``neñcuk kiṟāḷkaṭi tīpam
aṭaṅkā neṭum piṟavi
nañcukku nalla amutam.
tamiḻa naṉṉūṟ ṟuṟikaḷ
añcuk kilakkiyam āraṇa
cāram paraca mayap
pañcuk kaṉaliṉ poṟipara
kālaṉ paṉu valkaḷē''
eṉṟa periya tirumoḻic ciṟappup pāyirattil, tamiḻa naṉṉūṟṟuṟi kaḷañcukku eṉpataṉ poruḷi viḷakkumiṭattu, tiraviṭa cātiram, eḻuttu col poruḷ yāppu alaṅkāram eṉkiṟa vilakkaṇamāṉa pañcala kṣaṇat tōṭē kūṭiyiṟē niṟpatu eṉa varintirukkiṉṟār.
18 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tiruccirāppaḷḷiyil vāḻntirunta tāyumāṉa aṭikaḷ pāṭiya kallāta pērkaḷē nallavarkaḷ eṉṉum ceyyuḷil,
``vaṭamoḻiyilē
vallāṉ oruttaṉ vara
vuntirā viṭattilē
vantatā vivakarippēṉ
valla tamiḻaṟiñar variṉ
aṅṅaṉē vaṭa moḻiyil
vacaṉaṅkaḷ ciṟitu pukalvēṉ''
eṉṉum pakutiyil, tiraviṭam tamiḻ eṉṉum iru collum oru moḻi yiyē kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. āḻvārkaḷ aruḷic ceyta nālāyirat teyvap paṉuval, tamiḻ vētam, tiraviṭavētam, tiraviṭap pirapantam eṉṉum peyarkaḷāl vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
pākavata purāṇattil, cattiya virataṉ eṉṉum peyarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum oru tamiḻaracaṉ tiraviṭapati eṉappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. tiraviṭam eṉṉum collukku, tamiḻnāṭu eṉṉum poruḷum akarātikaḷiṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
āriyar intiyāviṟ kuṭipukuntu vaṭa intiyā muḻutum paraviya piṉ kumarilapaṭṭar kālam (7ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) vari teṉṉāṭum, teṉṉāṭṭu makkaḷiṉaṅkaḷum avi pēciya moḻikaḷum, tamiḻiṉ talimipaṟṟi, tiraviṭam eṉṟē aḻikkappaṭṭu vantaṉa.
maṉu, tam (āriya) aṟa nūlil (10 : 43,44) āriyarallāta palvēṟu iṉat tārik kuṟikkumiṭattu tamiḻariyum teluṅkar mutaliya tirāviṭariyum vēṟupaṭuttātu avarellāriyum oruṅkē taḻuvumāṟu - tirāviṭar eṉṉum colliyē āṇṭirukkiṉṟār. vintiya malikkut teṟkil vantu kuṭiyēṟiya pirāmaṇarum, tirāviṭap pirāmaṇar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. itu nāṭu paṟṟiyatēyaṉṟi iṉampaṟṟiyataṉṟu. malēyāt tamiḻar, teṉṉāppirikkat tamiḻar eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. immuṟi paṟṟiyē, tēvāra mūvaruḷ oruvarāṉa tiruñāṉacampantarum tirāviṭa cicu eṉappaṭṭār. vaṭa nāṭṭu moḻi nūlaṟiñar, mutaṟkaṇ, (āriya) vēta kālattu intiya vaṭṭāra moḻikaḷic camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiyuṇmi paṟṟip pirākirutam eṉa aḻittaṉar. avaṟṟil aḷaviṟanta āriyar coṟkaḷ kalantu citinta vaṭiviṟ kāṇappaṭuvatu paṟṟi, piṟ kālattār ammoḻikaḷi vaṭamoḻi (camaṟkiruta) vaḻiyiṉavākap piṟaḻavuṇarntaṉar. itē kāraṇam paṟṟi, paḻañcēra nāṭṭut tamiḻt tiripākiya maliyāḷatti vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu vantatāka iṉṟu cilar kūṟuvatu kāṇka.
ki. mu. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarākak karutappaṭum kāttiyāyaṉar, picāci, curacēṉi, mākati, makārāṭṭiri eṉap pirākiruta moḻikaḷi nāṉkākavē koṇṭaṉar. tirāviṭatti avar koḷḷātatu aṟiyāmiyō puṟakkaṇippō aṟikilam.
orukāl makārāṭṭiriyil ati aṭakkiṉar pōlum. piṟkālat tāciriyar cilar tirāviṭi eṉpatu oru ciṟutarap pirākirutam eṉṟaṉar. āyiṉ 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta pāpu rācēntiralāl mittirā eṉṉum vaṅkāḷar, ati cūra cēṉiyōṭotta perum pirākirutam eṉak kūṟiṉar.
tamiḻiṉiṉṟu mutalāvatu pirinta tiraviṭa moḻi. teluṅkākum. atu vaṭa ticiyil tōṉṟiyami paṟṟi vaṭuku eṉappaṭṭatu. kumarilapaṭṭar kālattil teluṅku taṉittuk kūṟappaṭumaḷavu vaḷarcciyaṭintuviṭṭataṉāl, avar atuvari tirāviṭam eṉṉum potup peyarālēyē vaḻaṅkiya centamiḻiyum koṭun tamiḻkaḷiyum, teluṅku tamiḻ moḻi eṉṉum poruḷpaṭa āntira tirāviṭa `pāci' eṉṟaṉar. teluṅkiṟkup piṉ tamiḻiṉiṉṟu pirinta perumoḻi kaṉṉaṭam ataṉāl
vaṭakali teṉkali vaṭuku kaṉṉaṭam eṉak kamparāmāyaṇac ciṟappuppāyirac ceyyuḷoṉṟil kaṉṉaṭamum cērkkap peṟṟatu. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkuppiṉ, maliyāḷam, tuḷu, kuṭakam mutaliya piṟa koṭun tamiḻkaḷum pirintu pōyiṉa. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil kālṭuvel tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ patiṉmūṉṟeṉak kaṇakkiṭṭār. iṉṟō avi pattoṉpa teṉap parō, emaṉō eṉṉum iru mēli moḻi nūl vallārāl kaṇak kiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. oru kālattil intiyā muḻutum tiraviṭam paravi yiruntateṉpataṟku - iṉṟum peluc cittāṉattil pirākuvīyum, vaṅkāḷat til irācamakālum vaḻaṅki varuvatē pōtiya cāṉṟām. āriyar vantu vaṭa intiyā muḻutum paraviyapiṉ, vintiya malikkut teṟkiluḷḷa moḻika ḷellām tiraviṭam eṉṉuṅ koḷkiyiruntatu. ataṉālēyē, teluṅku, karunāṭakam, marātti, kūrccaram, (kucarātti) tirāviṭam eṉṉum aintum pañca tirāviṭam eṉṟu vaṭa nūlkaḷum kūṟiṉa. ippaṭṭiyil tirāviṭam eṉṉum peyar tamiḻi maṭṭuṅ kuṟittal kavaṉikkattakkatu. tirāviṭa(m) eṉpatu cila vaṭa nūlkaḷil tirāvira(m) eṉṟum tirintatu. ṭakaram rakaramāṉatu pōli. itukāruṅkūṟiyavaṟṟāl, toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻiyē taṉippaṭavum talimiyākavum kuṟittu vanta tiraviṭam eṉṉum col, tamiḻam eṉpataṉ tiripē eṉpatu, varalāṟṟuṇarcci yuṭiyārkkellām teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkutal tiṇṇam. āyiṉum, āṉikkum aṭicaṟukkum eṉṉum paḻamoḻikkēṟpa, kālṭuvel tiraviṭam eṉṉum collē muṟiyē, (traviṭam - tramiṭam - tramiḷa tamiḻ) eṉat tirintateṉṟu talikīḻākak koṇṭār. āyiṉ, kiriyarcaṉ atit tirutti uṇmiyik kūṟiyatu makiḻccikkuriyatu. eṉiṉum, iṉṟum cilar mayaṅkuvataṟkuk kāraṇam varalāṟṟaṟiviṉmiyē. āriyar intiyāviṟ kāl vittu ki. mu. 3000 āṇṭelliyeṉṟum, tamiḻ muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭintu muttamiḻāy vaḻaṅkiya talik kaḻakak kālam ki. mu. 10,000 āṇṭelliyeṉṟum, aṟiyiṉ, oruvarum aiyuṟār eṉpatu tēṟṟam. vaṭamoḻiyil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa teṉ coṟkaḷiruntum avaṟṟi yellām vaṭacolleṉak kāṭṭuvatiyē vaṭamoḻi yakarātikaḷellām kōṭpāṭākak koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamoḻi tēvamoḻiyeṉum mayakku pulavaruḷḷattilu miruntatiṉāl kampar atit ``tēvapāṭi'' eṉṟār. tolkāppiyac collatikāravuriyil (401) tamiḻc col vaṭapāṭikkaṭ cellāmiyāṉum, vaṭacol ellāt tēyattiṟkum potuvātalāṉum eṉavurittār cēṉāvariyar. ittakiya nilikaḷ, vaṭavar tiraviṭam eṉṉum coṟkup poruntap poyttalākac cila poruṭ kāraṇam kāṭṭat tūṇṭiṉa. tru eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi vērturattutaṟ poruḷ taruvateṉṟum, āriyarāl teṟkē turattap paṭṭavar tiraviṭar eṉappaṭṭaṉar eṉṟum kūṟuvar oru cārār. tiraviṭam eṉpatu teṟku eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvateṉṟum, teṉkōṭi mākāṇattār tiraviṭar eṉappaṭṭaṉar eṉṟum kūṟuvar maṟṟoru cārār. tiraviṭam teṟkēyiruppatiṉālēyē, tiraviṭam eṉṉum coṟkut teṟkeṉṉum vaḻip poruḷ tōṉṟiṟṟu. civañāṉa muṉivar uḷḷatik koṇṭu nallatip paṇṇum muṟiyil, turattutal allatu oṭṭutal eṉṉum vērpporuḷi oppuk koṇṭu, tīviṉi yakaṟṟum aṟa nūlkaḷ tamiḻiṟ ciṟantiruppatu paṟṟi, tam kāñcippurāṇattil,
evviṉiyum opputalāl
tirāviṭam eṉṟiyal pāṉaṭa'' eṉṟu pāṭiṉār.
tru eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi vēr `tura' eṉṉum tamiḻ vēriṉ tiripē. ittamiḻ vēr mēliyāriyattil tiyūttāṉiyam eṉṉum kiḷiyilum ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
cerumaṉ : Treiben - paṇṭi āṅkilam Drifen.
āṅkilam : Drive,
turattal eṉpatu ōṭṭutal.
ākavē, vaṭamoḻiyār kūṟum iru poruḷum, tiraviṭam eṉṉum colli vaṭacollākak kāṭṭa iyalāmi kāṇka.
iṉi, oru cila tamiḻar, tiru + iṭam = tiruviṭam - tiraviṭam - tirāviṭam eṉa vantatākak kāṭṭuvar. ivvaralāṟu uttikkup poruntāmiyōṭu cāṉṟum aṟṟatu. oru cār iḷampulavar, tiru + iṭam = tirāviṭam eṉap puṇarttu, 
aṉṟu varukāli
āvākutalum
ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ
uritteṉa moḻipa''	(258)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya vitiyiyum,
atu muṉ varumaṉ
ṟāṉṟān tūkkiṉ	(180)
eṉṉum naṉṉūl vitiyiyum tuṇiyākak kāṭṭuvar. atu + aṉṟu eṉpatu, ataṉṟu eṉṟu puṇarntu ceyyuḷil atō aṉṟu eṉṟu aḷapeṭutta niliyiyē mēṟ kāṭṭiya vitikaḷ kuṟippatāl, avar kūṟṟu caṟṟum poruntātateṉa viṭukka. mēlum, avar karuttuppaṭi, tiru + iṭam eṉpatu tirīṭam allatu tirīyiṭam eṉṟē puṇara vēṇṭum eṉka.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, tiraviṭam eṉpatu teṉ collē yeṉṟu teḷika.
tamiḻ eṉpatu tūymiyāṉa tamiḻiyum, tiraviṭam eṉpatu tamiḻiṉiṉṟu tirinta teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭa mutaliya iṉamoḻikaḷiyum iṉṟu kuṟikkumeṉka. vaṭamoḻiyil uyirmeym mutali meymutalākki rakaratti vaḻic cerukaṟku, paṭi - prati, pavaḻam - pravāḷam eṉpaṉa eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷ.
naṉṟi : teṉṟal 19.9.1959
14
tamiḻ mukam
tamiḻ, kaṭanta mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka akappakiyālum puṟappakiyālum, maṟiyuṇṭum kuṟiyuṇṭum vantiruntum airōppāvi lum iṅkilāntilum orucila naṭunili moḻiyārāycciyāḷar tamiḻ mukam tirumpi ataṉ toṉmiyiyum muṉmiyiyum vaṭamoḻikkuc col vaḻaṅkiya vaṉmiyiyum uṇarntu, ati ulakiṉukkuṇartta muṉ vantiruppatu mika makiḻattakkatē. āyiṉum; ēmāṟṟuvatilum aṟi pōvatilum tuṟipōya orucila puṟattamiḻarum pōlit tamiḻarum, iṉṟum, mukam eṉṉum col vaṭacolleṉṟu valikkat tuṇivatu ettaṉi iraṅkattakka ceytiyām!
vaṭamoḻic coṟṟokutikaḷil maṭṭumiṉṟi, ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻakarātiyilum, paṇṭārakar Dr. u. vē. cāminātiyar avarkaḷiṉ kuṟuntokiyarāyccik kuṟippilum, mukam eṉpatu vaṭa colleṉak kuṟittiruppatu, ataṉ vaṭamoḻi mūlakkoḷki viruppiṉarkkuc cāṉṟāyt tōṉṟum.
āṅkilar avartam tāymoḻiyām āṅkilattil āppirikkamum āttirēliyamum pōṉṟa ettuṇip puṉmoḻic coṟkaḷ kalantiruppi ṉum, avi emmoḻiyiṉa veṉat tāmākavē ārāyntu makiḻvoṭu uṇmiyi veḷippaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar. āyiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyāḷarō, kaṭanta mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷākat tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpukoṇṭiruppiṉum, vaṭamoḻiyum ataṉ ilakkaṇa vilakkiyamum teṉmoḻiyālum ataṉ ilakkaṇavilakkiyattālum mika vaḷampaṭuttappaṭṭiruppiṉum, vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kalantuḷḷa (āyirakkaṇakkāṉa allāviṭiṉ) nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa teṉ coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟukūṭat teṉcolleṉa oppuk koṇṭilar. āyiṉum, makaṉ tantiyit tantiyeṉa oppukkoḷḷāviṭiṉ, tanti taṉ tantimiyiṟ ṟiriyāmipōl, teṉcollum teṉ colleṉa oppukkoḷḷappaṭāviṭiṉum taṉ teṉ coṉmiyiṉiṉṟu tirintuviṭā teṉka.
vaṭamoḻi ekkāraṇam paṟṟiyum piṟamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉkoḷḷā teṉak kūṟal vēṇṭumeṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyāḷar vaṉkōḷātaliṉ, atu aṟiñar āyviṟkuriyateṉa appāl otukka.
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻakarātiyum, vaṭanūlāriyē kuruṭṭuttaṉamāyp piṉpaṟṟuvatāṉum, tamiḻaṉparālum tamiḻmoḻiyaṟiña rālum tokukkap peṟāmiyālum, ataṉiyum poruṭpaṭuttaṟka.
iṉi, paṇṭārakar cāminātiyarum, paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyap patippīṭṭu vakiyil taliciṟanta toṇṭāṟṟiṉarēyaṉṟi taṉittamiḻ viruppiṉarallar. vaṭamoḻiyuyarvum teṉmoḻit tāḻvum eṉṟum nilavavēṇṭumeṉṉum koḷkiyiṉar; vaṭacoṟkaḷālaṉṟi vaṭaveḻuttu kaḷālum tamiḻiṉ tūymiyik kulittavar; tamiḻi ilakkiyac cemmoḻiyāka koḷḷa iciyātavar; moḻinūṟpayiṟci ciṟitum illātavar; ātaliṉ avar kūṟṟum koḷḷattakkataṉṟeṉa viṭukka.
mukam eṉṉum col teṉcollēyeṉa oṉpāṉ āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē eṉ `mutaṟṟāymoḻi'yil viḷakkiyiruppiṉum, ataṉip pārāmiyālō, ārāycciyiṉmiyālō, vaṭamoḻi veṟipaṟṟiya tamiḻ veṟuppālō, aṭimit taṉattiṉālō, `mukam' vaṭacolleṉa iṉṟum orucār tamiḻāciriyar vakuppiṟ colvatum, atukēṭṭu māṇavar mayaṅkuvatum, cila tērvāḷarmāṇavar pōṭṭit tērvukaḷil mukam emmoḻic col eṉa viṉavi uṇmiyurippārit tavaṟuvippatum vaḻakkamāyiruntuvarukiṉṟaṉa. itu palarkkum iṭarppāṭṭi viḷittaliṉ, iṉimēl ipporuḷpaṟṟi aiyuṟavum ēmāṟṟum tarukkamum illātavāṟu, mukam eṉpatu teṉcollēyeṉa muṭinta muṭipāka nāṭṭutaṟku eḻuntatikkaṭṭuriyeṉka.
mukam eṉpatu teṉcollēyeṉak kōṭaṟku maṟukkoṇāc cāṉṟukaḷ varumāṟu :-
(1) tamiḻil mūlamum poruḷum muṟṟum poruntutal :-
mukam (muku + am) = muṉpu, muṉṉuṟuppu, muṉpakkam.
muṉi, nuṉi, tōṟṟam.
mukappu, mukaṉi, mukaccarakku, mukatali, mukamaṇṭapam, mukamaṉi, mukavācal, mukavuri, urimukam, tuṟimukam, nūṉmukam, pōrmukam mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷi nōkki muṉmik karuttē mukam eṉṉum colliṉ vērpporuḷ eṉpatu viḷaṅkum.
uyirkaḷiṉ (pirāṇikaḷiṉ) muṉpakkattiṟ ciṟanta uṟuppu taliyiṉ muṉpuṟamātaliṉ, atu mukam eṉappaṭṭatu.
uyirkaḷiṉ iyalpāṉa iyakkam allatu tōṟṟam muṉṉōkkiyē nikaḻtaliṉ muṉmik karuttil tōṟṟak karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
e-kā :
mukam	- tōṟṟam
mukañceytal - tōṉṟutal
mukampeṟutal	 - tōṉṟutal
muku + uḷ - mukuḷ - mukiḷ = arumpu
mukiḷ + am - mukiḷam = arumpu
arumputal = tōṉṟutal
mukuḷ - (mukur) - mukuram = taḷir
mukiḷ - mukiḻ = arumpu. mukiḷam - mukiḻam = malarum paruvat tarumpu
mukiḻttal - tōṉṟutal
``mūvaki yulaku mukiḻttaṉa muṟiyē'' (ainkuṟunūṟu kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu).
muku + ai - muki = arumpu, mukital - arumputal.
mukittal - arumputal
muku - mukku - mokku = arumpu. mokku - mokkuḷ = arumpu.
mukam eṉṉum collil muku eṉṉum pakutiyum, muku eṉṉum pakutiyil mu eṉṉum uyirmeyyil u eṉṉum uyirum, uyirnāṭiyāṉa uṟuppukkaḷām.
muṉ, muntu mutaliya coṟkaḷil mukaramum, ūṅku, uṅku, uṅṅaṉ, utu, uvaṉ mutaliya coṟkaḷil ūkāra ukaraṅkaḷum, aṭippaṭiyāyiruntu muṉmik karuttiyuṇarttutal kāṇka.
mukam - mukar - mukari - mōri.
mukar - mukari - muṉpuṟam, toṭakkam
mukam - mukaṉ - mukaṉi - mōṉi.
muku - muka - mukappu.
(2) vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kūṟum mūlamum poruḷum oru ciṟitum poruntāmi :
vaṭamoḻiyil mukam eṉpatu mukha (mukha) eṉṟē vaḻaṅkiṉum, ataṉi vaṭacollākak kāṭṭal vēṇṭi, ṉakara (nakara) meyyi īṟṟiṟ cērttu mukhaṉ (Mukhan) eṉṉum vaṭivip paṭittu; atil `mu' eṉṉum mutaṉmiyāṉa pakutiyip poruḷaṟṟa muṉṉoṭṭāka (Prefix) ttaḷḷi, eñciya khaṉ (Khan) eṉṉum kūṟṟit tōṇṭutaṟ poruḷtarum viṉippakutiyākki, tōṇṭutal, tōṇṭappaṭṭa kiṭaṅku, kiṭaṅku pōṉṟa vāy, vāyuḷḷa iṭam (mukam) eṉa, muṟiyē mukam eṉṉum coṟkup poruḷurippar vaṭanūlār.
itu pakuttaṟiviṟkuc caṟṟum poruntāmiyoṭu,
``ūḻiṟ peruvali yāvuḷa maṟṟoṉṟu,
cūḻiyaṉun tāṉ mun tuṟum''
eṉṉum teyvat tirumaṟikkēṟpa, mīṇṭum mukam eṉpatu teṉcollē yeṉpati valiyuṟuttal kāṇka.
mukam - mukaṉ (kaṭippōli).
iṉi, kaṉ eṉṉum collum teṉcollē.
kal - kaṉ. kallutal = tōṇṭutal. kaṉ - kaṉṉam = tōṇṭutal,
cuvarit tuḷittut tiruṭutal.
lakaram ṉakaramākat tirital iyalpu.
o.nō ; āl - āṉ (3ām vēṟṟumiyurupu).
mēla - mēṉa.
(3) vaṭamoḻiyilum mukha eṉpatu mukam eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkal.
e-kā : mukha - kamala - mukattāmari (tāmari mukam)
mukha eṉṉum coṟku vaṭamoḻiyil vāy eṉpatē mutaṉmip poruḷāk koḷḷiṉum, mukam eṉṉum poruṭkum vaṭṭañcuṟṟi vaḻiyē varutal kāṇka.
(4) toṭarpuṭiya teṉ coṟkaḷ :
taliyil mukam muṉpuṟamāyiruttalpōl mukattil mūkku muṉṉuṟuppāyiruttalāl, muṉmik karutti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa mukam eṉṉum col tamiḻil mūkkiyum uṇarttum.
mukam - muka, mukattal = mūkkāl maṇam nukartal.
muka - mō - mōppu - mōppam
mukam - mukar, mukartal = mōttal. mukar - mōr.
muku - mukku - mūkku
mūkku - mūkkup pōṉṟa mūli
mukku - mukki = āṟu tirumpum mūli.
(5) etuki vaṭivāṉa iṉapporuṭ coṟkaḷ :
muku eṉṉum aṭikku etukiyāṉa nuku, puku mutaliya aṭikaḷum, muṉmik karuttiṉ vaḻippaṭṭa tōṉṟaṟ karuttiyuṇarttuvaṉavākum.
nuku - nukumpu = paṉiyiṉ iḷamaṭal
nuku - nuṅku = iḷampaṉaṅkāyccuḷi allatu koṭṭi.
nuku - (naku) nāku = iḷami.
puku - pū, pūttal = tōṉṟutal.
``pūntaliṟ pūvāmi naṉṟu''
pū - pō - pōttu = iḷaṅkiḷi.
pōttu - pōntu = paṉaṅkuruttu
pōntu - pōnti = paṉaṅkuruttu.
puku - (poku) - pokil = arumpu
pokil - pōkil = arumpu
ḻakaram cila coṟkaḷil kakaramākat tiriyum.
e - ṭu : toḻuti - tokuti. muḻi - muki.
iṅṅaṉamē, nuḻu, puḻu, muḻu mutaliya ḻakara uyirmeyyīṟṟuc coṟkaḷum kakara vuyir meyyīṟṟiṉavāyt tirintiruttal vēṇṭum.
muḷ - muḻu muku, nuḷ - nuḻu - nuku,
muḷ - muḷi. muḷittal = tōṉṟutal.
nuḻuntu - iḷampākku. nuḻāy = iḷampākku.
āyirakkaṇakkāṉa paḻan teṉcoṟkaḷ aḻintu pōyiṉmiyāl, pala karuttukaḷi iṇikkum aṇṭukaḷi ellā moḻi mutal aṭikaṭkum kāṭṭamuṭintilatu.
(6) vaṭamoḻiyiṟ piṟa colliruttal :
mukattik kuṟittaṟku āṉaṉam, vataṉam, mutaliya piṟacoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiluḷa.
(7) teṉmoḻiyiṟ piṟa colliṉmi :
teṉmoḻiyil mukattik kuṟittaṟkut toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvatu mukam eṉṉum col oṉṟē.
(8) teṉcol vaḷam :
ili, tāḷ, tōki, ōli eṉa orē, nilittiṇicciṉiyi nālvakippaṭuttavum, vaṭu (mā), mūcu (palā), kaccal (vāḻi) eṉa mukkaṉikaṭkum piñcuniliyil ciṟappuccol vaḻaṅkavum terinta matimāṉ paṇṭit tamiḻarkku mukattik kuṟittuc collavilliyeṉpatu, pakuttaṟivuṭiyārkkuk kūṟum kūṟṟaṉṟu.
(9) vaṭamoḻiyil teṉcoluṇmi :
āyirakkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil veḷippaṭiyāy vaḻaṅkivaruvatāl vaṭamoḻiyil teṉcollik kaṭaṉ koḷḷāteṉṉum pittar kūṟṟi eḷḷiyikaḻka.
(10) mēliyāriyattil mukam eṉṉum colliṉmi :
mēliyāriya moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟilēṉum mukam eṉṉum col liṉmiyāl, mukam eṉpatu teṉpāli mukattut tōṉṟiya teṉ collē yeṉa ōṅki aṟika.
``poyyuṭi yoruvaṉ colvaṉmiyiṉāl
meypōlummē meypōlummē''
``meyyuṭi yoruvaṉ colakāṭṭāmiyāṟ
poy pōlummē poypō lummē.''
15
vaḷḷuvaṉ eṉṉum peyar
vaḷḷuvaṉ eṉṉum peyar oru kulattiyum, tirukkuṟaḷ āciriya rākiya tiruvaḷḷuvariyum kuṟippatākum. ivaṟṟuḷ kulattik kuṟippataṟkuk kāraṇattiyē ārāya eḻuntatik kaṭṭuri. tirukkuṟaḷāciriyarik kuṟippataṟkuk kāraṇam aṭutta itaḻil ārāyappaṭum.
vaḷḷuvaṉ eṉum peyar mutaṉmutal paṟiyaruḷ oru pirivārāy aracaṉ āṇiyi avaratu nakara makkaṭku yāṉi mītēṟip perumpaṟi (pēriki) yaṟintaṟivikkum viḷamparat talivaṉik kuṟittatu. it talivaṉ aracāṇiyi makkaṭ kaṟivikkum aḷavil iṟṟi viḷampara mantiripōlvaṉ.
vaḷḷuvar paṟiyar kulattoru piriviṉareṉpatu, avaratu paṟi yariyum toḻilālum, vaḷḷuvappaṟiyaṉ eṉṟōr colluṇmiyālum, ikkālattum avar paṟiyariṉum uyarntavarāyiruppiṉum piṟa uyar kulattārāl iḻivā yeṉṉappaṭuvatālum paṟiyarkkuk kuruvāyiruppa tālum aṟiyappaṭum.
paṇṭikkālattil kulappiriviṉi piṟkālattiṟpōl mikak koṭitāyirāmiyāṉum, vaḷḷuvar aracar viṉiyic ceytuvantami yālum avarkku iḻiviruntilatu. itu muntiya itaḻiṟ kūṟiya pāṇar eṉṉum kaṭṭuriyālum aṟiyappaṭum.
accuvitti mikac ciṟantu pattirikikaḷālum tuṇṭaṟikkikaḷālum virintu ceyti parappum ikkālattum, vēttiyal, potuviyal ākiya iruvakic ceytikaḷiyum paṟiyaṟintu viḷamparañceyyum vaḻakku vīḻntilatu. paṇṭikkālattil paṟiyaṟitaloṉṟē ceyti parappum ciṟanta vāyilākum. itaṉāl, paṟiyaṟital eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyaruṅkūṭa viḷamparattik kuṟippatākum. ``nākkaṭippāka vāyppaṟiyaṟintu cāṟṟak kēṇmiṉ'' eṉṟār kapilarum.
potu makkaṭkup paṟiyaṟipavar allatu viḷamparañ ceypavar paṟiyareṉum potup peyarālum, aracarkku avviṉikaḷic ceypavar vaḷḷuvar eṉum ciṟappup peyarālum aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar. orē viṉiyi, atu ettuṉi iḻintatāyiṉum, aracarkkuc ceypavar uyarvatum, potu makkaṭkuc ceypavar iḻivatum ennāṭṭilum iyalpām. utāraṇamāka, aracarkku mayirviṉi ceypavaṉ cātāraṇa ampaṭṭariṉum uyarvā yeṇṇappaṭuvāṉ. avar potumakkaṭku viṉi ceyvatum, aṅṅaṉam ceypavaruṭaṉ uṟavu kalappatumilli; taṉpōṉṟa piṟa araca ampaṭṭaruṭa ṉēyē koḷviṉi koṭuppaṉiyum vittuk koḷvāṉ. iṅṅaṉamē potukkulamāṉa paṟiyariṉiṉṟum vaḷḷuvar taṉikkulamākap piriya nērntatu.
paṇṭi nūlkaḷil vaḷḷuvaṉ toḻil aracarāṇiyip paṟiyaṟin taṟivippatākavē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
``vaḷḷuvar muracamūtū raṟikeṉavaruḷiṉāṉē'' eṉṟār cīvakacintā maṇiyāciriyar tiruttakkatēvar (cey. 2149). itil vaḷḷuvar eṉpatu icipaṟṟi vaḷḷuvār eṉa nīṇṭatu. iṉi vaḷḷuvaṉ eṉṉum peyariṉ mūlatti ārāyiṉum atu paṟiyaṟiyum toḻilukku ēṟṟa poruḷiyē tarāniṟkum.
vaḷḷuvaṉ eṉum col vaḷ eṉṉum mūlattiṉiṉṟum piṟantatu. vaḷ eṉpatu paṟikkuvēṇṭiya tōliyum vāriyum avaṟṟāṟ ceyappaṭum pala poruḷkaḷiyuṅ kuṟippatākum.
vaḷ - vār (cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭu)
vaḷ - vāḷuṟi (akarāti nikaṇṭu)
vaḷ - kaṭivāḷam (akanāṉūṟu. pā. 4)
vaḷ eṉṉum mūlam puvvīṟu peṟṟum ipporuḷkaḷi uṇarttum.
``vaḷpu terintūr mativalavaniṉ
puḷḷiyaṟ kalimāp pūṇṭatērē''
eṉa aiṅkuṟu nūṟṟil (pā. 486) kaṭivāḷattiyum,
``mācaṟavicitta vārpuṟa vaḷpiṉ .......... urukeḻu muracam'' (pā. 50)
eṉap puṟanāṉūṟṟil vāriyum vaḷpu eṉṉum col kuṟittatu.
vaḷ eṉṉum mūlattiṉiṉṟum piṟanta vaḷḷuram eṉṉuñ col tōliṉ iṉapporuḷāṉa ūṉi (māmicatti)k kuṟikkum.
vaḷ eṉṉuñcol ukaraccāriyiyum aṉ īṟum peṟṟu vaḷḷuvaṉ eṉṟāyatu.
vaḷḷuvaṉ araca campantamāṉatoḻilic ceypavaṉātaliṉ, piṅkala nikaṇṭil,
``vaḷḷuvaṉ cākki yeṉumpeyar aracark
kuḷpaṭu karumat talivarkkoṉṟum''
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itil `vaḷḷuvaṉ' eṉpataṟku etukiyāyum, campantappaṭṭa eṉṉum poruḷilum vanta `uḷpaṭu' eṉṉum cāmāṉiyat toṭari mikacciṟanta poruḷākak koṇṭu `aracarkkuḷpaṭu karumam' eṉpatu āṅkila nāṭṭuc cēmparlēṉ (Chamberlin) pōṉṟa ōr atikārattik kuṟikkumeṉpar. itu emakku uṭaṉpāṭaṉṟu.
iṉic cārpiṉālum vaḷḷuvaṉ toḻil yāṅkūṟiyatē eṉpatu pōtarum, mēṟkūṟiya cūttirattil `vaḷḷuvaṉ' eṉpati aṭuttu niṟkum `cākki' yeṉum peyar. veṟṟik kālattilum amitikkālattum aracarkkuk kūttāṭi makiḻcciyiyuṇṭupaṇṇum vēttiyaṟ kūttarik kuṟikkum. vaḷḷuvarum cākkiyarum tamakkuk kīḻppaṭṭa pala tuṇi variyum tattam toḻiṟku muḻuppoṟuppu muṭimiyāṉ karumat talivareṉappaṭṭār.
10ām nūṟṟāṇṭiliyaṟṟappaṭṭa cīvaka cintāmaṇiyil vaḷḷuvaṉ aracāṇiyip paṟiyaṟintu viḷamparañ ceypavaṉākak kūṟappaṭutalāl, ataṟku iraṇṭoru nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muntiya piṅkalattilum atuvēyātal vēṇṭum.
paṇṭikkālattiṟ pāṇarkku icittoḻilē kulattoḻilāyiṉum, avaruṭpalar atil piḻippiṉmipaṟṟip piṟatoḻili mēṟkoṇṭatu pōl, vaḷḷuvarum araca viṉiyillārellām kaṇi (cōtiṭa)t toḻil purintu vantaṉar. cātakaṅkaṇittalum kuṟiccollutalum ikkālattum vaḷḷuvarkkuriya.
paṇṭikkālattilēyē vaḷḷuvar nimittikat toḻiluñ ceytuvantamiyiṉ, vaḷḷuvar eṉum peyar nimittikaṉ eṉum poruḷi yun taḻuviyatu. (cintāmaṇi 419). nimittikaṉ nimittam (cakuṉam) kūṟupavaṉ. vaḷḷuvaṉ kuṟi colluvataṉāl, at toḻiṟku vaḷḷuva cāttirameṉṟum, avaṉukku vaḷḷuvap paṇṭārameṉṟum peyar uṇṭu. paṇṭārameṉpatu muṟkālattil nūlakaṅkaṭkum pala nūl payiṉṟa pulavarkku allatu ñāṉi kaṭkum vaḻaṅkiya peyar. atu piṉpu muṟiyē tuṟavupūṇṭa aṟiñarkkum pōlit tuṟavikaḷukkum, peyarāyiṟṟu.
puṟanāṉūṟṟiṟ cila pāṭalkaḷil nāñcil eṉṉum malikkut talivaṉāṉa oruvaṉ vaḷḷuvaṉ eṉṉum peyarāṟ kūṟappaṭukiṟāṉ. avaṉ orukāṟ cēraṉukkum, piṉpu pāṇṭiyaṉukkum paṭittalivaṉāyiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. avaṉ 137m 140m 380 puṟappāṭṭukkaḷil porunaṉ eṉṟu viḷikkappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. porunaṉ eṉṉum peyar ērkkaḷam pāṭuvōṉ, pōrkkaḷam pāṭuvōṉ, paraṇi pāṭuvōṉ eṉṉum mūvakip pulavaruḷ allatu pāṭakaruḷ oruvariyāvatu pōrvīraṉiyāvatu kuṟikkum. iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭa nāñcil vaḷḷuvaṉ ōr paṭittalivaṉātaliṉ pōrt toḻiṟpaṟṟiyē porunaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ.
vaḷḷuvak kulattāṉoruvaṉē ōr aruñceyalpaṟṟi allatu ciṟanta pōrttoḻilpaṟṟi nāñcil eṉṉum maliyik koṇṭa oru ciṟu nāṭu (cēraṉāl allatu pāṇṭiyaṉāl) koṭukkappaṭṭirukkalām. paṇṭikkālattil pōr vīrar perumpālum maṟavarum kaḷḷarumāyiruntā rēṉum, piṟa kulattārum attoḻiṟku ettuṇiyum vilakkappaṭṭila reṉpatu tēṟṟam. ātalāl nāñcil vaḷḷuvaṉi vaḷḷuvak kulattāṉākak kūṟuvataṟkup peritun taṭiyilli. tiruvitāṅkūrc cīmiyic cērnta oru nāṭu vaḷḷuva nāṭeṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. atu oruvēḷi nāñcil vaḷḷuvaṉ nāṭāyiruntirukkalām.
iṉi, vaḷḷuvaṉ eṉṉum peyari vaḷḷal eṉṉum peyariṉ maṟuvaṭivākak koṇṭu, ataṟku upakāri eṉṉum poruḷum urittākkuvar. vaḷḷal eṉṉum peyarum vaḷ eṉṉum mūlattiṉiṉṟē piṟantatāyiṉum, ammūlam upakāri yeṉṉum poruḷil varumpōtu, vaḷḷal, vaḷḷiyaṉ, vaḷḷiyāṉ, vaḷḷiyōṉ, eṉṟa vaṭivukaḷi ēṟpatallatu vaḷḷuvaṉ eṉṉum vaṭivi evviṭattum ēlāmiyiṉ, atu pōliyuriyeṉa maṟukka.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, vaḷḷuvaṉ eṉpatu kulappeyarē yeṉṟum, atu nimittikaḷiyum kuṟikkumeṉṟum, upakāri yeṉṟum poruḷil vārāteṉṟum aṟintukoḷka.
16
kaḻakamellām cūtāṭumiṭamā?
`kaḻakam' eṉpatu ciṟappākavum perumpāṉmiyākavum kaṟṟōr kūṭṭattiyē kuṟikkumēṉum, ciṟupāṉmi cūtāṭumiṭattiyum kuṟikkumāṟu ilakkiyattil āḷappaṭṭiruppatāl, ivviru poruḷkaḷuḷ etu ciṟantatu eṉṉum aiyamum, `kaḻakam' cūtāṭumiṭattik kuṟittatevvāṟu eṉṉum kalakkamum, cilarkkut tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
kaḻakam eṉṉum colliṉ vērum varalāṟum teriyiṉ, iv viyamum kalakkamum muṟṟum nīṅkiviṭum.
kul - kula - kulavu, kulavutal = kūṭutal.
kula - kala.
kala - kalavi.
kala - kalavi, kalappu - kalampu -
kalampam - katampam.
kalampu -kalampakam
kala - kalaṅku - kalakku- kalakkam
kala - kalakam, kalām, kalāpam, kalāpaṇi.
iruvar allatu iru kaṭciyār kalantē poruvatāl, kalattaṟ karuttiṟ pōrkkaruttut tōṉṟiṟṟu, ki kalattal eṉṉum vaḻakkiyum, poru, camar eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ vērp poruḷiyum nōkkuka.
mēṟkāṭṭiya coṟṟirivup paṭṭiyiṉiṉṟu, kalattaṟcoṟku, irupāṟ kūṭṭam, kalappu, kalakkam, kalakam eṉṉum nāṟporuḷkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷami kāṇka. nīrum maṇṇum pōlap pala poruḷkaḷ allatu karuttukaḷ kalappatē kalakkam.
kul - kuḷ - kaḷ. kaḷḷutal = kūṭutal, poruntutal ottal.
kaḷ - kaḷam = ērkkaḷamum pōrkkaḷamum pōlap palar kūṭumiṭam.
kaḷam - kaḷaṉ = avikkaḷam
kaḷaṉ - kaḻaṉi = ērkkaḷam uḷḷa vayal.
kaḷam - kaḷar = avi.
kaḷar - kaḷari = avi, kalvi nāṭakam mal vil payilum araṅku, vaḻakku maṉṟam, toḻil ceyyum iṭam.
kaḷḷam = pōla.
``kaḷḷa matippa vella vīḻa''	(tol. po. 289).
kuḷ - kuḻu - kuḻampu - kuḻappu - kuḻappam.
kuḻavi = tiraṇṭa ari kal.
kūṭutaṟ karuttil tiraṭcik karuttut tōṉṟum,
``cērē tiraṭci''	(tol. 846)
kūṟutal kāṇka.
kuḻu - kuḻūu.
kuḻu - kuḻām.
kuḻu - kuḻumpu.
kuḻu - kuḻumu - kuḻuvu, kuḻumu - kuḻumal,
kuḻumam, kuḻuvu - kuḻuval, kuḻumutal = kūṭutal, kuḻuvutal = kūṭutal, tiraḷutal. kuḻumu - kaḻumu - kaḻumal = mayakkam, niṟivu, mikuti.
kuḻu - keḻu - keḻumu - keḻuvu.
keḻumutal = poruntutal, keḻuvutal = poruntutal, mayaṅkutal, keḻuvu = naṭpu, `keḻuvi' ōr uvamavurupu
keḻu - keḻi = naṭpu
kuḻu - kuḻi = tiraṇṭa kātaṇi.
kuḻu - (kuḻaku) - kaḻaku - kaḻakam = olakkam (Durbar), pulavaravi, kali payiliṭam, paṭikkalam, mal payiliṭam, cūtāṭumiṭam.
kuḷ - (kūḷ) - kūṭu.
kaḻakam eṉṉum col, kūṭutal eṉṉum poruḷkoṇṭa kuḻu eṉṉum vēraṭiyākap piṟantiruttalāl, potuvākak kūṭṭam eṉṟē poruḷ paṭuvatākum. atu, mutaṟkaṇ, ciṟanta kūṭṭamākiya pulavaravikkup peyarāl vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
``kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷuṅ kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntavip pacuntami ḻēṉi
maṇṇi ṭiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇi ṭippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō''.
eṉṉum parañcōti muṉivar tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇac ceyyuḷil, `kaḻakam' pulavaraviyik kuṟittatu. caṅkam eṉṉum vaṭa coṟku nēr teṉcol kaḻakam eṉpatē.
paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭṭu muttamiḻk kaḻakaṅkaḷum tamakku muntiya tamiḻilakkiyatti ārāyntu vantatiṉāl, kaṟṟōr payilum iṭattikkuṟitta kaḻakam eṉṉumcol, nāḷaṭivil, kaṟpōr payilum iṭattiyum kuṟikkat talippaṭṭatu. ``avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭa maturiyeṉpa.'', ``avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu kapāṭapuratteṉpa.'', ``avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu uttara maturiyeṉpa.'', eṉṟu iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷuri. muṟiyē muttamiḻk kaḻakaṅkaḷiyum paṟṟik kūṟutal kāṇka. itaṉāl, iḷiñar kalvi payilumiṭatti.
``kantaṉi yaṉiyavar kaliteri kaḻakam''
eṉṟār kampar (kamparā. nāṭṭup. 48). iṉi, īṇṭik kaḻakam eṉṉum coṟku, viṟkali maṟkali mutaliya pōrttuṟi payilum iṭam eṉavum poruḷurippar. atuvē kampar karuttāyiṉ pōrkkaliyiyum kalvit tuṟikaḷum oṉṟāka aṭakkikkoḷka. tivākaram, paṭikkaḷam maṟpōr mutaliyaṉa payilum iṭattiyum kaḻakam eṉak kuṟikkum.
ērkkaḷattilum avikkaḷattilumpōl pōrkkaḷattilum makkaḷ kūṭuvatāl, kaḷam eṉṉum col am mūṉṟiṭattiyum kuṟittateṉa muṉṉarkkaṇṭōm. cūtāṭutaṟkum makkaḷ kūṭuvatālum, cūtum oru vakip pōrātalālum kaḻakam eṉṉum col mūṉṟāvatāka allatu iṟutiyākac cūtāṭumiṭattiyum kuṟittatu. cūtum oru vakip pōr eṉpatic cūtu porutal eṉṉum vaḻakkiṉālum, cūtu pōrccarukkam eṉṉum villi pāratac carukkap peyarālum, uttiyālum, aṟiyalām. vēttaviyiluḷḷā rellām ovvoru tuṟiyil tērcci peṟṟirupparātaliṉ, ōlakkamum pulavaraviyiṉ aṭaṅkum. ākavē, pulavaravi, kalvi payiliṭam, cūtāṭu miṭam ākiya mūvēṟu poruḷik kaḻakam eṉṉum col kuṟikkum eṉpatu teḷivām.
``paḻakiya celvamum paṇpuk keṭukkum
kaḻakattuk kāli pukiṉ.''	(937)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷil, cūtāṭumiṭattiyum, ``kaḻakat tiyaluṅ kavaṟṟi ṉiliyum'' eṉṉum puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālit toṭaril (358), cūtāṭṭiyum, kaḻakam eṉṉum coṟ kuṟittatu.
iṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟippiṉum itu arukiya vaḻakkē, iṟṟi ulaka vaḻakkilum, nāṭṭāṇmik kaḻakam, centamiḻk kaḻakam, civa cittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, māṇavar kaḻakam, eṉak kaḻakam eṉṉum col cūtāṭu kaḷamallāta piṟa kaḷarikaḷiyē kuṟittal kāṇka.
kuḻu eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟē kuḻām, kuḻumam mutaliya palvēṟu tokutip peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyiruppiṉum, aviyāvum parupporuḷi laṉṟi nuṇporuḷil ottaṉavalla.
kuḻu = ciṟukūṭṭam (Committee).
kuḻūu = maṟiporuṭ kuṟiyīṭu vaḻaṅkum toḻiṟ kulam allatu vakuppu.
kuḻām = kuḻuviṉum caṟṟup periya kūṭṭam (Party).
kōṣṭi eṉṉum vaṭacoṟku nērāṉa teṉcol kuḻām.
pajaṉik kōṣṭi eṉpatit tiruppāṭṭukkuḻām eṉṉalām.
kuḻumal, kuḻuval = kuḻāttiṉum periya kūṭṭam (Gathering).
kuḻumam = vaṇikar caṅkamum toḻilāḷikaḷ caṅkamum pōṉṟa kūṭṭam (Guild).
kuḻumpu = yāṉiniri tōḻamik kūṭṭam (Herd, Company). ``kaḷiṟṟuk kuḻumpiṉ'' (maturik. 24).
kaḻakam = caṅkam (Society Association).
ākavē, kuḻu, kuḻūu, kuḻām, kuḻumam eṉpaṉa muṟiyē oṉṟi ṉoṉṟu periya tokutikaḷām. kaḻakam eṉpatu uyartiṇikkē yuriyatāyp perumpālum kalvipaṟṟiyatum niliyāṉatum kuḻumattiṟkuc camamāṉatu māṉa kūṭṭattiyē kuṟikkum. itaṉālēyē, paṇṭit tamiḻc caṅkattik kaḻakam eṉṟār parañcōti muṉivar. talikkaḻakap pulavar ainnūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar; iṭikkaḻakap pulavar aimpattoṉpatiṉmar; kaṭik kaḻakap pulavar nāṟpat toṉpatiṉmar. ākavē, kaḻakam eṉpatu ciṟu kuḻuvik kuṟippataṉṟu.
iṉi cūtāṭu karuvikkuk kaḻaṅku, kaḻaṅkam eṉap peyariruppatāl, kaḻaṅkam allatu kaḻaṅkakam eṉṉuñ col kaḻakam eṉat tokkuc cūtāṭu miṭattik kuṟittatōveṉa aiyuṟavum iṭamuṇṭu. āyiṉum, āyntu nōkkuvārkku, mēṟkāṭṭiya varalāṟē uṇmiyāṉa teṉpatu pulaṉām.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, kaḻakam eṉpatu kuḻu eṉṉum vēraṭiyāyp piṟantateṉṟum, kūṭṭam allatu kūṭumiṭam eṉpatiyē aṭippaṭip poruḷākak koṇṭateṉṟum, mutaṟkaṇ kaṟṟōraviyi allatu kaṟpōr kūṭṭattik kuṟitta piṉṉarc cūtāṭumiṭattik kuṟittateṉṟum, caṅkam eṉṉum vaṭa coṟ poruḷil toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya tūya paḻantamiḻc colleṉṟum, paṇṭiyilakkiyam muṟṟum iṟantupaṭṭa ikkālattil tirukkuṟaḷik koṇṭu coṟporuḷ variciyik kāṇamuṭiyāteṉṟum, collārāycciyiṉ tuṇiyiṉālēyē atit tuṇital kūṭumeṉṟum, teṟṟeṉat terintu koḷka.
17
intip payiṟci
ceṉṟa cillāṇṭukaḷāka intiyik kaṭṭāyap pāṭamākka vēṇṭumeṉṟum,tēciyamoḻi (atāvatu intutēcap potumoḻi )yākka vēṇṭumeṉṟum vaṭanāṭṭārum teṉṉāṭṭil vaṭamoḻip paṟṟuḷḷa oru cārārum ceytuvarum  muyaṟci mutalāvatu kavaṉippiṟ kiṭamiṉṟi eḷḷi nakiyāṭappaṭṭatēṉum, itupōtu, teṉṟal muṟṟip peruṅ
kāṟṟāvatu pōlum, turumpu peruttut tūṇāṉāṟ pōlum, ciṟu poṟi peruntī yāṉāṟ pōlum mikkup perukit  tamiḻ vaḷarcci taṭippaṭumāṟu attuṇi intip payiṟciyip paṉṉūṟāṇṭukaḷāka vēṟṟuk koḷkikaṭ kaṭimippaṭṭut tāymoḻippaṟṟu tiṉittaṉiyu millāta tamiḻmakkaḷiṭi paraviyuḷḷatu.
ēṉiya nāṭṭārellām tattam tāymoḻiyi ettuṇiyō vaḷam
paṭutti varukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻnāṭṭārō ‘v§bfÊby‹ ñāyi ṟemakku eṉṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. āṅkilattiṟkum piṟavaṟṟiṟkum āyirak kaṇakkāṉa poṟkācukaḷi valiyak koṭṭiyaḷakkum tamiḻarum uḷar. tamiḻukkup perum poruḷ vaḻaṅkun tamiḻarō tamiḻula kaṉittiṉum oruvariruvarē uḷḷaṉar. intu tēcattil vaṭanāṭṭār mēṉāṭṭārum piṟpaṭumpaṭi tāymoḻip paṟṟil taliciṟantuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻmakkaḷō  mēlik kaliccuṭarkaḷ miḷirntoḷirum iv virupa tām nūṟṟāṇṭilum cutantara vuṇarcciyiṉum tāymoḻip paṟṟiṉum  cettu viṟittuc cilliṭat tuyirppikkum niliyumiṉṟi irāmaṉ āṇṭā leṉṉa, parata ṉāṇṭā leṉṉa eṉṟu kaṇmūṭittaṉamāyk kālaṅ kaḻittu varukiṉṟaṉar.
intip payiṟciyip pāṭacālikaḷil valintu pukutta virumpuvār tam koḷkikkut tēciyap pōrvi pōrppiṉum, ataṟkuk kāraṇam avarkkuḷḷa āṅkilap pakiyum vaṭamoḻi vēṭkiyumē eṉpatu kūrntu nōkkuvār yārkkum pulaṉākāmaṟ pōkātu.
tamiḻuṇarcci ciṟitumillāta tamiḻmakkaḷiṭi intip payiṟciyip parappuvatiṉāl, orukāl tamiḻkkuk kēṭākap perumpāṉmi (majority) maṭṭumpaṟṟi inti kaṭṭāyap payiṟcit tīrmāṉam īṭēṟavuṅ kūṭum. itaṉāl intip payiṟciyē vēṇṭāveṉpatilli, ataṉ kaṭṭāyap payiṟciyē vēṇṭāveṉkiṉṟōm. payaṉil muyaṟciyum tīmi viḷikkum tīrmāṉamum vēṇṭāveṉpati aḻukkāṟāk koḷḷār aṟivuṭiyār. tamiḻāl vaḷarntu tamiḻāl vāḻntu tamiḻukkē kēṭucūḻum taṉiyaṭiyārum tokiyaṭiyārum mikkirukkiṉṟa ikkālattuk kāṟṟuṭaṉ neruppuk kūṭip pakimuyaṟci valiyuṟā vaṇṇam, tamiḻaṉparum tamiḻi vaḷarkka maṭitaṟṟut tāmuntuṟum kaḻakaṅkaḷum tamiḻit taḻīik kāppārāka.
mutalāvatu intip payiṟci vēṇṭuvatā eṉṟum, piṉpu atu kaṭ ṭāyamāy vēṇṭuvatā eṉṟum naṭuvu niliyāy ārāytal vēṇṭum.
``kāytal uvattal akaṟṟi oruporuṭkaṇ
āytal aṟivuṭiyār kaṇṇatē - kāyvataṉkaṇ
uṟṟa kuṇantōṉṟā tākum uvappataṉkaṇ
kuṟṟamun tōṉṟāk keṭum''	(aṟaneṟi.42)
(1) karuttaṟivippu, (2) kalviyaṟivu, (3) aluvaṟpēṟu, (4) moḻi
nūṟ payiṟci, (5) viruppam eṉṉum aintu kāraṇampaṟṟiyē makkaḷāl moḻi allatu moḻikaḷ payilappaṭuvaṉavākum.
oruvaṉ taṉittiruntāl oru moḻi vēṇṭuvatilli. piṟaruṭaṉ kalantuṟintāl taṉ karuttip piṟarkkaṟivittaṟku oru tāymoḻiyēṉun terintiruttal vēṇṭum. teṉṉāṭṭārkkut tamiḻ maliyāḷam mutaliya tāymoḻika ḷiruttalāl tam karutti yaṟivittaṟkup piṟitōr moḻi vēṇṭuvārallar.
kalvi, aluval eṉṟa iraṇṭiṟkum tamiḻ orukāl muḻuttuṇiyā yiruntu itupōtu āṅkila āṭciyiṉālum viññāṉak kali           vaḷarcciyiṉālum kuṟipāṭuḷḷatāyiṟṟu. āyiṉum, ilakkaṇam,  ici,  nīti, kāviyam, illaṟa cittāntam, maruttuvam mutaliya pala tuṟikaḷil iṉṉum immi maṟumip payaṉkaḷuk kēṟṟa perumpulami yaḷippatum, taṉiyākavum āṅkila vāyilākavum pala aluvalkaṭku ātāramumāka vuḷḷatu. ilakkaṇam nīti eṉṟa iraṇṭi nōkkiṉ   ulaka muḻuvatilum oppuyarvaṟṟatākavum viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu!
moḻinūṟ payiṟcikku ulakattiluḷḷa moḻikaḷelām okka 
vēṇṭuvavēṉum, tamiḻāṉatu taṉ toṉmi, mutaṉmi, tāymi, iyaṟkiyoli, colvaḷam, ilakkiyac ciṟappu mutaliyavaṟṟāl intiyil maṭṭumalla āṅkilattiṉum paṉmaṭaṅku  ciṟantatākum.
inti karuttaṟivippu, kalvi, aluval eṉṉum muk kāraṇampaṟṟit teṉṉāṭṭārukku muṟṟum vēṇṭuvataṉṟu, moḻinūlaṟiñar ulaka muḻuvatiṉum viralvit teṇṇumāṟu attuṇic cilarāvar. viruppam paṟṟi inti kaṟpavarum cilarē. ātalāṉ ivarporuṭ ṭellārkkum potuvāy intiyik kaṭṭāyap pāṭamākkuvatu kalvi muṟikkum niyāyattiṟkum māṟākum.
vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷellām perumpālum vaṭamoḻiyit tāyāka vuṭiyaṉa.  teṉṉāṭṭilum tamiḻoḻinta piṟa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻit tuṇiyip peritum vēṇṭuvaṉa. intiyum makamatiyariṟ perumpālār pēcum intuttāṉiyum ēṟattāḻa oṉṟē. ātalāṉ vaṭamoḻik kiḷiyākiya intiyip potumoḻiyākkat tamiḻaroḻinta piṟarellārum iciyiṉum iciyalām. āṉāl, vaṅkāḷattār ataṉi muṟṟilum veṟukkiṉṟaṉar. piṟar perumpālārātaliṉ avar koḷki valiyuṟuvatākak kūṟalām. āṉāl, putumoḻip payiṟci eṅkum  aḷaviṟ palarēṉum cilarēṉum ovvoruvarkkum taṉittaṉi varuttamaṉṟē. palarāṉāl varuttaṅ kūṭuvatum cilarāṉāl kuṟivatu milliyē. mēlum, tamiḻnāṭu ciṟu nāṭēṉum talināṭaṉṟō?
 nīrāruṅ kaṭaluṭutta nilamaṭantik keḻiloḻukum
cīrārum vataṉameṉat tikaḻparata kaṇṭamitil
takkaciṟu piṟinutalun tarittanaṟum tilakamumē
tekkaṇamu matiṟciṟanta tiraviṭanaṟ ṟirunāṭum
eṉṟu nam cuntaram piḷḷiyavarkaḷ kūṟiyatu carittiramum moḻinūlum nōkkiṉ uyarvunaviṟciyaṉṟi uṇmi naviṟciyē yaṉṟō?
 ulakattil makkaḷ carācari antakkaraṇa vali iru moḻiyē payila utavum. āyiram pāṭṭiṟku aṭi teriyum eṉṟāṟ pōlum, ‘Jack of all trades, master of none’ eṉṟāṟ pōlum nuṉippul mēyccalāy arikuṟiyāyk kaṟpatāṉāl palarum  paṉmoḻikaḷ payilalām. āṉāl, aṟpa aṟivu allaṟkiṭam (Little knowledge is a dangerous thing’) eṉṟapaṭi atu tuṉpan taruvatēyaṉṟi iṉpantaruvataṉṟu. oruvaruṭiya kāriyacitti peritum avaruṭiya colvaṉmiyip poṟuttatākum. oru col vellum, oru col kollum eṉpar.
 ākkamuṅ kēṭum ataṉāl varutalāṟ
 kāttōmpal colliṉkaṭ cōrvu. 	(kuṟaḷ.642)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
 oruvar cāturiyamāyum māturyamāyum pēciṉāl karutiya kāriyaṅ kikūṭum; pakivarum vayappaṭuvar.
 tiṟaṉaṟintu colluka colli yaṟaṉum
 poruḷum ataṉiṉūṅ kil. 	(kuṟaḷ. 644)
 virintu toḻilkēṭkum ñālam nirantiṉitu
 collutal vallārp peṟiṉ. 	(kuṟaḷ. 648)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
oruvar kūṟṟu, tiṭṭam (precision), iṉimi, tūymi (purity), 
poruttam (propriety), virivu mutaliya kuṇaṅkaḷit taḻuvic ciṟanta coṟkaḷik koṇṭuḷḷatāyiṉ, mika valiyuṟukiṉṟatu. oruvar paṉmoḻi vallunarēṉum arikuṟiyāyk kaṟṟa oru putumoḻiyiṟ piḻipaṭap pēcuvārāyiṉ avar ikaḻappaṭuvatik kaṇkūṭākak kāṇkiṉṟām.
 iḷami mutal iṅkilīṣ payiṉṟu iṅkilīṣ aṭikkaṭi pēcukiṟa       periya paṭṭatārikaḷkūṭac cila vēḷikaḷiṟ piḻi viṭukiṉṟaṉar. iṅkilīṣ naṉṟāyp payiliṉ tamiḻiṟkum, tamiḻ naṉṟāyp payiliṉ iṅkilīṣiṟkum pōtiya kāla millātu pōkiṉṟatu. iv virumoḻiyum vallār tamiḻnāṭṭilum mikamikac cilarē. iṅṅaṉamirukka iṉṉoru moḻiyik kūṭṭiṉ, oruvaṉ uttiyōka (25) vayatiṟku muṉ mummoḻi payila eṅṅaṉ muṭiyum? antakkaraṇa vali (uṇarccivali, niṉivāṟṟal) yiruppiṉum  kālaṅkāṇātē. arikuṟiyāy mummoḻi payilvatiṉum oru ciṟitēṉum niṟivāka irumoḻi payilal ēṟṟamaṉṟō! peski (vīramāmuṉivar), mākkacu mullar, pōp, kālṭuvel mutaliyōr paṉmoḻi payiṉṟārē yeṉiṉ, avarkkirunta antakkaraṇa vali aṉivarkku milliyē!
 iṅkilīṣiṟkup patilāka intiyi vippōmeṉiṉ aḵtu evvatum iyalvatā? iṅkilīṣāṉatu iṅkilīṣkārariṉ  uyarviṉāl ulakap potumoḻiyā yaṉṟō ākivarukiṉṟatu!  ulakil 1/3 paṅku āṅkila vayam. carvatēca caṅkattil āṅkilat talimi. ulaka vāṇikattiṟku uyirnāṭikaḷākavuḷḷa vaḻikaḷum tuṟikaḷum iṅkilīṣ kāraruṭiyaṉa. iṅkilāntu pōṉṟē ēṟṟamaṭintuḷḷa pirāñcu, cerumaṉi mutaliya piṟa nāṭukaḷilum uṇṭiccāli (hotel), nūlniliyam mutaliya potuvakaṅkaḷil  āṅkila maṟintavarē aluval peṟukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. aimperuṅ kaṇṭaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya amerikkāviṟ perumpālār tāymoḻi āṅkilamē. āṅkilarum amerikkarum putu nirumāṇa viññāṉak kaliyil taliciṟantuḷḷaṉar. itukāli  intiyarkkut teriyāta viññāṉak kalikaḷellām iṅkilīṣil eḻutappaṭṭuḷa. āṭikkāṟṟil ammiyē paṟakkiyil ālili enta mūli eṉṟapaṭi ettuṇiyō ilakkiyap perumiyum moḻit talimiyu muḷḷa vaṭamoḻi  teṉmoḻi yeṉṉum iru pērintiyat tāymoḻikaḷē viññāṉakkali  nūliṉmiyāl     iṅkilīṣiṟku etirnillā veṉiṉ iṉi oru perumiyumillāta inti etirniṟkumā? cūriyaṉukkumuṉ miṉmiṉiyum, yāṉikku muṉ pūṉiyum pōlvatē yaṉṟō?
intiyiṉiṉṟum tamiḻukku vantatellām niyāyap pirakācam pōṉṟa iraṇṭoru nūṉ moḻipeyarppē. tarukkamuṟi yiraṇṭaṉuṭ ciṟanta vicēṭikamuṟi akattiyar kālattē tamiḻiliruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. niyāyap pirakācam pōṉṟa iraṇṭoru vacaṉa nūlkaḷi nīkkuvatiṉāl tamiḻilakkiyattiṟku yātoru kuṟivumiṉṟu. tamiḻilakkiyam eṉṟu collappaṭuvatellām paṇṭic caṅkanūlkaḷum caṅkamaruviya nūlkaḷumē. iṉṟum tamiḻaṟiñariṟ palar intinūṉ moḻipeyarppu kaḷiyē aṟintilar.
intiyil mēlik kalikaḷi yellām moḻipeyarttuk koḷvameṉiṉ, aḵtu eḷitil iyalvatā? ellāk kalikaṭkum ēṟṟa coṟkaḷ intiyiluṇṭā? vaṭamoḻit tuṇikoṇṭu moḻi peyarttuviṭiṉum, intip payiṟci mēṉāṭṭiṟkup payaṉpaṭumā? intiyiṟ payiṉṟu piṉpu āṅkilattiṟ payilvōmeṉiṉ oruvēlik kiruvēliyā? īṉaṉuk kirucelavu' eṉṟaṉṟō ākiviṭum! āṅkilaṅ kaṟṟāl ulakaṉittum cellum. intiyō intiyāviṟkuḷtāṉ cellum. mēlum, avvak kalivallārāl eḻutappaṭṭa mūlanūlkaḷik kaṟka iṭamirukkumpōtu avaṟṟi ēṉ moḻipeyarttuk kaṟka vēṇṭum? āṅkilattiṟ putitu putitāy nūlkaḷ tōṉṟikkoṇṭē yirukkum. avaṟṟi yellām moḻipeyarppatāyiṉ oru moḻipeyarppu ilākāvē  niliyāka vēṇṭumaṉṟō? ettuṇik kālamum poruḷum muyaṟciyum vīṇām! iṅkilīṣi yām uyarttuk kūṟuvatāl iṅkilīṣ kārariṉ aracāṭciyi virumpik kūṟuvatāka eṇṇaṟka. iṅkilīṣ aracāṭci oḻiyalām. iṅkilīṣkārarum nīṅkalām; āṉāl, iṅkilīṣ nīṅkuvataṟkō eṭṭuṇiyum iṭamiṉṟu. ovvoru moḻiyiluḷḷa ilakkiyamum avvam moḻiyip pēcuvāriṉ karuttu kaḷit taḻuviyatākum. iṅkilīṣkārariṭam   cila tīkkuṇaṅkaḷiruppiṉum nām piṉpaṟṟakkūṭiya pala  naṟkuṇaṅkaḷuḷa veṉpati naṭuvuniliyuḷḷa evarum  maṟukkoṇātu. ārāycci, naṭuvunili, camutāya nili mutaliya vaṟṟil avar taliciṟantavarāvar. j®°l‹(Thurston) eḻutiya  teṉ ṉintiyak kulamarapu nūlum, kālṭuvel eḻutiya tirāviṭa oppiya lilakkaṇamum avar ārāyccikkuc ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭām. āṅkilar namatu nāṭṭiṟku vantu nammippaṟṟiyē namakku aṟivippatu namakkup peritum nāṇit tarukiṉṟatu. aṅṅaṉam aṟivittum avaṟṟi aṟintavar āyirattiṟ koruvarē āvar. uṇmiyāṉa carittiramum moḻinūlum āṅkilarē namakkuṇarttiṉar. cutantaram, camattuvam, cōtarattuvam mutaliyavipaṟṟiya paranta nōkkum tiruntiṉa vuṇarcciyum āṅkila nūlkaḷālēyē uṇṭākum. intiyaruḷ cīrtiruttam virumpuvā rellām āṅkila maṟintavarāyum  virumpātavarellārum perumpālum aṟiyātavarāyu miruttal kāṇka. 
inti nūlkaḷō kulap piriviṉiyum piṟappuppaṟṟiya ēṟṟiḻi puṅ kūṟi mēṉmēlum kēṭṭiṟkē nērvaḻi kāṭṭuvavākum.  taṭpaveppa nili, toḻil, oḻukkam, maṉitaṉ kaṭṭuppāṭu mutaliyavi paṟṟi iṭiyil ēṟpaṭṭa kulaṅkaḷ paṭippuk kālantoṭṭē yiruppaṉavāyiṉ, mēṉāṭukaḷil ēṉ avi yilli? piramāvukku intiyāviṟkuḷtāṉ atikāramō?  tāḻntōr ciṟitum taliyeṭā vaṇṇam avarkaḷi amiḻttik kollukiṉṟa aniyāyak koḷki kaḷum, carittirap puḷukukaḷum, kulappuraṭṭukaḷum, mantira tantira ēmāṟṟaṅ kaḷum malintuḷḷa itikāca purāṇak kuppikaḷē intiyiluḷḷaṉa. ākiyāl, itupōtu intiyap potumoḻiyāyum ulakap potumoḻiyāyum irukkiṉṟa āṅkilatti ataṟṟiviṭṭu intiyi ēṟpaṭuttal kōḷariyi nīkkiviṭṭuk kuḷḷanariyi iruttuvatiyē okkum.
inti āṇavat taṉmi vāyntu cevikkiṉpam payappateṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. aḵtu uṇmiyē. āṉāl, ataṉ āṇavat taṉmi eḷitāy uccarikka voṇṇāta ceyaṟki yolikaḷālāki muyaṟci varuttatti yuṇṭupaṇṇuvatāl atu kuṟṟamākavu muḷḷatu. iṉi iṉimiyō palavakippaṭum. mā, palā, vāḻi mutaliya pala kaṉikaḷiṉ cuviyum iṉimiyē yāyiṉum ovvoṉṟaṉ iṉimiyum vevvēṟu vakippaṭum. atupōla moḻikaḷiṉ iṉimiyum vevvēṟu vakippaṭum. ilattīṉ iṉimi vēṟu; intiyiṉimi vēṟu; tamiḻiṉimi vēṟu. tamiḻiṉimiyit tirukkuṟaḷ, tirukkōvi, cilappatikāram. māṇikkavācakar varalāṟuṅ kālamum mutaliya nūlkaḷiṟ kaṇṭukoḷka. āṇṭum cevippulaṉ nuṇukiyārkkē ciṟantu pulaṉām. tamiḻiṉpam iyaṟkiyum intiyiṉpam ceyaṟkiyumākum.
intip payiṟciyip parappuvār āṅkāṅku eḷiya kaṭṭaṇap    pāṭacālikaḷi ēṟpaṭuttit taṟkalvik kēṟṟa pala inti nūlkaḷiyum uriyuṭaṉ pala moḻikaḷil veḷiyiṭṭu eḷiya vilikku viṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉar. intiyap paṟṟuḷḷa tēciyat talivarkaḷum cuyarājyam virintu varumeṉṟum, vantavuṭaṉ intiyē tēca moḻiyeṉṟum, ataṟku muṉṉarē tāṅkaḷ iṉip pēcuvatellām intiyē yeṉṟum, intiyaṟiyātār taṅkaḷ coṟpoḻivukaḷik kēṭṭuṇara muṭiyāteṉṟum āṅkāṅku nākkaṭippāka vāyppaṟiyaṟikiṉṟaṉar. itaṉi nampip palar inti payilkiṉṟaṉar. vaṭamoḻi yiṟantupaṭṭatē eṉṟu  vayiṟerikiṉṟa vaṭamoḻi veṟiyārkku vaṭamoḻik kiḷiyāṉa intip payiṟci kōṭi maḻi pōlak kuḷircci   tarukiṉṟatu. inti vāyilāy vaṭamoḻiyi uyirppittu mīṭṭum vaḷarkkalāmeṉṟu avar pakaṟkaṉāk kāṇkiṉṟaṉar.
intip payiṟciyāl tēciya oṟṟumi uṇṭāku meṉṟu cila tēciyat toṇṭar ēmāṟṟukiṉṟaṉar; cilar ēmāṟukiṉṟaṉar. tēciya oṟṟumi karuti inti payilvatu avali niṉittu urali  iṭippatā kiṉṟatu. intu tēcattiluḷḷa piriviṉi kulamata vēṟupāṭṭāṉum piṟappuppaṟṟik kaṟpikkum ēṟṟiḻipāṉu maṉṟit tēcap potumoḻi yiṉmiyāṉaṉṟu. intip payiṟci oruvēḷi iṉṟuḷḷa piriviṉiyip perukkumē yaṉṟik kuṟikkā teṉpatu orutali.
iṉi, intiyāl tamiḻ vaḷarcciyuṟum eṉṟu ēmāṟṟuvārum uḷar.  oru kiḷimoḻi allatu cārpu moḻiyāyiṉ piṟa moḻic cārpāl vaḷarum. oru taṉit tāymoḻiyō piṟa moḻic cārpāl tūymi yiḻakkum. āṅkilam oru cārpu moḻiyāyum ulakap potumoḻiyāyu miruttaliṉ, atu pala moḻikaḷiṉuṅ kaṭaṉ koḷvatu iṉṟiyami yātatākiṉṟatu. āṅkilattiṉ perumikkuk kāraṇam āṅkilariṉ āṭci, vāṇikam, putu nirumāṇavaṉmi mutaliyaviyē yaṉṟi ayaṉmoḻiyiṟ kaṭaṉkōṭa laṉṟu. tamiḻ, teluṅku mutaliya intiya moḻikaḷ kaṭaṉ koḷvatiṉāl āṅkilam perumi yaṭiya muṭiyumā? ik kālattil oru vakuppār viññāṉakkalit tērcciyum putu nirumāṇavaṉmiyum peṟṟāloḻiya avar moḻi eṅṅaṉam perumi peṟak kūṭum? putu nirumāṇavaṉmiyiṟ ciṟanta pala vakuppār moḻikaḷuḷḷum āṅkilam taliciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatē ! teluṅku maliyāḷam mutaliya moḻikaḷ veṟumiyāy vaḻaṅkuvataṟkē piṟamoḻit tuṇiyi vēṇṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila moḻikaḷ moḻiyaḷavāyt tamittu vaḻaṅkiṉum kali vaḷarccikkup piṟamoḻit tuṇi vēṇṭuva vākiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻō etaṟkum piṟa moḻittuṇi vēṇṭātavāṟu attuṇic  colvaḷamuṭittā yuḷḷatu. āyiṉum, āṅkilattiṟkuriya perumi ataṟkilliyē! ituvari yillāta perumiyā iṉik kaṭaṉ kōṭalāl tamiḻukku vantuviṭum? ikkāla uyartarak  kalviyellām viññāṉak kaliyāka vaṉṟō vaṭivu koṇṭuḷḷatu? viññāṉak kalikaḷi yellām moḻi peyarttuk koḷvameṉiṉ, enta moḻiyiltāṉ (piṟamoḻit tuṇi koṇṭēṉum) avaṟṟi moḻipeyarkka muṭiyātu? moḻipeyartta vaḷavāṉē em moḻikkum ēṟṟam vantuviṭumā? ākiyāl, tamiḻ perumi peṟātataṟkuk kāraṇam atil viññāṉak
kali mutaṉū liṉmiyē yaṉṟi ataṉ kaṭaṉ koḷḷāmi yaṉṟu.
pāṭacālikaḷiluṅ kallūrikaḷilum māṇavar pāṭaṅkaḷuḷ ayaṉmoḻi āṅkilam oṉṟēyā yirukkumpōtē tamiḻppayiṟci mikak kuṉṟiyuḷatu. iṉi intiyum oru pāṭamāyiṉ tamiḻppayiṟci   mikamikak kuṉṟum eṉpataṟku aiyamiṉṟu. mēlum, tamiḻit tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭuḷḷa vaṭamoḻip paṟṟiṉarellām  intiyit tāymoḻiyeṉak kūṟuvar. aiyāyiram āṇṭukaṭku  muṉ iṟantupaṭṭa vaṭamoḻiyē iṉṟum tam tāymoḻiyeṉak kūcātu kūṟupavar, itupōtu vaḻakkiluḷḷa intiyik kūṟātu viṭuvarā? viṭāraṉṟē! ākiyāl, ippōtuḷḷa piriviṉi iṉṉum viraṅ koḷḷumaṉṟō?
iṉi, inti ayalār mukkiyamāyt tamiḻar eḷitiṟ payilumāṟu attuṇi ilakkaṇa nērmiyuṭiyatumaṉṟu. ataṉ mayakkukaḷiṟ cilavāvaṉa:
1. pālkaḷ tamiḻiṟpōṟ poruḷpaṟṟi yillāmal vaṭamoḻi yiṟpōl īṟupaṟṟiyē yirukkum. perumpālum a ā īṟṟa āṇ pāleṉṟum, i ī īṟṟa peṇpāleṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭum. āyiṉum, havā(kāṟṟu), tavā (maruntu), jukām(caḷi), iccā(viruppam), tuṉiyā (ulakam), jāytāt (cottu), taklip (kaṣṭam), mut( maraṇam), capā  (capi), tāvat (viruntu), pāt   (vārtti), taviyatā (tēkastiti), kapar (camāccāram), pūñc  (vāl), āṅk (kaṇ) mutaliya ettuṇiyō coṟkaḷ a ā īṟṟavēṉum peṇpālē. i ī īṟṟa āṇpāṟ coṟkaḷ  cilavē.
intiyiṟ cilaviṭattuc coṟkaḷi ṉīṟṟiluḷḷa akaram olippa tilli. āṇṭu olipaṟṟi meyyīṟāyiṉum eḻuttuppaṟṟi uyirīṟākavē koḷḷappaṭum.
āṅkilattil uccarip poḻuṅkiṉmipōl intiyiṟ pāloḻuṅ
kiṉmiyāl pala coṟkaṭkut taṉittaṉi pālaṟiya  vēṇṭu vatākiṉṟatu.
2. peyareccamum viṉiyeccamum pāl eṇ kāṭṭum.
e-ṭu:	peyareccam	:	accā laṭkā : nalla piyaṉ
			accī laṭkī : nalla peṇ piḷḷi	 						accē laṭkē : nalla piyaṉkaḷ.
	viṉiyeccam	:	kayā hi = pōyirukkiṟāṉ.
			kayī hi = pōyirukkiṟāḷ.	 
			kayē hiṉ = pōyirukkiṟārkaḷ.
3. `vah' eṉṉuñ col oṉṟē avaṉ, avaḷ, atu eṉṉum mūṉṟu orumippāṟ cuṭṭukaḷiyum, vē eṉṉuñ col  oṉṟē avarkaḷ, avi eṉṉum iru paṉmippāṟ  cuṭṭukaḷiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
4. iṟanta kāla viṉikaḷuḷ,
ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟiya viṉiyāṉāl tamiḻiṟpōl pāl eṇkaḷil eḻuvāyit taḻuvum. 
ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟā viṉiyāṉāl eḻuvāyit taḻuvātu ceyappaṭuporuḷit taḻuvum. eḻuvāy `ṉē' vikuti peṟum.
e-ṭu:	miṉē ēk kōṭā tēkkā) - nāṉ ōr āṇkutiriyip pārttēṉ.
	miṉē tas kōṭē tēkkēṉ) - nāṉ pattu āṇkutiri kaḷip pārttēṉ.
iṉi, ceyappaṭuporuḷ tokku niṉṟālum, `ki' eṉṟa vēṟṟumi yurupik koṇṭiruntālum, iṟantakāla  viṉi (eḻuvāy entap pāliṭa
māṉālum) eppōtum āṇpāṟ paṭarkki orumiyākavē yirukkum.
e-ṭu: 	sīttāṉē tēkkā = cīti pārttāḷ.
	hamṉē us strīkō tēkkā = nāṅkaḷ ap peṇṇip 	pārttōm.
5. āṟām vēṟṟumi yurupākiya `kā' eṉpatu piṉṉāl varum peyarccol āṇpālāyiruntu vēṟṟumiyurupu koṇṭiruntāl `kē' eṉa māṟum.
e-ṭu: rām kē kar mē 
im mātiriyē piṉṉāl vēṟṟumiyurupuṭaṉ varum āṇpāṟ peyarccolliṉ īṟṟiluḷḷa `ā' vum, ap peyarit taḻuvum uriccolliṉ īṟṟiluḷḷa `ā' vum `ē'  eṉa māṟum.
e-ṭu: rām kē paṭē kōṭē par.
iṉṉuñ cila mayakkaṅkaḷumuḷa.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟit tamiḻmakkaḷ cevvaṉē   ārāyntu, erimaṭuppiṉum iṭampeyarāta mācuṇam pōl  mantamāyirātu iṉi mēlāyiṉum puttuyir peṟṟeḻuntu tam tāymoḻikkuc ceyya vēṇṭiya paṇiyi yaṟintu kaṭippiṭippārāka.
